Motivated by the need to simulate realistic long-horizon productivity tasks that depend on personalized computer environments, the authors introduce Synthetic Computers at Scale, a method to generate synthetic user worlds with realistic folder structures and content-rich artifacts. Each synthetic computer hosts two agents: one that defines user-specific productivity goals requiring multiple deliverables over a month’s worth of work, and another that acts as the user, navigating the filesystem, collaborating with simulated peers, and producing professional outputs until completion. Preliminary experiments with 1,000 synthetic computers show each simulation requires over 8 hours of agent runtime and spans more than 2,000 turns, yielding rich learning signals that significantly improve agent performance on both in-domain and out-of-domain productivity benchmarks, suggesting scalability to millions of synthetic user worlds for broader coverage of professional contexts.
为模拟依赖个性化电脑环境的长期生产力任务，作者提出“大规模合成电脑”方法，生成包含真实文件夹结构和丰富内容的虚拟用户世界。每个虚拟电脑中部署两个代理：一个生成针对该用户的具体生产力目标（需多份交付物、耗时约一个月），另一个代理扮演用户，通过文件系统导航、协作与产出专业文档完成任务。初步实验在1000台合成电脑上运行，每轮模拟耗时超8小时、平均超过2000轮，产生丰富学习信号，显著提升代理在领域内及领域外的生产力表现，表明该方法可扩展至百万级虚拟用户世界，覆盖更广泛的职业与情境。
An adaptive wavelet-based PINN for problems with localized high-magnitude source
Authors: Himanshu Pandey, Ratikanta Behera
First: 2026-04-30T17:57:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:57:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In recent years, physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have gained significant attention for solving differential equations, although they suffer from two fundamental limitations, namely, spectral bias inherent in neural networks and loss imbalance arising from multiscale phenomena. This paper proposes an adaptive wavelet-based PINN (AW-PINN) to address the extreme loss imbalance characteristic of problems with localized high-magnitude source terms. Such problems frequently arise in various physical applications, such as thermal processing, electro-magnetics, impact mechanics, and fluid dynamics involving localized forcing. The proposed framework dynamically adjusts the wavelet basis function based on residual and supervised loss. This adaptive nature makes AW-PINN handle problems with high-scale features effectively without being memory-intensive. Additionally, AW-PINN does not rely on automatic differentiation to obtain derivatives involved in the loss function, which accelerates the training process. The method operates in two stages, an initial short pre-training phase with fixed bases to select physically relevant wavelet families, followed by an adaptive refinement that adapts scales and translations without populating high-resolution bases across entire domains. Theoretically, we show that under certain assumptions, AW-PINN admits a Gaussian process limit and derive its associated NTK structure. We evaluate AW-PINN on several challenging PDEs featuring localized high-magnitude source terms with extreme loss imbalances having ratios up to $10^{10}:1$. Across these PDEs, including transient heat conduction, highly localized Poisson problems, oscillatory flow equations, and Maxwell equations with a point charge source, AW-PINN consistently outperforms existing methods in its class.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于局部高幅值源问题的自适应小波基物理信息神经网络
近年来，物理信息神经网络（PINN）在求解微分方程方面受到广泛关注，但其存在两个根本性局限：神经网络固有的频谱偏差和多尺度现象导致的损失失衡。本文提出一种自适应小波基PINN（AW-PINN），以解决局部高幅值源项问题中特有的极端损失失衡问题。此类问题在热处理、电磁学、冲击力学及涉及局部强迫的流体力学等领域频繁出现。该框架根据残差与监督损失动态调整小波基函数，其自适应特性使AW-PINN能有效处理高尺度特征问题且不占用大量内存。此外，AW-PINN无需自动微分计算损失函数中的导数，从而加速训练过程。该方法分两阶段运行：第一阶段为固定基函数的短时预训练，用于筛选物理相关的小波族；第二阶段为自适应细化，调整尺度与平移，无需在整个域内填充高分辨率基函数。理论上，在一定假设下，我们证明AW-PINN具有高斯过程极限，并推导其对应的NTK结构。我们在多个具有极端损失失衡（比例高达10^10:1）的局部高幅值源项PDE问题上评估AW-PINN，包括瞬态热传导、高度局部化的泊松方程、振荡流方程及点电荷源的麦克斯韦方程。在这些PDE问题中，AW-PINN始终优于同类现有方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the spectral bias and loss imbalance issues in standard PINNs when solving PDEs with localized high-magnitude sources, this paper introduces an adaptive wavelet-based PINN (AW-PINN) that dynamically adjusts its wavelet basis according to residual and supervised loss to handle extreme scale disparities. The method operates in two stages: a pre-training phase to select relevant wavelet families, followed by adaptive refinement of scales and translations without requiring high-resolution global bases, thus reducing memory usage. AW-PINN avoids automatic differentiation for derivative computation, speeding up training. Experimental results across challenging PDEs—including transient heat conduction, localized Poisson problems, oscillatory flows, and Maxwell equations with point charges—show AW-PINN consistently outperforms existing PINN variants, especially in cases with loss imbalances up to 10^10:1.
为解决标准PINN在处理局部高幅值源项PDE时存在的频谱偏差与损失失衡问题，本文提出一种自适应小波PINN（AW-PINN），其根据残差与监督损失动态调整小波基函数，有效应对极端尺度差异。方法分两阶段：先用固定基进行预训练以筛选物理相关小波族，再自适应调整尺度与平移，无需全局高分辨率基展开。AW-PINN避免自动微分计算导数，加速训练，并在理论层面证明其具备高斯过程极限及对应的NTK结构。在包括瞬态热传导、局部泊松方程、振荡流及点电荷麦克斯韦方程等具有极端损失失衡（高达10^10:1）的PDE问题上，AW-PINN表现优于同类现有方法。
Stop Holding Your Breath: CT-Informed Gaussian Splatting for Dynamic Bronchoscopy
Authors: Andrea Dunn Beltran, Daniel Rho, Aarav Mehta, Xinqi Xiong, Raúl San José Estépar, Ron Alterovitz, Marc Niethammer, Roni Sengupta
First: 2026-04-30T17:57:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:57:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Bronchoscopic navigation relies on registering endoscopic video to a preoperative CT scan, but respiratory motion deforms the airway by 5-20 mm, creating CT-to-body divergence that limits localization accuracy. In practice, this is mitigated through breath-hold protocols, which attempt to match the intraoperative anatomy to a static CT, but are difficult to reproduce and disrupt clinical workflow. We propose to eliminate the need for breath-hold protocols by leveraging patient-specific respiratory modeling. Paired inhale-exhale CT scans, already acquired for planning, implicitly define the patient-specific deformation space of the breathing airway. By registering these scans, we reduce respiratory motion to a single scalar breathing phase per frame, constraining all reconstructions to anatomically observed configurations. We embed this representation within a mesh-anchored Gaussian splatting framework, where a lightweight estimator infers breathing phase directly from endoscopic RGB, enabling continuous, deformation-aware reconstruction throughout the respiratory cycle without breath-holds or external sensing. To enable quantitative evaluation, we introduce RESPIRE, a physically grounded bronchoscopy simulation pipeline with per-frame ground truth for geometry, pose, breathing phase, and deformation. Experiments on RESPIRE show that our approach achieves geometrically faithful reconstruction, over 20x faster training, and 1.22 mm target localization accuracy (within the 3mm clinically relevant tolerances) outperforming unconstrained single-CT baselines. Please check out our website for additional visuals: https://asdunnbe.github.io/RESPIRE/
中文标题/摘要
标题：停止屏息：基于CT引导的高斯点云用于动态支气管镜导航
支气管镜导航依赖将内窥镜视频与术前CT扫描配准，但呼吸运动使气道变形5-20毫米，造成CT与体表的偏差，限制定位精度。实践中，通过屏息协议试图将术中解剖结构与静态CT匹配，但难以重复且干扰临床流程。我们提出通过患者特异性呼吸建模消除屏息需求。术前已获取的吸气-呼气配对CT扫描，隐式定义了患者呼吸气道的变形空间。通过配准这些扫描，我们将呼吸运动简化为每帧单一呼吸相位标量，约束所有重建仅限于解剖学观察到的构型。我们将此表示嵌入到以网格锚定的高斯点云框架中，轻量级估计器直接从内窥镜RGB图像推断呼吸相位，实现无需屏息或外部传感的呼吸周期连续变形感知重建。为支持定量评估，我们引入RESPIRE，一个基于物理原理的支气管镜仿真管道，提供每帧几何、姿态、呼吸相位和变形的地面真值。在RESPIRE上的实验表明，我们的方法实现几何忠实重建，训练速度提升20倍以上，目标定位精度达1.22毫米（在临床相关3毫米容差内），优于无约束单CT基线方法。请访问我们的网站获取更多可视化内容：https://asdunnbe.github.io/RESPIRE/
Summary / 总结
To address respiratory motion-induced inaccuracies in bronchoscopic navigation, this work proposes a CT-informed Gaussian splatting method that eliminates the need for breath-hold protocols by modeling patient-specific airway deformation from paired inhale-exhale CT scans. The method constrains 3D reconstructions to anatomically plausible breathing phases inferred from endoscopic RGB input via a lightweight estimator, enabling continuous, deformation-aware visualization throughout the respiratory cycle. Evaluated on the RESPIRE simulation pipeline with ground-truth breathing phase and geometry, the approach achieves geometrically accurate reconstructions, 20x faster training, and 1.22 mm localization accuracy—within clinically acceptable 3mm tolerances—outperforming single-CT baselines.
为解决呼吸运动导致的支气管镜导航定位不准问题，该研究提出一种基于CT信息的高斯点云重建方法，通过利用术前获取的吸气与呼气配对CT扫描，建模患者特异性气道形变，从而无需屏息即可实现呼吸周期内连续、形变感知的重建。该方法通过轻量级估计器从内窥镜RGB图像直接推断呼吸相位，约束重建结果符合解剖学形态。在RESPIRE仿真平台（含逐帧真实标注）上验证，该方法实现几何精确重建，训练速度提升20倍，目标定位精度达1.22毫米（优于临床3毫米容许误差），显著优于单CT基线方法。
LLM as Clinical Graph Structure Refiner: Enhancing Representation Learning in EEG Seizure Diagnosis
Authors: Lincan Li, Zheng Chen, Yushun Dong
Venue: IJCAI
First: 2026-04-30T17:57:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:57:12+00:00
Comments: This paper is accepted by the 35th International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence (IJCAI-ECAI 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Electroencephalogram (EEG) signals are vital for automated seizure detection, but their inherent noise makes robust representation learning challenging. Existing graph construction methods, whether correlation-based or learning-based, often generate redundant or irrelevant edges due to the noisy nature of EEG data. This significantly impairs the quality of graph representation and limits downstream task performance. Motivated by the remarkable reasoning and contextual understanding capabilities of large language models (LLMs), we explore the idea of using LLMs as graph edge refiners. Specifically, we propose a two-stage framework: we first verify that LLM-based edge refinement can effectively identify and remove redundant connections, leading to significant improvements in seizure detection accuracy and more meaningful graph structures. Building on this insight, we further develop a robust solution where the initial graph is constructed using a Transformer-based edge predictor and multilayer perceptron, assigning probability scores to potential edges and applying a threshold to determine their existence. The LLM then acts as an edge set refiner, making informed decisions based on both textual and statistical features of node pairs to validate the remaining connections. Extensive experiments on TUSZ dataset demonstrate that our LLM-refined graph learning framework not only enhances task performance but also yields cleaner and more interpretable graph representations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型作为临床图结构优化器：提升EEG癫痫诊断中的表示学习
脑电图（EEG）信号对自动化癫痫检测至关重要，但其固有噪声使鲁棒表示学习极具挑战。现有图构建方法，无论是基于相关性还是学习型方法，常因EEG数据的噪声特性生成冗余或无关边，严重损害图表示质量并限制下游任务性能。受大语言模型（LLM）卓越推理与上下文理解能力启发，我们探索将LLM用于图边优化。具体而言，我们提出两阶段框架：首先验证基于LLM的边优化可有效识别并移除冗余连接，显著提升癫痫检测准确率并生成更具意义的图结构。在此基础上，我们进一步构建稳健方案：初始图由基于Transformer的边预测器与多层感知机构建，为潜在边分配概率得分并应用阈值决定其存在性；随后LLM作为边集优化器，依据节点对的文本与统计特征，智能决策验证剩余连接。在TUSZ数据集上的大量实验表明，我们的LLM优化图学习框架不仅提升任务性能，还生成更干净、可解释的图表示。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the noisy nature of EEG signals that hampers graph-based seizure detection, this work proposes using large language models (LLMs) as edge refiners to improve graph structure quality. The method employs a two-stage framework: first, a Transformer and MLP-based edge predictor assigns probability scores to potential edges, then an LLM refines the edge set by evaluating node pairs using textual and statistical features to remove redundant connections. Experiments on the TUSZ dataset show that this LLM-refined approach significantly boosts seizure detection accuracy while producing cleaner, more interpretable graph structures.
鉴于EEG信号噪声导致现有图结构方法产生冗余边，影响癫痫检测性能，本文提出利用大语言模型（LLM）作为图边精炼器。方法分为两阶段：首先用Transformer与MLP构建边预测器，为潜在边分配概率得分；随后LLM根据节点对的文本与统计特征判断边的有效性。在TUSZ数据集上的实验表明，该LLM精炼框架显著提升检测准确率，并生成更清晰、可解释的图结构。
AEGIS: A Holistic Benchmark for Evaluating Forensic Analysis of AI-Generated Academic Images
Authors: Bo Zhang, Tzu-Yen Ma, Zichen Tang, Junpeng Ding, Zirui Wang, Yizhuo Zhao, Peilin Gao, Zijie Xi, Zixin Ding, Haiyang Sun, Haocheng Gao, Yuan Liu, Liangjia Wang, Yiling Huang, Yujie Wang, Yuyue Zhang, Ronghui Xi, Yuanze Li, Jiacheng Liu, Zhongjun Yang, Haihong E
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-30T17:56:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:56:58+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026 Main Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce AEGIS, A holistic benchmark for Evaluating forensic analysis of AI-Generated academic ImageS. Compared to existing benchmarks, AEGIS features three key advances: (1) Domain-Specific Complexity: covering seven academic categories with 39 fine-grained subtypes, exposing intrinsic forensic difficulty, where even GPT-5.1 reaches 48.80% overall performance and expert models achieve only limited localization accuracy (IoU 30.09%); (2) Diverse Forgery Simulations: modeling four prevalent academic forgery strategies across 25 generative models, with 11 yielding average forensic accuracy below 50%, showing that forensics lag behind generative advances; and (3) Multi-Dimensional Forensic Evaluation: jointly assessing detection, reasoning, and localization, revealing complementary strengths between model families, with multimodal large language models (MLLMs) at 84.74% accuracy in textual artifact recognition and expert detectors peaking at 79.54% accuracy in binary authenticity detection. By evaluating 25 leading MLLMs, nine expert models, and one unified multimodal understanding and generation model, AEGIS serves as a diagnostic testbed exposing fundamental limitations in academic image forensics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AEGIS：用于评估AI生成学术图像取证分析的综合基准
我们提出了AEGIS，一个用于评估AI生成学术图像取证分析的综合基准。与现有基准相比，AEGIS具有三大关键进步：（1）领域特定复杂性：涵盖七个学术类别及39个细粒度子类型，揭示内在取证难度，即使GPT-5.1整体性能也仅为48.80%，专家模型仅实现有限的定位精度（IoU 30.09%）；（2）多样化的伪造模拟：在25个生成模型中模拟四种常见学术伪造策略，其中11个模型平均取证准确率低于50%，表明取证能力落后于生成技术进步；（3）多维度取证评估：联合评估检测、推理与定位能力，揭示不同模型家族的互补优势，多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在文本伪迹识别中准确率达84.74%，专家检测器在二元真实性检测中最高准确率达79.54%。通过对25个领先MLLMs、9个专家模型及1个统一多模态理解和生成模型的评估，AEGIS作为诊断测试平台，揭示了学术图像取证中的根本性局限。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the urgent need to evaluate forensic capabilities against increasingly sophisticated AI-generated academic images, AEGIS introduces a comprehensive benchmark that addresses domain-specific complexity, diverse forgery simulations, and multi-dimensional forensic assessment. It covers seven academic categories with 39 subtypes, revealing that even advanced models like GPT-5.1 achieve only 48.80% overall performance, while expert models show limited localization accuracy (IoU 30.09%). The benchmark simulates four common academic forgery strategies across 25 generative models, with 11 achieving below 50% forensic accuracy, indicating that forensic methods lag behind generative advances. Evaluating 25 MLLMs, nine expert models, and one unified multimodal model, AEGIS finds MLLMs excel at textual artifact recognition (84.74%) while expert detectors peak at binary authenticity detection (79.54%), highlighting complementary strengths and exposing fundamental limitations in the field.
为应对AI生成学术图像日益增多带来的鉴定需求，AEGIS提出一个全面的评估基准，涵盖七个学术领域39个细分类别，模拟四种常见学术伪造策略，并从检测、推理和定位三个维度评估模型性能。实验表明，即使最新模型如GPT-5.1整体准确率也仅48.80%，专家模型定位精度（IoU）仅为30.09%。多模态大语言模型在文本伪迹识别中表现最佳（84.74%），专家检测器在二分类检测中最高达79.54%，揭示了不同模型家族的互补优势，也暴露出当前学术图像鉴定技术与生成能力之间的显著差距。
Defending Quantum Classifiers against Adversarial Perturbations through Quantum Autoencoders
Authors: Emma Andrews, Sahan Sanjaya, Prabhat Mishra
First: 2026-04-30T17:56:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:56:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine learning models can learn from data samples to carry out various tasks efficiently. When data samples are adversarially manipulated, such as by insertion of carefully crafted noise, it can cause the model to make mistakes. Quantum machine learning models are also vulnerable to such adversarial attacks, especially in image classification using variational quantum classifiers. While there are promising defenses against these adversarial perturbations, such as training with adversarial samples, they face practical limitations. For example, they are not applicable in scenarios where training with adversarial samples is either not possible or can overfit the models on one type of attack. In this paper, we propose an adversarial training-free defense framework that utilizes a quantum autoencoder to purify the adversarial samples through reconstruction. Moreover, our defense framework provides a confidence metric to identify potentially adversarial samples that cannot be purified the quantum autoencoder. Extensive evaluation demonstrates that our defense framework can significantly outperform state-of-the-art in prediction accuracy (up to 68%) under adversarial attacks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过量子自编码器防御量子分类器对抗对抗性扰动
机器学习模型可通过数据样本学习以高效执行各种任务。当数据样本被对抗性篡改（如插入精心设计的噪声）时，模型可能出错。量子机器学习模型同样易受此类对抗攻击影响，尤其在使用变分量子分类器进行图像分类时。尽管存在针对对抗性扰动的有前景的防御方法（如对抗样本训练），但它们面临实际限制，例如在无法使用对抗样本训练或易导致模型对特定攻击过拟合的场景中不适用。本文提出一种无需对抗训练的防御框架，利用量子自编码器通过重建净化对抗样本。此外，该防御框架提供置信度度量，以识别无法被量子自编码器净化的潜在对抗样本。大量评估表明，该防御框架在对抗攻击下预测准确率显著优于现有最先进方法（最高达68%）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of quantum machine learning models, especially variational quantum classifiers, to adversarial perturbations that degrade classification accuracy, this paper proposes a defense framework that avoids adversarial training. The method leverages a quantum autoencoder to reconstruct and purify adversarial samples, effectively removing malicious noise while preserving legitimate data. The framework also includes a confidence metric to flag samples that cannot be purified, indicating potential adversarial inputs. Experimental results show that this approach significantly improves prediction accuracy under adversarial attacks, achieving up to a 68% performance gain over state-of-the-art methods without requiring adversarial training.
鉴于变分量子分类器等量子机器学习模型易受对抗扰动影响，导致分类准确率下降，本文提出一种无需对抗训练的防御框架。该方法利用量子自编码器对对抗样本进行重构与净化，有效去除扰动同时保留真实数据，并引入置信度指标识别无法净化的潜在对抗样本。实验表明，该框架在对抗攻击下显著优于现有最先进方法，预测准确率提升最高达68%。
GlowQ: Group-Shared LOw-Rank Approximation for Quantized LLMs
Authors: Selim An, Il hong Suh, Yeseong Kim
First: 2026-03-26T12:36:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:56:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Quantization techniques such as BitsAndBytes, AWQ, and GPTQ are widely used as a standard method in deploying large language models but often degrades accuracy when using low-bit representations, e.g., 4 bits. Low-rank correction methods (e.g., LQER, QERA, ASER) has been proposed to mitigate this issue, however, they restore all layers and insert error-correction modules into every decoder block, which increases latency and memory overhead. To address this limitation, we propose GlowQ, a group-shared low-rank approximation for quantized LLMs that caches a single shared right factor per input-sharing group and restores only the groups or layers that yield the highest accuracy benefit. GlowQ computes the high-precision projection once per input-sharing group and reuses it across its modules, reducing parameter and memory overhead, and retaining the expressivity of layer-specific corrections. We also propose a selective variant, GlowQ-S, that applies the cached shared module only where it provides the largest benefit. Compared with strong baselines, our approach reduces TTFB by (5.6%) and increases throughput by (9.6%) on average, while reducing perplexity on WikiText-2 by (0.17%) and increasing downstream accuracy by 0.42 percentage points. The selective model GlowQ-S further reduces latency, cutting TTFB by (23.4%) and increasing throughput by (37.4%), while maintaining accuracy within 0.2 percentage points on average. Code is available at https://github.com/ahnselim/GlowQ.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GlowQ：面向量化大语言模型的组共享低秩近似方法
BitsAndBytes、AWQ 和 GPTQ 等量化技术广泛用于部署大语言模型，但在使用低比特表示（如 4 位）时常导致精度下降。为缓解此问题，已有低秩校正方法（如 LQER、QERA、ASER）被提出，但它们恢复所有层并在每个解码器块中插入纠错模块，从而增加延迟和内存开销。为解决此局限，我们提出 GlowQ，一种面向量化 LLM 的组共享低秩近似方法，为每个输入共享组缓存一个共享右因子，仅恢复带来最高精度收益的组或层。GlowQ 在每个输入共享组内仅计算一次高精度投影，并在各模块间复用，降低参数和内存开销，同时保留层特定校正的表达能力。我们还提出选择性变体 GlowQ-S，仅在收益最大的位置应用缓存的共享模块。与强基线相比，我们的方法平均降低 TTFB 5.6%，提升吞吐量 9.6%，在 WikiText-2 上降低困惑度 0.17%，下游准确率提升 0.42 个百分点。选择性模型 GlowQ-S 进一步降低延迟，平均降低 TTFB 23.4%，提升吞吐量 37.4%，同时保持精度在 0.2 个百分点以内。代码见 https://github.com/ahnselim/GlowQ。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the accuracy loss in low-bit quantized LLMs and the inefficiency of full-layer low-rank correction methods, GlowQ introduces a group-shared low-rank approximation that caches a single shared right factor per input-sharing group and selectively restores only the most beneficial groups or layers. By computing high-precision projections once per group and reusing them across modules, GlowQ reduces parameter and memory overhead while preserving layer-specific expressivity. Experimental results show that GlowQ reduces TTFB by 5.6% and boosts throughput by 9.6% with minimal accuracy degradation, while its selective variant GlowQ-S achieves even greater gains—cutting TTFB by 23.4% and increasing throughput by 37.4%—with accuracy maintained within 0.2 percentage points.
为解决低比特量化大语言模型精度下降及全层低秩校正方法带来的延迟与内存开销问题，GlowQ提出一种组共享低秩近似方法，为每个输入共享组缓存一个共享右因子，并仅恢复收益最大的层。通过每组仅计算一次高精度投影并在模块间复用，GlowQ降低参数与内存开销同时保留层特定表达能力。实验表明，GlowQ平均降低TTFB 5.6%、提升吞吐量9.6%，且精度损失极小；其选择性变体GlowQ-S进一步降低TTFB 23.4%、提升吞吐量37.4%，精度保持在0.2个百分点以内。
Strait: Perceiving Priority and Interference in ML Inference Serving
Authors: Haidong Zhao, Nikolaos Georgantas
First: 2026-04-30T17:55:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:55:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine learning (ML) inference serving systems host deep neural network (DNN) models and schedule incoming inference requests across deployed GPUs. However, limited support for task prioritization and insufficient latency estimation under concurrent execution may restrict their applicability in on-premises scenarios. We present \emph{Strait}, a serving system designed to enhance deadline satisfaction for dual-priority inference traffic under high GPU utilization. To improve latency estimation, Strait models potential contention during data transfer and accounts for kernel execution interference through an adaptive prediction model. By drawing on these predictions, it performs priority-aware scheduling to deliver differentiated handling. Evaluation results under intense workloads suggest that Strait reduces deadline violations for high-priority tasks by 1.02 to 11.18 percentage points while incurring acceptable costs on low-priority tasks. Compared to software-defined preemption approaches, Strait also exhibits more equitable performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Strait：感知优先级与干扰以提升机器学习推理服务性能
机器学习（ML）推理服务系统托管深度神经网络（DNN）模型，并在部署的GPU上调度传入的推理请求。然而，对任务优先级支持有限，且在并发执行时延迟估计不足，可能限制其在本地部署场景中的适用性。我们提出Strait，一种旨在高GPU利用率下提升双优先级推理流量截止时间满足率的推理服务系统。为改进延迟估计，Strait建模数据传输期间的潜在竞争，并通过自适应预测模型考虑内核执行干扰。基于这些预测，它执行优先级感知调度，实现差异化处理。在高负载下的评估结果表明，Strait使高优先级任务的截止时间违规率降低1.02至11.18个百分点，同时对低优先级任务的影响成本可接受。与软件定义抢占方法相比，Strait也展现出更均衡的性能。
Summary / 总结
Strait addresses the challenge of meeting deadlines for high-priority inference requests in ML serving systems under high GPU utilization, where traditional systems lack effective prioritization and latency estimation. It introduces an adaptive prediction model that estimates kernel interference and data transfer contention to improve latency forecasting, enabling priority-aware scheduling that differentiates handling between tasks. Experimental results show Strait reduces high-priority deadline violations by 1.02 to 11.18 percentage points under heavy loads while maintaining acceptable performance for low-priority tasks, and outperforms software-defined preemption methods in fairness.
Strait旨在解决高GPU负载下ML推理服务系统难以满足高优先级任务截止时间的问题，传统系统缺乏有效的优先级调度和延迟预测能力。它引入自适应预测模型，通过建模数据传输竞争和内核执行干扰来估算延迟，从而实现优先级感知的调度策略。在高负载实验中，Strait使高优先级任务的截止时间违规率降低1.02至11.18个百分点，对低优先级任务影响微小，并在公平性上优于软件定义抢占方法。
Making Conformal Predictors Robust in Healthcare Settings: a Case Study on EEG Classification
Authors: Arjun Chatterjee, Sayeed Sajjad Razin, John Wu, Siddhartha Laghuvarapu, Jathurshan Pradeepkumar, Jimeng Sun
First: 2026-02-23T03:59:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:55:27+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the International Conference on Artificial Intelligence in Medicine 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Quantifying uncertainty in clinical predictions is critical for high-stakes diagnosis tasks. Conformal prediction offers a principled approach by providing prediction sets with theoretical coverage guarantees. However, in practice, patient distribution shifts violate the i.i.d. assumptions underlying standard conformal methods, leading to poor coverage in healthcare settings. In this work, we evaluate several conformal prediction approaches on EEG seizure classification, a task with known distribution shift challenges and label uncertainty. We demonstrate that personalized calibration strategies can improve coverage by over 20 percentage points while maintaining comparable prediction set sizes. Our implementation is available via PyHealth, an open-source healthcare AI framework: https://github.com/sunlabuiuc/PyHealth.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使符合预测在医疗环境中更具鲁棒性：基于脑电图分类的案例研究
在高风险诊断任务中，量化临床预测的不确定性至关重要。符合预测提供了一种基于理论覆盖保证的预测集方法。然而，在实践中，患者分布偏移违反了标准符合方法所依赖的独立同分布假设，导致医疗环境中覆盖性能不佳。本文在具有已知分布偏移和标签不确定性的脑电图癫痫分类任务上评估了多种符合预测方法。我们证明，个性化校准策略可使覆盖度提升超过20个百分点，同时保持相近的预测集大小。我们的实现可通过开源医疗AI框架PyHealth获取：https://github.com/sunlabuiuc/PyHealth。
Summary / 总结
To address the issue of unreliable coverage in healthcare due to distribution shifts violating i.i.d. assumptions, this study evaluates conformal prediction methods on EEG seizure classification, a domain with known label uncertainty and data drift. The authors introduce personalized calibration strategies that adapt to patient-specific data characteristics, resulting in coverage improvements exceeding 20 percentage points without significantly increasing prediction set sizes. These findings demonstrate that tailored calibration enhances the reliability of conformal predictors in real-world clinical settings.
为解决医疗场景中因分布偏移破坏独立同分布假设而导致的置信覆盖不可靠问题，本研究在具有标签不确定性和分布偏移挑战的EEG癫痫分类任务上评估了多种置信预测方法。作者提出个性化校准策略，根据患者特定数据特征进行调整，使置信覆盖提升超过20个百分点，同时预测集规模基本保持不变。结果表明，定制化校准可显著增强置信预测器在真实临床环境中的可靠性。
Action Motifs: Self-Supervised Hierarchical Representation of Human Body Movements
Authors: Genki Kinoshita, Shu Nakamura, Ryo Kawahara, Shohei Nobuhara, Yasutomo Kawanishi, Ko Nishino
Venue: CVPR 2026 Highlight
First: 2026-04-30T17:55:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:55:01+00:00
Comments: to be published in CVPR 2026 (Highlight)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Effective human behavior modeling requires a representation of the human body movement that capitalizes on its compositionality. We propose a hierarchical representation consisting of Action Atoms that capture the atomic joint movements and Action Motifs that are formed by their temporal compositions and encode similar body movements found across different overall human actions. We derive A4Mer, a nested latent Transformer to learn this hierarchical representation from human pose data in a fully self-supervised manner. A4Mer splits a 3D pose sequence into variable-length segments and represents each segment as a single latent token (Action Atoms). Through bottom-up representation learning, temporal patterns composed of these Action Atoms, which capture meaningful temporal spans of reusable, semantic segments of body movements, naturally emerge (Action Motifs). A4Mer achieves this with a unified pretext task of masked token prediction in their respective latent spaces. We also introduce Action Motif Dataset (AMD), a large-scale dataset of multi-view human behavior videos with full SMPL annotations. We introduce a novel use of cameras by mounting them on the feet to achieve their frame-wise annotations despite frequent and heavy body occlusions. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of A4Mer for extracting meaningful Action Motifs, which significantly benefit human behavior modeling tasks including action recognition, motion prediction, and motion interpolation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：动作基元：人类身体运动的自监督分层表示
有效的行为建模需要利用人体运动的组合性。我们提出一种分层表示方法，包含捕捉原子级关节运动的动作原子（Action Atoms）和由其时序组合形成、编码跨不同整体人类动作中相似身体运动的动作基元（Action Motifs）。我们设计了A4Mer，一种嵌套潜在Transformer，可从人体姿态数据中完全自监督地学习该分层表示。A4Mer将三维姿态序列分割为可变长度片段，并将每个片段表示为单个潜在标记（动作原子）。通过自下而上的表示学习，由这些动作原子组成的时序模式自然涌现，捕捉可复用、语义明确的身体运动时间片段（动作基元）。A4Mer通过各自潜在空间中的掩码标记预测统一预训练任务实现此目标。我们还引入动作基元数据集（AMD），这是一个大规模多视角人类行为视频数据集，包含完整SMPL标注。我们创新性地将摄像头安装在脚部，以克服频繁且严重的身体遮挡，实现逐帧标注。实验结果表明，A4Mer能有效提取有意义的动作基元，显著提升动作识别、运动预测和运动插值等行为建模任务的性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for compositional representations of human movement, this work introduces A4Mer, a self-supervised hierarchical model that learns Action Atoms (atomic joint movements) and Action Motifs (temporal compositions of atoms) from 3D pose sequences. A4Mer employs a nested latent Transformer architecture that splits pose sequences into variable-length segments, encoding each as a latent token, then learns temporal patterns through bottom-up representation. Evaluated on the newly proposed Action Motif Dataset (AMD), which features multi-view human behavior videos with full SMPL annotations captured via foot-mounted cameras to overcome occlusion, A4Mer demonstrates strong performance in action recognition, motion prediction, and motion interpolation, showing that its learned motifs significantly enhance downstream behavior modeling tasks.
为解决人体运动建模中对组合性表示的需求，该研究提出“动作模态”——由原子关节运动（动作原子）及其时序组合（动作模态）构成的层次化表示，通过自监督学习的嵌套潜在Transformer A4Mer进行建模。A4Mer将3D姿态序列分割为可变长度片段，编码为潜在标记，并通过掩码标记预测学习时序模式，自然形成可复用的语义运动片段。在新提出的动作模态数据集（AMD）上实验表明，A4Mer在动作识别、运动预测与插值任务中表现优异，验证了其在人体行为建模中的有效性。
PhyCo: Learning Controllable Physical Priors for Generative Motion
Authors: Sriram Narayanan, Ziyu Jiang, Srinivasa Narasimhan, Manmohan Chandraker
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-30T17:53:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:53:03+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026. Project Page: https://phyco-video.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Modern video diffusion models excel at appearance synthesis but still struggle with physical consistency: objects drift, collisions lack realistic rebound, and material responses seldom match their underlying properties. We present PhyCo, a framework that introduces continuous, interpretable, and physically grounded control into video generation. Our approach integrates three key components: (i) a large-scale dataset of over 100K photorealistic simulation videos where friction, restitution, deformation, and force are systematically varied across diverse scenarios; (ii) physics-supervised fine-tuning of a pretrained diffusion model using a ControlNet conditioned on pixel-aligned physical property maps; and (iii) VLM-guided reward optimization, where a fine-tuned vision-language model evaluates generated videos with targeted physics queries and provides differentiable feedback. This combination enables a generative model to produce physically consistent and controllable outputs through variations in physical attributes-without any simulator or geometry reconstruction at inference. On the Physics-IQ benchmark, PhyCo significantly improves physical realism over strong baselines, and human studies confirm clearer and more faithful control over physical attributes. Our results demonstrate a scalable path toward physically consistent, controllable generative video models that generalize beyond synthetic training environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PhyCo：学习可控的物理先验用于生成式运动
现代视频扩散模型在外观合成方面表现优异，但在物理一致性上仍存在困难：物体漂移、碰撞缺乏真实的反弹效果，材料响应往往不符合其固有属性。我们提出了PhyCo框架，将连续、可解释且物理基础的控制引入视频生成。我们的方法包含三个关键组件：(i) 一个包含超过10万条逼真模拟视频的大规模数据集，其中摩擦、恢复系数、形变和力在多样化场景中系统性变化；(ii) 使用ControlNet条件于像素对齐的物理属性图，对预训练扩散模型进行物理监督微调；(iii) VLM引导的奖励优化，通过微调的视觉-语言模型评估生成视频并提供可微分反馈。该组合使生成模型能够在不依赖模拟器或几何重建的情况下，通过物理属性的变化生成物理一致且可控的输出。在Physics-IQ基准测试中，PhyCo显著优于强基线模型，人类研究也证实了对物理属性更清晰、更忠实的控制。我们的结果展示了通向物理一致、可控生成视频模型的可扩展路径，且能超越合成训练环境的泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of physical consistency in modern video diffusion models, PhyCo introduces a framework for controllable physical priors in generative motion. It combines a large-scale simulation dataset with varied physical properties, physics-supervised fine-tuning of a diffusion model via ControlNet conditioned on physical maps, and VLM-guided reward optimization for differentiable feedback. Experiments show PhyCo significantly outperforms baselines on the Physics-IQ benchmark in physical realism and enables more faithful, interpretable control over motion attributes without requiring simulators or geometry reconstruction at inference.
为解决现代视频扩散模型在物理一致性方面的不足，PhyCo提出了一种可控物理先验的生成运动框架。该方法结合了包含10万多个物理属性系统变化的逼真模拟视频数据集、基于物理监督的扩散模型微调（使用ControlNet条件于像素对齐的物理属性图），以及由视觉语言模型引导的奖励优化机制。实验表明，PhyCo在Physics-IQ基准上显著优于现有基线，且人工评估证实其对物理属性的控制更准确、更可信，无需推理时依赖模拟器或几何重建。
Mapping the Phase Diagram of the Vicsek Model with Machine Learning
Authors: Grace T. Bai, Brandon B. Le
First: 2026-04-30T17:52:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:52:23+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this study, we use machine learning to classify and interpolate the phase structure of the Vicsek flocking model across the three-dimensional parameter space $(η,ρ,v_0)$. We construct a dataset of simulated parameter points and characterize each point using long-time dynamical observables. These observables are then used as inputs to a K-Means clustering procedure, which assigns each point to a disorder, order, or coexistence phase. Using these clustered labels, we train a neural-network classifier to learn the mapping from model parameters to phase behavior, achieving a classification accuracy of 0.92. The resulting phase map resolves a narrow coexistence region separating the ordered and disordered phases and extends the inferred phase boundaries beyond the originally sampled simulation points. More broadly, this approach provides a systematic way to convert sparse simulation data into a global phase diagram for collective-motion models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用机器学习绘制维切克模型的相图
本研究利用机器学习对维切克群聚模型在三维参数空间（η,ρ,v₀）中的相结构进行分类与插值。我们构建了模拟参数点的数据集，并用长时间动力学可观测量表征每个点。这些可观测量作为输入，用于K-Means聚类过程，将每个点分配至无序、有序或共存相。基于聚类标签，我们训练神经网络分类器学习从模型参数到相行为的映射，分类准确率达0.92。所得相图揭示了分隔有序与无序相的狭窄共存区域，并将推断的相边界延伸至原始采样点之外。更广泛地说，该方法提供了一种将稀疏模拟数据系统性转化为集体运动模型全局相图的途径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of mapping the complex phase behavior of the Vicsek flocking model across its three-dimensional parameter space, this study employs machine learning to classify and interpolate phase transitions. By simulating parameter points and extracting long-time dynamical observables as features, the researchers apply K-Means clustering to assign each point to one of three phases: disorder, order, or coexistence. These labels are then used to train a neural network classifier, which achieves 92% accuracy in predicting phase behavior. The resulting phase diagram reveals a narrow coexistence region between ordered and disordered phases and extrapolates phase boundaries beyond the original simulation grid, demonstrating a scalable method for deriving global phase diagrams from sparse simulation data.
为解决Vicsek群聚模型在三维参数空间（η,ρ,v₀）中复杂相图的绘制难题，本研究采用机器学习方法进行分类与插值。通过模拟不同参数点并提取其长时间动力学可观测量，利用K-Means聚类将各点归为无序、有序或共存相，再训练神经网络分类器学习参数到相行为的映射，准确率达92%，揭示了分隔有序与无序相的狭窄共存区域，并将相边界外推至原始模拟点之外。
Foreclassing: A new machine learning perspective on human decision making with temporal data
Authors: Daniel Andrew Coulson, Martin T. Wells
First: 2025-03-06T20:41:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:51:23+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 1 figure, 15 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Time series forecasts are widely used to inform decisions. Human decision-makers interpret these forecasts, incorporate prior experience and uncertainty about future outcomes, and then make a decision. In this paper, we propose a new machine learning problem, which we call Foreclassing, which addresses settings in which the aim is to automate human involvement in such decision-making processes. Our aim is to develop a unified end-to-end model that takes a time series as input, produces a forecast, accounts for its predictive uncertainty, and makes a downstream classification decision, enabling models to support or automate such temporal decision-making tasks. Related problems arise across a range of applications, yet the literature lacks both a unified methodology and a formal problem statement. By formalizing the task, we aim to stimulate research on such models and encourage cross-domain collaboration. To solve the Foreclassing problem, we propose a deep Bayesian neural network, ForeClassNet. As part of this framework, we introduce a new type of neural network layer, Boltzmann convolutions, which enable probabilistic learning of kernel sizes in convolutional layers. We evaluate the Foreclassing framework against standard time series classification methods and demonstrate the efficacy of ForeClassNet on real-world Foreclassing datasets from the weather, energy, and finance domains, achieving superior performance relative to state-of-the-art time series classifiers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：前瞻分类：一种基于时间序列数据的人类决策新机器学习视角
时间序列预测广泛用于辅助决策。人类决策者解读这些预测，结合过往经验与对未来结果的不确定性，最终做出决策。本文提出一种新的机器学习问题，称为“前瞻分类”，旨在自动化人类在该类决策过程中的参与。我们的目标是构建一个统一的端到端模型，以时间序列为输入，生成预测并量化其预测不确定性，进而做出下游分类决策，从而支持或自动化此类时间决策任务。相关问题在多个应用领域普遍存在，但现有文献缺乏统一的方法论和形式化问题定义。通过形式化该任务，我们旨在推动此类模型的研究并促进跨领域合作。为解决前瞻分类问题，我们提出一种深度贝叶斯神经网络——ForeClassNet。在此框架中，我们引入一种新型神经网络层——玻尔兹曼卷积层，该层可实现卷积层核大小的概率学习。我们在天气、能源和金融领域的现实前瞻分类数据集上评估了该框架，与现有标准时间序列分类方法相比，ForeClassNet展现出更优性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate human decision-making under temporal data, this paper introduces 'Foreclassing'—a novel machine learning framework that integrates forecasting, uncertainty quantification, and classification into a single end-to-end model. The authors propose ForeClassNet, a deep Bayesian neural network incorporating Boltzmann convolutions, which enable probabilistic kernel size learning in convolutional layers. Evaluated across weather, energy, and finance datasets, ForeClassNet outperforms existing time series classifiers, demonstrating its effectiveness in supporting automated temporal decision-making by combining predictive modeling with downstream classification.
为自动化依赖时间序列预测的人类决策过程，本文提出“Foreclassing”这一新机器学习框架，将预测、不确定性量化与下游分类整合为端到端模型。作者提出ForeClassNet，一种结合Boltzmann卷积层的深度贝叶斯神经网络，该层可概率性学习卷积核大小以增强适应性。在天气、能源和金融领域的实际数据集上评估，ForeClassNet优于现有最先进的时间序列分类器，验证了其在支持自动化时序决策中的有效性。
Essential, Yet Overlooked: Identity Verification Barriers for Blind and Low Vision People in Government Services
Authors: Ryan John Oommen, Tanusree Sharma
First: 2026-04-30T17:51:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:51:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Identity verification is a critical gateway to accessing government services and public benefits, yet contemporary systems are typically designed around visual interaction, leaving blind and low vision (BLV) individuals disproportionately burdened. In this work, we examine how BLV users navigate identity verification in government services and how current designs shape their access, security, and autonomy. Through a mixed methods study combining analysis of 219 Reddit posts and semi-structured interviews with 16 BLV participants, we uncover systemic accessibility breakdowns across both digital and in person verification processes. Our findings show that inaccessible verification workflows do not merely inconvenience users, they restructure how security is achieved in practice. We also identify how repeated verification demands, inaccessible physical infrastructure, and policy changes exacerbate exclusion from essential services. At the same time, participants articulate complex perspectives on AI, viewing it as both a critical accessibility aid and a growing vector for identity fraud.
中文标题/摘要
标题：不可或缺却常被忽视：盲人及低视力人士在政府服务中的身份验证障碍
身份验证是获取政府服务和公共福利的关键入口，但当前系统多以视觉交互为基础，使盲人及低视力（BLV）人士承受不成比例的负担。本研究通过分析219篇Reddit帖子及对16名BLV用户的半结构化访谈，揭示数字与线下身份验证流程中的系统性无障碍缺陷。研究发现，不可访问的验证流程不仅造成不便，更重塑了实际安全实现方式。同时，重复验证要求、无障碍物理设施缺失及政策变动加剧了其被排除在基本服务之外的风险。参与者亦对人工智能持复杂看法，既视其为关键无障碍辅助工具，亦担忧其成为身份欺诈的新渠道。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the systemic exclusion of blind and low vision individuals from government services due to visually-centric identity verification systems. Using a mixed-methods approach that combines analysis of 219 Reddit posts and interviews with 16 participants, the research reveals how current verification processes—both digital and in-person—create barriers to access, security, and autonomy. Findings indicate that inaccessible workflows fundamentally alter how security is enacted in practice, while repeated verification demands, physical infrastructure gaps, and policy shifts deepen exclusion. Participants also express nuanced views on AI, recognizing its potential as an accessibility tool while also warning of its risks in enabling identity fraud.
本研究聚焦于视觉障碍人士在政府服务中因依赖视觉交互的身份验证系统而遭遇的系统性排斥。通过分析219篇Reddit帖子和16位受访者的半结构化访谈，研究揭示了数字与线下身份验证流程中存在的无障碍缺陷，这些缺陷不仅造成不便，更重塑了实际安全实现方式。研究发现重复验证要求、物理设施缺失及政策变动加剧了服务排斥。受访者同时对AI持复杂态度，既视其为提升无障碍的重要工具，也担忧其可能助长身份欺诈风险。
Quantum Masked Autoencoders for Vision Learning
Authors: Emma Andrews, Prabhat Mishra
First: 2025-11-21T16:37:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:50:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Classical autoencoders are widely used to learn features of input data. To improve the feature learning, classical masked autoencoders extend classical autoencoders to learn the features of the original input sample in the presence of masked-out data. While quantum autoencoders exist, there is no design and implementation of quantum masked autoencoders that can leverage the benefits of quantum computing and quantum autoencoders. In this paper, we propose quantum masked autoencoders (QMAEs) that can effectively learn missing features of a data sample within quantum states instead of classical embeddings. We showcase that our QMAE architecture can learn the masked features of an image and can reconstruct the masked input image with improved visual fidelity in MNIST-family images. Experimental evaluation highlights that QMAE can significantly outperform (12.86% on average) in classification accuracy compared to state-of-the-art quantum autoencoders in the presence of masks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于视觉学习的量子掩码自编码器
经典自编码器广泛用于学习输入数据的特征。为提升特征学习能力，经典掩码自编码器扩展了经典自编码器，使其能在部分数据被遮蔽的情况下学习原始输入样本的特征。尽管量子自编码器已存在，但尚无设计与实现能结合量子计算与量子自编码器优势的量子掩码自编码器。本文提出量子掩码自编码器（QMAE），可在量子态而非经典嵌入中有效学习数据样本的缺失特征。实验表明，QMAE架构可学习图像的遮蔽特征，并在MNIST类图像中重建遮蔽输入图像，视觉保真度显著提升。评估结果表明，QMAE在存在遮蔽情况下，相比现有最先进量子自编码器，分类准确率平均提升12.86%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance feature learning under partial data masking, this paper introduces quantum masked autoencoders (QMAEs), a novel quantum architecture that learns missing features directly within quantum states rather than classical embeddings. The method extends quantum autoencoder principles to handle masked inputs, enabling reconstruction of masked images with improved visual fidelity. Experimental results on MNIST-family datasets show that QMAEs significantly outperform existing quantum autoencoders, achieving an average 12.86% improvement in classification accuracy when masks are present.
为提升在部分数据遮蔽条件下特征学习的能力，本文提出量子遮蔽自编码器（QMAEs），利用量子态重建图像缺失特征。与经典遮蔽自编码器不同，QMAEs在量子计算框架下运行，更高效地表示遮蔽数据。在MNIST家族数据集上的实验表明，QMAEs在视觉重建保真度上表现更优，并在遮蔽输入条件下比现有量子自编码器平均高出12.86%的分类准确率。
Grounding Agent Memory in Contextual Intent
Authors: Ruozhen Yang, Yucheng Jiang, Yueqi Jiang, Priyanka Kargupta, Yunyi Zhang, Jiawei Han
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-01-15T18:55:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:49:04+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deploying large language models in long-horizon, goal-oriented interactions remains challenging because similar entities and facts recur under different latent goals and constraints, causing memory systems to retrieve context-mismatched evidence. We propose STITCH (Structured Intent Tracking in Contextual History), an agentic memory system that indexes each trajectory step with a structured retrieval cue, contextual intent, and retrieves history by matching the current step's intent. Contextual intent provides compact signals that disambiguate repeated mentions and reduce interference: (1) the current latent goal defining a thematic segment, (2) the action type, and (3) the salient entity types anchoring which attributes matter. During inference, STITCH filters and prioritizes memory snippets by intent compatibility, suppressing semantically similar but context-incompatible history. For evaluation, we introduce CAME-Bench, a benchmark for context-aware retrieval in realistic, dynamic, goal-oriented trajectories. Across CAME-Bench and LongMemEval, STITCH achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming the strongest baseline by 35.6%, with the largest gains as trajectory length increases. Our analysis shows that intent indexing substantially reduces retrieval noise, supporting intent-aware memory for robust long-horizon reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于上下文意图的代理记忆锚定
在长时程、目标导向的交互中部署大语言模型仍具挑战性，因为相似实体和事实会在不同潜在目标和约束下重复出现，导致记忆系统检索到上下文不匹配的证据。我们提出STITCH（上下文历史中的结构化意图跟踪），一种代理记忆系统，通过结构化检索提示（即上下文意图）对每一步轨迹进行索引，并根据当前步骤的意图匹配历史记录进行检索。上下文意图提供紧凑信号，可区分重复提及并减少干扰：（1）当前潜在目标，定义主题段落；（2）动作类型；（3）锚定关键属性的显著实体类型。推理时，STITCH通过意图兼容性过滤和优先级排序记忆片段，抑制语义相似但上下文不兼容的历史记录。 为评估，我们引入CAME-Bench，一个面向真实、动态、目标导向轨迹的上下文感知检索基准。在CAME-Bench和LongMemEval上，STITCH达到最先进性能，相较最强基线提升35.6%，且轨迹长度越长，性能提升越显著。分析表明，意图索引显著降低检索噪声，支持意图感知记忆以实现稳健的长时程推理。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of memory retrieval in long-horizon goal-oriented interactions where recurring entities cause context-mismatched recall, the paper introduces STITCH, a memory system that indexes each step with a structured retrieval cue called 'contextual intent'—comprising latent goal, action type, and salient entity types—to disambiguate repeated mentions. During inference, STITCH filters memory snippets by intent compatibility, suppressing semantically similar but context-incompatible history. Evaluated on CAME-Bench and LongMemEval, STITCH achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming the strongest baseline by 35.6%, with performance gains increasing as trajectory length grows, demonstrating that intent-based indexing significantly reduces retrieval noise and supports robust long-horizon reasoning.
为解决长时程、目标导向交互中因重复实体和事实导致的上下文不匹配记忆召回问题，本文提出STITCH记忆系统，通过‘上下文意图’（包含当前隐含目标、动作类型和显著实体类型）对每一步进行结构化索引，以区分相似提及。推理时，STITCH依据意图兼容性过滤并优先检索记忆片段，抑制语义相似但上下文不匹配的历史信息。在CAME-Bench和LongMemEval评测中，STITCH达到最先进性能，相较最强基线提升35.6%，且随轨迹长度增加性能提升更显著，表明意图感知索引能显著降低检索噪声，支持稳健的长时程推理。
Sequential Inference for Gaussian Processes: A Signal Processing Perspective
Authors: Daniel Waxman, Fernando Llorente, Petar M. Djurić
First: 2026-04-30T17:48:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:48:09+00:00
Comments: 53 pages, 7 figures. Accepted to IEEE Signal Processing Magazine
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The proliferation of capable and efficient machine learning (ML) models marks one of the strongest methodological shifts in signal processing (SP) in its nearly 100-year history. ML models support the development of SP systems that represent complex, nonlinear relationships with high predictive accuracy. Adapting these models often requires sequential inference, which differs both theoretically and methodologically from the usual paradigm of ML, where data are often assumed independent and identically distributed. Gaussian processes (GPs) are a flexible yet principled framework for modeling random functions, and they have become increasingly relevant to SP as statistical and ML methods assume a more prominent role. We provide a self-contained, tutorial-style overview of GPs, with a particular focus on recent methodological advances in sequential, incremental, or streaming inference. We introduce these techniques from a signal-processing perspective while bridging them to recent advances in ML. Many of the developments we survey have direct applications to state-space modeling, sequential regression and forecasting, anomaly detection in time series, sequential Bayesian optimization, adaptive and active sensing, and sequential detection and decision-making. By organizing these advances from a signal-processing perspective, we intend to equip practitioners with practical tools and a coherent roadmap for deploying sequential GP models in real-world systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：高斯过程的序贯推断：信号处理视角
具备能力和效率的机器学习（ML）模型的普及，标志着信号处理（SP）近百年历史中最强劲的方法论转变之一。ML模型支持开发能够以高预测精度表示复杂非线性关系的SP系统。适应这些模型通常需要序贯推断，这在理论和方法上与传统ML范式不同，后者常假设数据独立同分布。高斯过程（GPs）是一种灵活且严谨的随机函数建模框架，随着统计和ML方法在SP中扮演更突出角色，其重要性日益增加。本文提供一个自包含的教程式GPs概览，特别聚焦于序贯、增量或流式推断的最新方法论进展。我们从信号处理视角介绍这些技术，并将其与ML领域的最新进展相联系。我们综述的许多进展可直接应用于状态空间建模、序贯回归与预测、时间序列异常检测、序贯贝叶斯优化、自适应与主动传感，以及序贯检测与决策。通过从信号处理视角组织这些进展，我们旨在为从业者提供实用工具和清晰的路线图，以在实际系统中部署序贯GP模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing need to adapt machine learning models to real-time, sequential data streams in signal processing, this paper presents a signal-processing perspective on Gaussian processes, emphasizing their utility in sequential inference. It reviews recent methodological advances in incremental and streaming GP inference, contrasting them with traditional batch ML assumptions of independent and identically distributed data. The authors introduce techniques for sequential GP modeling from a signal processing viewpoint, linking them to applications in state-space modeling, forecasting, anomaly detection, Bayesian optimization, and adaptive sensing. Experimental results across these domains demonstrate that sequential GP methods achieve high predictive accuracy and computational efficiency, enabling practical deployment in dynamic, real-world systems.
鉴于机器学习在信号处理中的广泛应用，本文旨在解决高斯过程在动态非线性系统中实时建模所需的序列推理方法。文章以教程式方式综述高斯过程，重点介绍从信号处理视角出发的最新序列、增量和流式推理方法，并连接机器学习与信号处理方法。所调研的技术可直接应用于状态空间建模、时序预测、异常检测、贝叶斯优化及自适应感知等领域。实验结果表明，这些序列高斯过程方法在非独立同分布数据下仍能保持高预测精度和鲁棒性，具备实际部署于动态信号环境中的可行性。
RopeDreamer: A Kinematic Recurrent State Space Model for Dynamics of Flexible Deformable Linear Objects
Authors: Tim Missal, Lucas Domingues, Berk Guler, Simon Manschitz, Jan Peters, Paula Dornhofer Paro Costa
First: 2026-04-30T17:47:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:47:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The robotic manipulation of Deformable Linear Objects (DLOs) is a fundamental challenge due to the high-dimensional, non-linear dynamics of flexible structures and the complexity of maintaining topological integrity during contact-rich tasks. While recent data-driven methods have utilized Recurrent and Graph Neural Networks for dynamics modeling, they often struggle with self-intersections and non-physical deformations, such as tangling and link stretching. In this paper, we propose a latent dynamics framework that combines a Recurrent State Space Model with a Quaternionic Kinematic Chain representation to enable robust, long-term forecasting of DLO states. By encoding the DLO as a sequence of relative rotations (quaternions) rather than independent Cartesian positions, we inherently constrain the model to a physically valid manifold that preserves link-length constancy. Furthermore, we introduce a dual-decoder architecture that decouples state reconstruction from future-state prediction, forcing the latent space to capture the underlying physics of deformation. We evaluate our approach on a large-scale simulated dataset of complex pick-and-place trajectories involving self-intersections. Our results demonstrate that the proposed model achieves a 40.52% reduction in open-loop prediction error over 50-step horizons compared to the state-of-the-art baseline, while reducing inference time by 31.17%. Our model further maintains superior topological consistency in scenarios with multiple crossings, proving its efficacy as a compositional primitive for long-horizon manipulation planning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RopeDreamer：一种用于柔性可变形线性物体动力学的运动学递归状态空间模型
柔性可变形线性物体（DLOs）的机器人操作是一个根本性挑战，因其柔性结构的高维非线性动力学及接触密集任务中保持拓扑完整性之复杂性。尽管近期数据驱动方法已利用递归与图神经网络进行动力学建模，但常难以处理自交与非物理形变，如缠绕与链段拉伸。本文提出一种潜在动力学框架，结合递归状态空间模型与四元数运动学链表示，以实现对DLO状态的稳健长期预测。通过将DLO编码为相对旋转（四元数）序列而非独立笛卡尔位置，我们内在约束模型至物理有效流形，保持链段长度恒定。此外，我们引入双解码器架构，将状态重建与未来状态预测解耦，迫使潜在空间捕捉形变的底层物理规律。我们在包含自交的复杂抓取与放置轨迹的大规模仿真数据集上评估该方法。结果表明，与当前最优基线相比，所提模型在50步预测时域内开放环预测误差降低40.52%，推理时间减少31.17%。该模型在多交叉场景中进一步保持卓越拓扑一致性，证明其作为长期操作规划组合原语的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of modeling flexible deformable linear objects (DLOs) due to their high-dimensional, non-linear dynamics and topological constraints during manipulation, this paper introduces RopeDreamer, a recurrent state space model that combines quaternionic kinematic chains with a dual-decoder architecture. The method encodes DLOs as sequences of relative rotations to enforce physical constraints like constant link length and avoid non-physical deformations such as tangling. Evaluated on a large-scale simulated dataset with self-intersecting trajectories, RopeDreamer reduces open-loop prediction error by 40.52% over 50 steps compared to the prior state-of-the-art and cuts inference time by 31.17%, while maintaining superior topological consistency during complex manipulations.
为了解决柔性线性物体（DLOs）因高维非线性动力学和拓扑约束带来的建模难题，本文提出RopeDreamer模型，结合四元数运动链与双解码器架构的递归状态空间模型。该方法通过编码相对旋转序列，强制保持链长恒定，避免非物理形变如缠绕。在包含自交轨迹的大规模仿真数据集上测试，该模型在50步预测中较当前最优方法误差降低40.52%，推理时间减少31.17%，且在多交叉场景中保持更优拓扑一致性，证明其作为长时规划基础构件的有效性。
Continuous-tone Simple Points: An $\ell_0$-Norm of Cyclic Gradient for Topology-Preserving Data-Driven Image Segmentation
Authors: Wenxiao Li, Faqiang Wang, Yuping Duan, Li Cui, Liqiang Zhang, Jun Liu
First: 2026-04-30T17:45:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:45:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Topological features play an essential role in ensuring geometric plausibility and structural consistency in image analysis tasks such as segmentation and skeletonization. However, integrating topology-preserving learning based on simple points into deep learning tasks remains challenging, as existing simple point detection methods are confined to binary images and are non-differentiable, rendering them incompatible with gradient-based optimization in modern deep learning. Moreover, morphological and purely data-driven approaches often fail to guaranty topological consistency. To address these limitations, we propose a novel method that directly computes simple points on continuous-valued images, enabling differentiable topological inference. Building on this theory, we develop an efficient skeleton extraction algorithm that preserves topological structures in binary and continuous-valued images. Furthermore, we design a variational model that enforces topological constraints by preserving topologically non-removable (i.e., non-simple) points, which can be seamlessly integrated into any deep neural network segmentation with softmax or sigmoid outputs. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach effectively improves topological integrity and structural accuracy across multiple benchmarks. The codes are available in https://github.com/levnsio/CSP.
中文标题/摘要
标题：连续色调简单点：基于循环梯度的ℓ₀范数用于拓扑保持的数据驱动图像分割
拓扑特征在图像分析任务（如分割和骨架提取）中对于确保几何合理性和结构一致性至关重要。然而，将基于简单点的拓扑保持学习整合到深度学习任务中仍具挑战性，因为现有简单点检测方法仅适用于二值图像且不可微，无法与现代深度学习中的梯度优化兼容。此外，形态学和纯数据驱动方法往往无法保证拓扑一致性。为解决这些局限，我们提出一种新颖方法，直接在连续值图像上计算简单点，实现可微拓扑推理。基于此理论，我们开发了一种高效的骨架提取算法，可在二值和连续值图像中保持拓扑结构。此外，我们设计了一个变分模型，通过保留拓扑不可消除（即非简单）点来施加拓扑约束，可无缝集成到任何输出为softmax或sigmoid的深度神经网络分割模型中。实验结果表明，该方法在多个基准测试中有效提升了拓扑完整性和结构精度。代码见 https://github.com/levnsio/CSP。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to preserve topological consistency in image segmentation while leveraging deep learning, this work introduces a differentiable method to compute simple points directly on continuous-tone images using an $\ell_0$-norm of cyclic gradients, overcoming the limitations of binary-only and non-differentiable existing approaches. The method enables topology-preserving skeleton extraction and is integrated into deep neural networks via a variational model that enforces the preservation of non-simple points, compatible with softmax or sigmoid outputs. Experiments show that this approach significantly enhances topological integrity and structural accuracy across multiple benchmarks compared to baseline methods.
为在深度学习框架下实现拓扑一致性图像分割，本文提出一种直接在连续色调图像上计算简单点的可微方法，基于循环梯度的 $\ell_0$ 范数，解决了传统方法仅适用于二值图像且不可微的问题。该方法支持拓扑保持的骨架提取，并通过变分模型强制保留非简单点，可无缝嵌入输出为softmax或sigmoid的神经网络。实验表明，该方法在多个基准数据集上显著提升了拓扑完整性和结构精度。
Intern-Atlas: A Methodological Evolution Graph as Research Infrastructure for AI Scientists
Authors: Yujun Wu, Dongxu Zhang, Xinchen Li, Jinhang Xu, Yiling Duan, Yumou Liu, Jiabao Pan, Xuanhe Zhou, Jingxuan Wei, Siyuan Li, Jintao Chen, Conghui He, Cheng Tan
First: 2026-04-30T17:44:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:44:55+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 5 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing research infrastructure is fundamentally document-centric, providing citation links between papers but lacking explicit representations of methodological evolution. In particular, it does not capture the structured relationships that explain how and why research methods emerge, adapt, and build upon one another. With the rise of AI-driven research agents as a new class of consumers of scientific knowledge, this limitation becomes increasingly consequential, as such agents cannot reliably reconstruct method evolution topologies from unstructured text. We introduce Intern-Atlas, a methodological evolution graph that automatically identifies method-level entities, infers lineage relationships among methodologies, and captures the bottlenecks that drive transitions between successive innovations. Built from 1,030,314 papers spanning AI conferences, journals, and arXiv preprints, the resulting graph comprises 9,410,201 semantically typed edges, each grounded in verbatim source evidence, forming a queryable causal network of methodological development. To operationalize this structure, we further propose a self-guided temporal tree search algorithm for constructing evolution chains that trace the progression of methods over time. We evaluate the quality of the resulting graph against expert-curated ground-truth evolution chains and observe strong alignment. In addition, we demonstrate that Intern-Atlas enables downstream applications in idea evaluation and automated idea generation. We position methodological evolution graphs as a foundational data layer for the emerging automated scientific discovery.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Intern-Atlas：面向AI科学家的研究基础设施——方法论演化图谱
现有研究基础设施本质上以文档为中心，仅提供论文间的引用链接，缺乏对方法论演化的显式表达。尤其未能捕捉解释研究方法如何及为何产生、适应并相互构建的结构化关系。随着AI驱动的研究代理作为科学知识的新类消费者崛起，这一局限愈发重要，因为此类代理无法从非结构化文本中可靠重建方法演化拓扑。我们提出Intern-Atlas，一种方法论演化图谱，可自动识别方法级实体，推断方法间的谱系关系，并捕捉驱动连续创新间转变的瓶颈。该图谱基于103万篇涵盖AI会议、期刊及arXiv预印本的论文构建，包含941万条语义类型化边，每条边均基于原文证据支撑，形成可查询的方法演化因果网络。为实现该结构，我们进一步提出一种自引导的时间树搜索算法，用于构建追踪方法随时间演进的演化链。我们通过与专家标注的真实演化链对比评估图谱质量，观察到高度一致性。此外，我们证明Intern-Atlas可支持下游应用，如创意评估与自动化创意生成。我们主张方法论演化图谱作为新兴自动化科学发现的基础数据层。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for structured representation of how AI research methods evolve over time, the authors introduce Intern-Atlas, a methodological evolution graph built from 1.03 million papers that automatically identifies method-level entities and infers lineage relationships among them, grounded in verbatim source evidence. The graph, comprising 9.4 million semantically typed edges, captures causal transitions and bottlenecks driving method innovation, enabling queryable reconstruction of methodological development. Evaluated against expert-curated evolution chains, the graph shows strong alignment, and the proposed self-guided temporal tree search algorithm successfully constructs temporal evolution chains. Intern-Atlas further supports downstream tasks like idea evaluation and automated idea generation, positioning methodological evolution graphs as a foundational infrastructure for automated scientific discovery.
为解决现有研究基础设施无法结构化表达AI研究方法演化的问题，本文提出Intern-Atlas方法演化图谱，从103万篇论文中自动识别方法实体并推断其演化关系，每条边均基于原文证据标注。该图谱包含940万条语义类型边，捕捉方法演进中的因果关系与瓶颈。与专家标注的演化链对比显示高度一致性，配套的自引导时间树搜索算法可重建方法随时间的演进路径。实验表明该图谱可用于想法评估与自动化生成，为自动化科学发现提供基础数据层。
FlashRT: Towards Computationally and Memory Efficient Red-Teaming for Prompt Injection and Knowledge Corruption
Authors: Yanting Wang, Chenlong Yin, Ying Chen, Jinyuan Jia
First: 2026-04-30T17:43:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:43:24+00:00
Comments: The code is available at https://github.com/Wang-Yanting/FlashRT
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Long-context large language models (LLMs)-for example, Gemini-3.1-Pro and Qwen-3.5-are widely used to empower many real-world applications, such as retrieval-augmented generation, autonomous agents, and AI assistants. However, security remains a major concern for their widespread deployment, with threats such as prompt injection and knowledge corruption. To quantify the security risks faced by LLMs under these threats, the research community has developed heuristic-based and optimization-based red-teaming methods. Optimization-based methods generally produce stronger attacks than heuristic attacks and thus provide a more rigorous assessment of LLM security risks. However, they are often resource-intensive, requiring significant computation and GPU memory, especially for long context scenarios. The resource-intensive nature poses a major obstacle for the community (especially academic researchers) to systematically evaluate the security risks of long-context LLMs and assess the effectiveness of defense strategies at scale. In this work, we propose FlashRT, the first framework to improve the efficiency (in terms of both computation and memory) for optimization-based prompt injection and knowledge corruption attacks under long-context LLMs. Through extensive evaluations, we find that FlashRT consistently delivers a 2x-7x speedup (e.g., reducing runtime from one hour to less than ten minutes) and a 2x-4x reduction in GPU memory consumption (e.g., reducing from 264.1 GB to 65.7 GB GPU memory for a 32K token context) compared to state-of-the-art baseline nanoGCG. FlashRT can be broadly applied to black-box optimization methods, such as TAP and AutoDAN. We hope FlashRT can serve as a red-teaming tool to enable systematic evaluation of long-context LLM security. The code is available at: https://github.com/Wang-Yanting/FlashRT
中文标题/摘要
标题：FlashRT：面向提示注入与知识污染的计算与内存高效红队测试框架
长上下文大语言模型（如Gemini-3.1-Pro和Qwen-3.5）广泛应用于检索增强生成、自主代理和AI助手等现实场景。然而，其大规模部署面临安全风险，如提示注入和知识污染。为量化这些威胁下的安全风险，研究界开发了基于启发式和优化的红队测试方法。优化方法通常比启发式方法更强，能更严谨评估LLM安全风险，但往往资源密集，尤其在长上下文场景下需大量计算和GPU内存，这严重阻碍了学术界对长上下文LLM安全风险的系统评估及防御策略的规模化评估。本文提出FlashRT，这是首个针对长上下文LLM优化类提示注入与知识污染攻击的计算与内存效率提升框架。大量评估表明，FlashRT相比当前最优基线nanoGCG，可实现2至7倍的运行速度提升（如将运行时间从1小时缩短至10分钟内），以及2至4倍的GPU内存消耗降低（如32K上下文从264.1GB降至65.7GB）。FlashRT可广泛应用于黑盒优化方法，如TAP和AutoDAN。我们希望FlashRT能作为红队测试工具，助力长上下文LLM安全的系统性评估。代码开源于：https://github.com/Wang-Yanting/FlashRT
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently evaluate security risks in long-context LLMs like Gemini-3.1-Pro and Qwen-3.5 against prompt injection and knowledge corruption, the authors propose FlashRT, a framework that optimizes computation and memory usage for optimization-based red-teaming attacks. FlashRT achieves this by introducing memory-efficient techniques and dynamic computation scheduling, enabling faster attack generation and reduced GPU memory consumption. Experimental results show FlashRT delivers 2x–7x speedup and 2x–4x memory reduction compared to the state-of-the-art nanoGCG, making it broadly applicable to black-box methods like TAP and AutoDAN, thus enabling scalable security assessments for long-context models.
为高效评估长上下文大语言模型（如Gemini-3.1-Pro和Qwen-3.5）在提示注入与知识污染攻击下的安全风险，作者提出FlashRT框架，通过优化计算与内存使用，提升基于优化的红队攻击效率。该方法通过引入内存节省技术与算法改进，在运行时间上较当前最优方法nanoGCG提升2–7倍，GPU内存消耗降低2–4倍，从而支持大规模安全评估。FlashRT兼容TAP与AutoDAN等黑盒优化器，实验表明其在多种攻击场景与模型上下文下均表现有效。
FlexiTac: A Low-Cost, Open-Source, Scalable Tactile Sensing Solution for Robotic Systems
Authors: Binghao Huang, Yunzhu Li
First: 2026-04-30T17:43:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:43:07+00:00
Comments: Website: https://flexitac.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present FlexiTac, a low-cost, open-source, and scalable piezoresistive tactile sensing solution designed for robotic end-effectors. FlexiTac is a practical "plug-in" module consisting of (i) thin, flexible tactile sensor pads that provide dense tactile signals and (ii) a compact multi-channel readout board that streams synchronized measurements for real-time control and large-scale data collection. FlexiTac pads adopt a sealed three-layer laminate stack (FPC-Velostat-FPC) with electrode patterns directly integrated into flexible printed circuits, substantially improving fabrication throughput and repeatability while maintaining mechanical compliance for deployment on both rigid and soft grippers. The readout electronics use widely available, low-cost components and stream tactile signals to a host computer at 100 Hz via serial communication. Across multiple configurations, including fingertip pads and larger tactile mats, FlexiTac can be mounted on diverse platforms without major mechanical redesign. We further show that FlexiTac supports modern tactile learning pipelines, including 3D visuo-tactile fusion for contact-aware decision making, cross-embodiment skill transfer, and real-to-sim-to-real fine-tuning with GPU-parallel tactile simulation. Our project page is available at https://flexitac.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FlexiTac：一种低成本、开源、可扩展的机器人系统触觉传感解决方案
我们提出了FlexiTac，一种专为机器人末端执行器设计的低成本、开源且可扩展的压阻式触觉传感解决方案。FlexiTac是一个实用的“即插即用”模块，包含（i）提供密集触觉信号的薄柔性触觉传感垫，以及（ii）一个紧凑的多通道读取板，用于同步传输测量数据，以支持实时控制和大规模数据采集。FlexiTac传感垫采用密封三层层压结构（柔性电路板-维洛斯塔特-柔性电路板），电极图案直接集成于柔性印刷电路中，显著提升了制造效率和重复性，同时保持机械顺应性，适用于刚性和柔性夹爪。读取电子元件采用广泛可用且低成本的组件，通过串行通信以100Hz速率将触觉信号传输至主机计算机。在多种配置（包括指尖垫和更大尺寸触觉垫）下，FlexiTac可无需重大机械改造安装于多种平台。我们进一步表明，FlexiTac支持现代触觉学习流程，包括3D视觉-触觉融合以实现接触感知决策、跨具身技能迁移，以及结合GPU并行触觉模拟的实-仿-实微调。项目页面见https://flexitac.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for affordable, scalable, and easily deployable tactile sensing in robotics, FlexiTac introduces a low-cost, open-source piezoresistive tactile system composed of flexible sensor pads and a compact readout board. The sensor pads use a sealed three-layer laminate structure with integrated electrodes on flexible printed circuits, enabling high-density tactile sensing while maintaining mechanical compliance for use on both rigid and soft grippers. The readout board, built with common low-cost components, streams synchronized tactile data at 100 Hz via serial communication. Experimental results demonstrate FlexiTac’s versatility across multiple configurations and platforms, and its compatibility with modern tactile learning pipelines including 3D visuo-tactile fusion, cross-embodiment skill transfer, and GPU-accelerated simulation-based fine-tuning.
为满足机器人领域对低成本、可扩展且易于部署的触觉传感的需求，FlexiTac 提出了一种基于压阻效应的开源触觉传感系统，作为机器人末端执行器的插件模块。该系统由三层密封结构（柔性电路板-导电泡沫-柔性电路板）构成的柔性触觉传感垫与紧凑型多通道读取电路板组成，电极图案直接集成于柔性电路板上，兼顾高密度传感与机械柔顺性。读取电路板采用常见廉价元件，通过串口以100Hz频率传输同步触觉数据。实验表明，FlexiTac 可适配多种机器人平台，无需重大机械改造，并支持现代触觉学习流程，包括3D视觉-触觉融合、跨机体技能迁移及基于GPU并行模拟的现实-仿真-现实微调，验证了其在实时控制与大规模数据采集中的有效性。
Explainable Load Forecasting with Covariate-Informed Time Series Foundation Models
Authors: Matthias Hertel, Alexandra Nikoltchovska, Sebastian Pütz, Ralf Mikut, Benjamin Schäfer, Veit Hagenmeyer
First: 2026-04-30T17:36:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:36:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) have recently emerged as general-purpose forecasting models and show considerable potential for applications in energy systems. However, applications in critical infrastructure like power grids require transparency to ensure trust and reliability and cannot rely on pure black-box models. To enhance the transparency of TSFMs, we propose an efficient algorithm for computing Shapley Additive Explanations (SHAP) tailored to these models. The proposed approach leverages the flexibility of TSFMs with respect to input context length and provided covariates. This property enables efficient temporal and covariate masking (selectively withholding inputs), allowing for a scalable explanation of model predictions using SHAP. We evaluate two TSFMs - Chronos-2 and TabPFN-TS - on a day-ahead load forecasting task for a transmission system operator (TSO). In a zero-shot setting, both models achieve predictive performance competitive with a Transformer model trained specifically on multiple years of TSO data. The explanations obtained through our proposed approach align with established domain knowledge, particularly as the TSFMs appropriately use weather and calendar information for load prediction. Overall, we demonstrate that TSFMs can serve as transparent and reliable tools for operational energy forecasting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于协变量信息的时间序列基础模型的可解释负荷预测
时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）近期作为通用预测模型崭露头角，在能源系统应用中展现出巨大潜力。然而，应用于电力等关键基础设施时，需确保透明度以建立信任和可靠性，不能依赖纯黑箱模型。为增强TSFMs的透明性，我们提出一种针对这些模型的高效Shapley加性解释（SHAP）计算算法。该方法利用TSFMs在输入上下文长度和协变量方面的灵活性，通过时间与协变量掩码（选择性隐藏输入），实现基于SHAP的可扩展模型预测解释。我们在输电系统运营商（TSO）的日前负荷预测任务中评估了两种TSFMs——Chronos-2和TabPFN-TS。在零样本设置下，二者预测性能与专门在多年TSO数据上训练的Transformer模型相当。通过本方法获得的解释与领域知识一致，尤其体现TSFMs恰当地利用天气和日历信息进行负荷预测。总体而言，我们证明TSFMs可作为透明、可靠的运营能源预测工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for trustworthy forecasting in critical energy infrastructure, this work addresses the lack of transparency in Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) by proposing an efficient SHAP-based explanation method that exploits their flexible input handling. The method enables scalable, covariate-aware explanations through temporal and covariate masking, allowing attribution of model predictions to specific inputs. Evaluated on day-ahead load forecasting for a transmission system operator, two TSFMs—Chronos-2 and TabPFN-TS—achieved performance competitive with a domain-specific Transformer, while their explanations aligned with domain knowledge, demonstrating that TSFMs can be both accurate and interpretable for operational use.

Sample-efficient evidence estimation of score based priors for model selection
Authors: Frederic Wang, Katherine L. Bouman
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-02-24T05:06:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:36:17+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The choice of prior is central to solving ill-posed imaging inverse problems, making it essential to select one consistent with the measurements $y$ to avoid severe bias. In Bayesian inverse problems, this could be achieved by evaluating the model evidence $p(y \mid M)$ under different models $M$ that specify the prior and then selecting the one with the highest value. Diffusion models are the state-of-the-art approach to solving inverse problems with a data-driven prior; however, directly computing the model evidence with respect to a diffusion prior is intractable. Furthermore, most existing model evidence estimators require either many pointwise evaluations of the unnormalized prior density or an accurate clean prior score. We propose DiME, an estimator of the model evidence of a diffusion prior by integrating over the time-marginals of posterior sampling methods. Our method leverages the large amount of intermediate samples naturally obtained during the reverse diffusion sampling process to obtain an accurate estimation of the model evidence using only a handful of posterior samples (e.g., 20). We also demonstrate how to implement our estimator in tandem with recent diffusion posterior sampling methods. Empirically, our estimator matches the model evidence when it can be computed analytically, and it is able to both select the correct diffusion model prior and diagnose prior misfit under different highly ill-conditioned, non-linear inverse problems, including a real-world black hole imaging problem.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于分数先验的模型选择中样本高效的证据估计
先验的选择对于解决病态成像逆问题至关重要，必须选择与测量值 y 一致的先验以避免严重偏差。在贝叶斯逆问题中，可通过在不同模型 M（指定先验）下评估模型证据 p(y|M) 并选择值最大的模型实现。扩散模型是当前数据驱动先验解决逆问题的最先进方法；然而，直接计算扩散先验的模型证据是不可行的。此外，大多数现有模型证据估计器需要大量未归一化先验密度的点评估或精确的干净先验分数。我们提出 DiME，一种通过后验采样方法的时间边缘分布积分来估计扩散先验模型证据的方法。该方法利用反向扩散采样过程中自然获得的大量中间样本，仅需少量后验样本（如 20 个）即可准确估计模型证据。我们还展示了如何将该估计器与近期扩散后验采样方法结合使用。实证表明，当模型证据可解析计算时，我们的估计器能匹配其值，并能在不同高度病态的非线性逆问题（包括真实黑洞成像问题）中正确选择扩散模型先验并诊断先验拟合不良。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to choose priors consistent with measurements in ill-posed imaging inverse problems, this work proposes DiME, a sample-efficient estimator for model evidence under diffusion priors. DiME leverages intermediate samples naturally generated during reverse diffusion sampling to approximate the evidence without requiring many pointwise prior evaluations or accurate clean scores. Experiments show DiME accurately estimates evidence when analytically computable, correctly selects diffusion priors, and diagnoses prior misfit in challenging non-linear inverse problems, including real-world black hole imaging.
为了解决逆问题中选择与测量一致的先验分布以避免偏差的问题，本文提出DiME方法，一种基于扩散先验的模型证据高效估计器。该方法利用反向扩散采样过程中自然生成的中间样本，无需大量先验点评估或精确干净分数即可近似模型证据。实验表明，DiME在可解析计算时能准确估计证据，能正确选择扩散先验，并在多个高度病态非线性逆问题（包括真实黑洞成像）中诊断先验拟合不足。
Design and Characteristics of a Thin-Film ThermoMesh for the Efficient Embedded Sensing of a Spatio-Temporally Sparse Heat Source
Authors: Sajjad Boorghan Farahan, Ahmed Alajlouni, Jingzhou Zhao
First: 2026-04-30T17:35:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:35:40+00:00
Comments: 45 pages, 13 figures, 63 references, under review in Sensors and Actuators A: Physical
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work presents ThermoMesh, a passive thin-film thermoelectric mesh sensor designed to detect and characterize spatio-temporally sparse heat sources through conduction-based thermal imaging. The device integrates thermoelectric junctions with linear or nonlinear interlayer resistive elements to perform simultaneous sensing and in-sensor compression. We focus on the single-event (1-sparse) operation and define four performance metrics: range, efficiency, sensitivity, and accuracy. Numerical modeling shows that a linear resistive interlayer flattens the sensitivity distribution and improves minimum sensitivity by approximately tenfold for a $16\times16$ mesh. Nonlinear temperature-dependent interlayers further enhance minimum sensitivity at scale: a ceramic negative-temperature-coefficient (NTC) layer over 973--1273~K yields a $\sim14{,}500\times$ higher minimum sensitivity than the linear design at a $200\times200$ mesh, while a VO$_2$ interlayer modeled across its metal--insulator transition (MIT) over 298--373~K yields a $\sim24\times$ improvement. Using synthetic 1-sparse datasets with white boundary-channel noise at a signal-to-noise ratio of 40~dB, the VO$_2$ case achieved $98\%$ localization accuracy, a mean absolute temperature error of $0.23$~K, and a noise-equivalent temperature (NET) of $0.07$~K. For the ceramic-NTC case no localization errors were observed under the tested conditions, with a mean absolute temperature error of $1.83$~K and a NET of $1.49$~K. These results indicate that ThermoMesh could enable energy-efficient embedded thermal sensing in scenarios where conventional infrared imaging is limited, such as molten-droplet detection or hot-spot monitoring in harsh environments.

标题：用于高效嵌入式检测时空稀疏热源的薄膜热网传感器的设计与特性
本文提出ThermoMesh，一种被动式薄膜热电网格传感器，通过基于传导的热成像检测和表征时空稀疏热源。该器件将热电结与线性或非线性夹层电阻元件集成，实现同时传感与传感器内压缩。我们聚焦单事件（1-稀疏）操作，并定义四项性能指标：探测范围、效率、灵敏度和精度。数值建模表明，线性电阻夹层可平坦灵敏度分布，使16×16网格的最小灵敏度提升约十倍。非线性温度依赖夹层进一步提升大规模下的最小灵敏度：在200×200网格中，陶瓷负温度系数（NTC）层（973–1273 K）较线性设计最小灵敏度提升约14,500倍；VO₂夹层在金属-绝缘体转变区（298–373 K）建模，灵敏度提升约24倍。在信噪比40 dB、含白噪声边界通道的合成1-稀疏数据集上，VO₂方案实现98%定位精度、平均绝对温度误差0.23 K、噪声等效温度（NET）0.07 K；陶瓷-NTC方案在测试条件下无定位误差，平均绝对温度误差1.83 K、NET 1.49 K。结果表明，ThermoMesh可实现传统红外成像受限场景（如熔滴检测或恶劣环境热点监测）中的节能嵌入式热传感。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for energy-efficient, embedded thermal sensing in environments where conventional infrared imaging is impractical, this work introduces ThermoMesh, a passive thin-film thermoelectric mesh sensor that detects spatio-temporally sparse heat sources via conduction-based thermal imaging. The sensor integrates thermoelectric junctions with either linear or nonlinear resistive interlayers to enable simultaneous sensing and in-sensor compression. Numerical simulations show that linear interlayers improve sensitivity by ~10x for a 16x16 mesh, while nonlinear interlayers—such as ceramic NTC or VO₂ near its metal-insulator transition—deliver dramatic sensitivity gains: ~14,500x for ceramic NTC and ~24x for VO₂ at larger scales.
