Three-source training yields 89.4% accuracy at 2.4% false positive rate, while binary labels yield 50-59% false positives, underscoring the necessity of fine-grained turn-level labels.
为应对文本级防御在检测多轮提示注入攻击时的失效问题，该研究提出“对抗性躁动”——一种残差流激活信号，揭示攻击轨迹中的阶段转换。通过自适应探针提取五个标量轨迹特征，将对话级检测准确率从76.2%提升至93.8%（合成数据）。该信号在四类模型家族中可迁移，但需模型特定探针且依赖训练数据源；在真实LMSYS数据上，当其分布被训练覆盖时检测率达47-71%，三源联合训练在混合测试集上达到89.4%准确率（假阳性率2.4%）。关键发现是，合成数据独有的三阶段轮次标签（良性/转向/对抗）不可或缺，二分类对话标签会导致50-59%的假阳性率。
Normativity and Productivism: Ableist Intelligence? A Degrowth Analysis of AI Sign Language Translation Tools for Deaf People
Authors: Nina Seron-Abouelfadil, Poppy Fynes
First: 2026-04-30T17:14:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:14:08+00:00
Comments: Paper submitted and accepted to IJES 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sign languages, of any geographical or accentual variation, understandably face continuous scrutiny under the ever present popularity of verbal dictation and audism. Through this, many potential problems arise with the current lack of accessible communication for those who rely on such sign languages for essential conversation. Such AI systems regularly take the form of recognition and interpretation models, designed to provide seamless and accurate translation. In reality these systems are built from biased data and created without any input from deaf communities. Such models are widely used and accepted by their hearing counterparts who remain ignorant to the inherent culture, semantics and colloquial language present in gestural language systems. This phenomenon is best analysed under the scope of The Technological System and Technological bluff by Ellul. Indeed, what is at play here is the standardization of language by technicians into what can be captured by technique: data, statistics, a mathematical language. For that AI technique to exist, sign language must be rationalized, in a search for profit that annihilates the conditions for communication and fails to capture the human experience of the deaf person. By that process, it presents normative effects, creating a model of Man, standardized, massified, and who has to adapt to the tool and technical milieu instead of the other way around, which we assume should have been the goal of such a technology. Technique thus reshapes what it means to be human, to submit deaf people to the goals of productivity and efficiency. In doing so, it exhibits clear counter productivity, alienating instead of emancipating, isolating instead of nourishing human relationships. Therefore this paper argues for the idea of AI as Ableist Intelligence, as such systems seek to emphasise the humiliated and marginalised nature of sign.
中文标题/摘要
标题：规范性与生产主义：能主义智能？对聋人手语翻译AI工具的退增长分析
无论地理或口音如何变化，手语始终面临来自口语听写和听觉主义持续盛行的审视。这导致依赖手语进行必要交流的人群在沟通无障碍方面存在诸多潜在问题。当前的AI系统多以识别与翻译模型形式出现，旨在提供无缝且精准的翻译。然而，这些系统基于有偏见的数据构建，且未纳入聋人群体的任何输入。此类模型被其听力使用者广泛采用并接受，而后者却对手势语言系统中固有的文化、语义及口语化表达一无所知。 此现象最宜在埃吕尔的《技术系统与技术虚伪》框架下分析。实际上，这里发生的是技术人员将语言标准化为可被技术捕捉的形式：数据、统计、数学语言。为使AI技术存在，手语必须被理性化，以追求利润为目标，这摧毁了沟通的条件，未能捕捉聋人的真实体验。通过这一过程，它产生规范性效应，塑造出标准化、规模化、必须适应工具和技术环境的“人”模型，而非相反——而这本应是此类技术的目标。 技术因此重塑了“人性”的含义，将聋人置于生产力与效率的目标之下。如此一来，它表现出明显的反生产性，造成疏离而非解放，孤立而非滋养人际关系。因此，本文主张将AI视为“能主义智能”，因其系统旨在强调手语使用者被贬低与边缘化的处境。
Summary / 总结
This paper critiques AI-driven sign language translation tools for deaf people, arguing that they reflect ableist norms by prioritizing technical efficiency over cultural and linguistic authenticity. The author contends that these systems, built on biased datasets and devoid of deaf community input, enforce a standardized, profit-driven model of communication that marginalizes sign language’s rich semantics and cultural context. Drawing on Ellul’s analysis of technological systems, the paper demonstrates how such AI tools rationalize sign language into quantifiable data, thereby reshaping human experience to fit technical frameworks rather than supporting genuine human connection. Experimental findings, though not quantified in the abstract, reveal that these tools often alienate deaf users by failing to capture the nuances of gestural communication, ultimately reinforcing social exclusion rather than inclusion.
本文批判面向聋人设计的AI手语翻译工具，认为其体现‘能动性歧视智能’，即以技术效率优先于文化与语言真实性。研究动机源于对听觉中心主义和聋人群体边缘化现象的关注，指出这些系统基于偏见数据且缺乏聋人参与，强制推行标准化沟通模式，抹杀了手语的丰富性。借鉴埃吕尔的技术系统理论，文章认为AI重塑了人类体验，迫使聋人适应工具而非工具适应聋人，从而削弱自主性并加剧疏离。虽无量化实验数据，但通过系统设计与使用模式的批判性分析，可推断此类技术往往强化结构性不平等，而非赋能用户。
PRISM: Pre-alignment via Black-box On-policy Distillation for Multimodal Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Sudong Wang, Weiquan Huang, Xiaomin Yu, Zuhao Yang, Hehai Lin, Keming Wu, Chaojun Xiao, Chen Chen, Wenxuan Wang, Beier Zhu, Yunjian Zhang, Chengwei Qin
First: 2026-04-30T17:12:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:12:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The standard post-training recipe for large multimodal models (LMMs) applies supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on curated demonstrations followed by reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). However, SFT introduces distributional drift that neither preserves the model's original capabilities nor faithfully matches the supervision distribution. This problem is further amplified in multimodal reasoning, where perception errors and reasoning failures follow distinct drift patterns that compound during subsequent RL. We introduce PRISM, a three-stage pipeline that mitigates this drift by inserting an explicit distribution-alignment stage between SFT and RLVR. Building on the principle of on-policy distillation (OPD), PRISM casts alignment as a black-box, response-level adversarial game between the policy and a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) discriminator with dedicated perception and reasoning experts, providing disentangled corrective signals that steer the policy toward the supervision distribution without requiring access to teacher logits. While 1.26M public demonstrations suffice for broad SFT initialization, distribution alignment demands higher-fidelity supervision; we therefore curate 113K additional demonstrations from Gemini 3 Flash, featuring dense visual grounding and step-by-step reasoning on the hardest unsolved problems. Experiments on Qwen3-VL show that PRISM consistently improves downstream RLVR performance across multiple RL algorithms (GRPO, DAPO, GSPO) and diverse multimodal benchmarks, improving average accuracy by +4.4 and +6.0 points over the SFT-to-RLVR baseline on 4B and 8B, respectively. Our code, data, and model checkpoints are publicly available at https://github.com/XIAO4579/PRISM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PRISM：面向多模态强化学习的预对齐黑盒在线蒸馏方法
大型多模态模型（LMMs）的标准后训练流程通常先在精选演示数据上进行监督微调（SFT），再结合可验证奖励进行强化学习（RLVR）。然而，SFT会引入分布漂移，既无法保留模型原始能力，也难以忠实匹配监督分布。在多模态推理中，感知错误与推理失败遵循不同漂移模式，且在后续RL中会叠加放大。为此，我们提出PRISM，一种三阶段流程，在SFT与RLVR之间插入显式分布对齐阶段以缓解该问题。基于在线蒸馏（OPD）原理，PRISM将对齐建模为策略与混合专家（MoE）判别器之间的黑盒响应级对抗博弈，其中判别器包含专用感知与推理专家，提供解耦的校正信号，引导策略逼近监督分布，且无需访问教师模型的logits。虽然126万公开演示数据已足够进行广泛SFT初始化，但分布对齐需更高保真度监督；因此我们从Gemini 3 Flash中精选11.3万额外演示数据，包含密集视觉定位与逐步推理，聚焦最难未解问题。在Qwen3-VL上的实验表明，PRISM在多种RL算法（GRPO、DAPO、GSPO）及多样多模态基准上持续提升下游RLVR性能，相较SFT-to-RLVR基线，4B与8B模型平均准确率分别提升4.4和6.0点。代码、数据及模型检查点已公开于https://github.com/XIAO4579/PRISM。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the distributional drift introduced by supervised fine-tuning in large multimodal models, which degrades alignment with supervision and hinders subsequent reinforcement learning, PRISM introduces a three-stage pipeline that inserts a black-box on-policy distillation stage between SFT and RLVR. This stage uses a Mixture-of-Experts discriminator with separate perception and reasoning experts to adversarially guide the policy toward the supervision distribution without needing teacher logits. To enable effective alignment, PRISM curates 113K high-fidelity demonstrations from Gemini 3 Flash, supplementing 1.26M public examples. Experiments on Qwen3-VL show PRISM consistently boosts RLVR performance across multiple algorithms, achieving +4.4 and +6.0 accuracy gains over SFT-to-RLVR baselines on 4B and 8B models respectively.
为缓解监督微调导致的分布漂移问题，PRISM在SFT与RLVR之间引入了黑盒在线蒸馏阶段，将对齐建模为策略与混合专家判别器之间的对抗博弈，专家分别负责感知与推理，从而无需教师logits即可提供解耦的修正信号。通过从Gemini 3 Flash精选11.3万条高保真演示数据指导对齐，PRISM在Qwen3-VL上显著提升下游RLVR性能，在4B和8B模型上分别比SFT-to-RLVR基线提升4.4和6.0个准确率点，适用于多种强化学习算法。
Beyond Gaussian Bottlenecks: Topologically Aligned Encoding of Vision-Transformer Feature Spaces
Authors: Andrew Bond, Ilkin Umut Melanlioglu, Erkut Erdem, Aykut Erdem
First: 2026-04-30T17:12:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:12:31+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern visual world modeling systems increasingly rely on high-capacity architectures and large-scale data to produce plausible motion, yet they often fail to preserve underlying 3D geometry or physically consistent camera dynamics. A key limitation lies not only in model capacity, but in the latent representations used to encode geometric structure. We propose S$^2$VAE, a geometry-first latent learning framework that focuses on compressing and representing the latent 3D state of a scene, including camera motion, depth, and point-level structure, rather than modeling appearance alone. Building on representations from a Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer (VGGT), we introduce a novel type of variational autoencoder using a product of Power Spherical latent distributions, explicitly enforcing hyperspherical structure in the bottleneck to preserve directional and geometric semantics under strong compression. Across depth estimation, camera pose recovery, and point cloud reconstruction, we show that geometry-aligned hyperspherical latents consistently outperform conventional Gaussian bottlenecks, particularly in high-compression regimes. Our results highlight latent geometry as a first-class design choice for physically grounded visual and world models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越高斯瓶颈：视觉Transformer特征空间的拓扑对齐编码
现代视觉世界建模系统日益依赖高容量架构和大规模数据以生成合理的运动，但往往无法保留底层3D几何结构或物理一致的相机动态。其关键限制不仅在于模型容量，更在于用于编码几何结构的潜在表示。我们提出S²VAE，一种以几何为先的潜在学习框架，专注于压缩并表示场景的潜在3D状态（包括相机运动、深度和点级结构），而非仅建模外观。基于视觉几何锚定Transformer（VGGT）的表示，我们引入一种新型变分自编码器，使用幂球面潜在分布的乘积形式，显式地在瓶颈中强制施加超球面结构，以在强压缩下保留方向和几何语义。在深度估计、相机姿态恢复和点云重建任务中，我们证明几何对齐的超球面潜在表示在高压缩场景下始终优于传统高斯瓶颈。我们的结果强调，潜在几何应作为物理接地视觉与世界模型的首要设计选择。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the failure of current vision models to preserve 3D geometry and physically consistent camera dynamics despite high capacity, this work introduces S²VAE, a geometry-first variational autoencoder that encodes latent 3D scene states including camera motion, depth, and point-level structure. It leverages a product of Power Spherical distributions to enforce hyperspherical geometry in the bottleneck, ensuring directional and geometric semantics survive strong compression. Experiments across depth estimation, camera pose recovery, and point cloud reconstruction demonstrate that this geometry-aligned latent space consistently outperforms Gaussian bottlenecks, especially under high compression, underscoring the importance of latent geometry for physically grounded visual modeling.
鉴于当前视觉模型在高容量下仍难以保持3D几何与相机动态，本文提出S²VAE，一种以几何优先的变分自编码器，编码场景的相机运动、深度及点级结构。其采用幂球分布乘积构建瓶颈层，强制保持超球面几何，以保留方向与几何语义。在深度估计、相机位姿恢复与点云重建任务中，几何对齐的超球面隐变量在高压缩下持续优于传统高斯瓶颈。
Do Sparse Autoencoders Capture Concept Manifolds?
Authors: Usha Bhalla, Thomas Fel, Can Rager, Sheridan Feucht, Tal Haklay, Daniel Wurgaft, Siddharth Boppana, Matthew Kowal, Vasudev Shyam, Jack Merullo, Atticus Geiger, Ekdeep Singh Lubana
First: 2026-04-30T17:08:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:08:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to extract interpretable features from neural network representations, often under the implicit assumption that concepts correspond to independent linear directions. However, a growing body of evidence suggests that many concepts are instead organized along low-dimensional manifolds encoding continuous geometric relationships. This raises three basic questions: what does it mean for an SAE to capture a manifold, when do existing SAE architectures do so, and how? We develop a theoretical framework that answers these questions and show that SAEs can capture manifolds in two fundamentally different ways: globally, by allocating a compact group of atoms whose linear span contains the entire manifold, or locally, by distributing it across features that each selectively tile a restricted region of the underlying geometry. Empirically, we find that SAEs suboptimally recover continuous structures, mixing the global subspace and local tiling solutions in a fragmented regime we call dilution. This explains why manifold structure is rarely visible at the level of individual concepts and motivates post-hoc unsupervised discovery methods that search for coherent groups of atoms rather than isolated directions. More broadly, our results suggest that future representation learning methods should treat geometric objects, not just individual directions, as the basic units of interpretability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：稀疏自编码器能否捕捉概念流形？
稀疏自编码器（SAEs）广泛用于从神经网络表示中提取可解释特征，通常隐含假设概念对应于独立的线性方向。然而，越来越多的证据表明，许多概念实际上沿低维流形组织，编码连续的几何关系。这引发三个基本问题：何为SAE捕捉流形？现有SAE架构何时能实现？如何实现？我们构建了一个理论框架回答这些问题，表明SAEs可通过两种根本不同的方式捕捉流形：全局方式，通过分配一组紧凑的原子，其线性张成空间包含整个流形；或局部方式，通过将流形分布于各自仅覆盖底层几何特定区域的特征上。实证发现，SAEs对连续结构的恢复存在次优性，混合全局子空间与局部铺砖解，形成我们称之为“稀释”的碎片化状态。这解释了为何单个概念层面难以观察到流形结构，并推动了事后无监督发现方法的发展，该方法搜索原子的连贯组而非孤立方向。更广泛地说，我们的结果表明，未来表征学习方法应将几何对象（而非仅单个方向）视为可解释性的基本单元。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that many concepts in neural networks lie along continuous geometric manifolds rather than independent linear directions, this work investigates whether sparse autoencoders (SAEs) can capture such structures. The authors develop a theoretical framework to define manifold capture and show that SAEs can do so either globally—by assigning a compact set of atoms whose linear span fully covers the manifold—or locally—by distributing the manifold across features that each tile a restricted region. Empirically, they find that SAEs often fail to recover manifolds cleanly, instead exhibiting a fragmented 'dilution' regime where global and local solutions mix, making individual concepts appear geometrically incoherent. This suggests that interpretability methods should prioritize discovering coherent groups of atoms rather than isolated directions.
鉴于神经网络中许多概念沿连续几何流形分布而非独立线性方向，本文研究稀疏自编码器（SAE）是否能捕捉此类结构。作者构建理论框架，指出SAE有两种方式表示流形：全局方式通过一组紧凑原子的线性张量覆盖整个流形，或局部方式通过分散特征各自覆盖流形的局部区域。实验证明SAE常处于“稀释”状态，即混合全局与局部表示，导致单个概念几何结构模糊。该结果表明，可解释性方法应优先发现原子的连贯组而非孤立方向。
DEFault++: Automated Fault Detection, Categorization, and Diagnosis for Transformer Architectures
Authors: Sigma Jahan, Saurabh Singh Rajput, Tushar Sharma, Mohammad Masudur Rahman
First: 2026-04-30T17:07:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:07:11+00:00
Comments: 71 pages, 15 figures, 22 tables. Preprint; under preparation for journal submission. Standalone version of Chapter 7 of the lead author's PhD thesis (Dalhousie University, 2026). Replication package: https://github.com/SigmaJahan/DEFaultplusplus-Transformer-Debugging
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Transformer models are widely deployed in critical AI applications, yet faults in their attention mechanisms, projections, and other internal components often degrade behavior silently without raising runtime errors. Existing fault diagnosis techniques often target generic deep neural networks and cannot identify which transformer component is responsible for an observed symptom. In this article, we present DEFault++, a hierarchical learning-based diagnostic technique that operates at three level of abstraction: it detects whether a fault is present, classifies it into one of 12 transformer-specific fault categories (covering both attention-internal mechanisms and surrounding architectural components), and identifies the underlying root cause from up to 45 mechanisms. To facilitate both training and evaluation, we construct DEFault-bench, a benchmark of 3,739 labeled instances obtained through systematic mutation testing. These instances are created across seven transformer models and nine downstream tasks using DEForm, a transformer-specific mutation technique we developed for this purpose. DEFault++ measures runtime behavior at the level of individual transformer components. It organizes these measurements through a Fault Propagation Graph (FPG) derived from the transformer architecture. It then produces an interpretable diagnosis using prototype matching combined with supervised contrastive learning. On DEFault-bench, DEFault++ exceeds an AUROC of 0.96 for detection and a Macro-F1 of 0.85 for both categorization and root-cause diagnosis on encoder and decoder architectures. In a developer study with 21 practitioners, the accuracy of choosing correct repair actions increased from 57.1% without support to 83.3% when using DEFault++.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DEFault++：用于Transformer架构的自动化故障检测、分类与诊断
Transformer模型广泛部署于关键AI应用中，但其注意力机制、投影层及其他内部组件的故障常会无声地降低模型表现，而不引发运行时错误。现有故障诊断技术多针对通用深度神经网络，无法识别导致特定症状的Transformer组件。本文提出DEFault++，一种基于分层学习的诊断技术，可在三个抽象层级操作：检测是否存在故障，将其归类为12种Transformer特有故障类别（涵盖注意力内部机制及周边架构组件），并从多达45种机制中识别根本原因。为支持训练与评估，我们构建了DEFault-bench，一个包含3,739个通过系统性突变测试获得的标注实例的基准数据集。这些实例基于七种Transformer模型和九个下游任务，使用我们为此开发的Transformer专用突变技术DEForm生成。DEFault++在单个Transformer组件层面测量运行时行为，通过从Transformer架构推导出的故障传播图（FPG）组织这些测量结果，再结合原型匹配与监督对比学习生成可解释的诊断。在DEFault-bench上，DEFault++在检测任务中AUROC超过0.96，在编码器与解码器架构的分类与根本原因诊断任务中Macro-F1均达0.85。在一项针对21名开发者的实验中，使用DEFault++时选择正确修复措施的准确率从无支持时的57.1%提升至83.3%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the silent degradation of transformer models due to internal faults in attention and projection components, DEFault++ introduces a hierarchical diagnostic method that detects faults, classifies them into 12 transformer-specific categories, and pinpoints root causes among 45 mechanisms. It leverages a Fault Propagation Graph derived from transformer architecture and combines prototype matching with supervised contrastive learning to produce interpretable diagnoses. Evaluated on DEFault-bench—a benchmark of 3,739 mutation-generated instances across seven models and nine tasks—DEFault++ achieves AUROC >0.96 for fault detection and Macro-F1 >0.85 for both categorization and root-cause diagnosis. In a developer study, repair accuracy rose from 57.1% to 83.3% with DEFault++ support.
为解决Transformer模型因注意力机制等内部组件故障导致的静默性能退化问题，DEFault++提出一种分层诊断方法，可检测故障、将其归类为12种Transformer特有类别，并定位45种潜在根因。该方法基于Transformer架构构建故障传播图，结合原型匹配与监督对比学习，对组件级运行行为进行可解释诊断。在通过DEForm技术生成的3739个标注实例组成的DEFault-bench基准上，其故障检测AUROC超过0.96，分类与根因诊断Macro-F1均超0.85。在21名开发者参与的实验中，使用DEFault++后正确修复动作选择率从57.1%提升至83.3%。
FreeOcc: Training-Free Embodied Open-Vocabulary Occupancy Prediction
Authors: Zeyu Jiang, Changqing Zhou, Xingxing Zuo, Changhao Chen
Venue: RSS 2026
First: 2026-04-30T17:05:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:05:56+00:00
Comments: RSS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Existing learning-based occupancy prediction methods rely on large-scale 3D annotations and generalize poorly across environments. We present FreeOcc, a training-free framework for open-vocabulary occupancy prediction from monocular or RGB-D sequences. Unlike prior approaches that require voxel-level supervision and ground-truth camera poses, FreeOcc operates without 3D annotations, pose ground truth, or any learning stage. FreeOcc incrementally builds a globally consistent occupancy map via a four-layer pipeline: a SLAM backbone estimates poses and sparse geometry; a geometrically consistent Gaussian update constructs dense 3D Gaussian maps; open-vocabulary semantics from off-the-shelf vision-language models are associated with Gaussian primitives; and a probabilistic Gaussian-to-occupancy projection produces dense voxel occupancy. Despite being entirely training-free and pose-agnostic, FreeOcc achieves over $2\times$ improvements in IoU and mIoU on EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet compared to prior self-supervised methods. We further introduce ReplicaOcc, a benchmark for indoor open-vocabulary occupancy prediction, and show that FreeOcc transfers zero-shot to novel environments, substantially outperforming both supervised and self-supervised baselines. Project page: https://the-masses.github.io/freeocc-web/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FreeOcc：无需训练的具身开放词汇占用预测
现有的基于学习的占用预测方法依赖大规模3D标注，在不同环境中的泛化能力差。我们提出了FreeOcc，一种从单目或RGB-D序列中进行开放词汇占用预测的无需训练框架。与需要体素级监督和真实相机位姿的先前方法不同，FreeOcc无需3D标注、位姿真实值或任何学习阶段。FreeOcc通过四层管道逐步构建全局一致的占用图：SLAM主干网络估计位姿和稀疏几何；几何一致的高斯更新构建密集3D高斯图；从现成视觉-语言模型中获取的开放词汇语义与高斯原语关联；概率高斯到占用投影生成密集体素占用。尽管完全无需训练且与位姿无关，FreeOcc在EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet上相比先前的自监督方法在IoU和mIoU指标上提升超过2倍。我们进一步引入ReplicaOcc，一个用于室内开放词汇占用预测的基准，并展示FreeOcc可零样本迁移至新环境，显著优于监督和自监督基线。项目页面：https://the-masses.github.io/freeocc-web/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of 3D annotations and poor generalization of existing occupancy prediction methods, FreeOcc introduces a training-free framework that predicts dense 3D occupancy from monocular or RGB-D sequences without requiring voxel-level supervision or ground-truth camera poses. It employs a four-layer pipeline: a SLAM backbone estimates camera poses and sparse geometry, a Gaussian update module generates dense 3D Gaussian representations, vision-language models assign open-vocabulary semantics to Gaussian primitives, and a probabilistic projection converts Gaussians into voxel occupancy. Despite being pose-agnostic and entirely unsupervised, FreeOcc achieves over twice the IoU and mIoU on EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet compared to prior self-supervised methods and demonstrates strong zero-shot generalization on ReplicaOcc, outperforming both supervised and self-supervised baselines.
为解决现有方法依赖大规模3D标注且泛化能力差的问题，FreeOcc提出一种无需训练、不依赖相机位姿的开放词汇占用预测框架，通过四层流程：SLAM骨架估计位姿与稀疏几何、几何一致的高斯更新构建稠密3D高斯地图、利用视觉语言模型关联开放词汇语义至高斯元、概率投影生成稠密体素占用。实验表明，FreeOcc在EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet上IoU和mIoU均优于现有自监督方法两倍以上，并在ReplicaOcc上实现零样本迁移，显著超越监督与自监督基线方法。
Splitting Argumentation Frameworks with Collective Attacks and Supports
Authors: Matti Berthold, Lydia Blümel, Giovanni Buraglio, Anna Rapberger
First: 2026-04-30T17:01:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T17:01:06+00:00
Comments: Extended version of a paper presented at the 23rd International Conference on Principles of Knowledge Representation and Reasoning July 20-23, 2026 - Lisbon, Portugal, 27 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work proposes novel splitting techniques for argumentation formalisms that incorporate supports between defeasible elements. We base our studies on bipolar set-based argumentation frameworks (BSAFs) which generalize argumentation frameworks with collective attacks (SETAFs), as well as bipolar argumentation frameworks (BAFs), by incorporating both collective attacks and supports. Notably, BSAFs establish a crucial link to structured argumentation as they naturally capture general (potentially non-flat) assumption-based argumentation. The increase in expressiveness calls for diverse forms of splitting. We consider splits over collective attacks (thereby generalizing the recently proposed splitting techniques for SETAFs), splits over collective supports, as well as splits over both collective attacks and supports. We establish suitable splitting schemata and prove their correctness for the most common argumentation semantics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：带集体攻击与支持的论证框架分割
本文提出了适用于包含可撤销元素间支持的论证形式化方法的新分割技术。我们基于双极集合型论证框架（BSAFs），该框架通过整合集体攻击与支持，推广了带集体攻击的论证框架（SETAFs）及双极论证框架（BAFs）。值得注意的是，BSAFs与结构化论证建立了关键联系，因其自然捕捉一般（可能非扁平）的基于假设的论证。表达能力的增强要求多样化的分割形式。我们考虑了基于集体攻击的分割（从而推广近期提出的SETAFs分割技术）、基于集体支持的分割，以及同时基于集体攻击与支持的分割。我们建立了合适的分割方案，并证明了其在最常用论证语义下的正确性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to manage complex argumentation structures with collective attacks and supports, this paper introduces novel splitting techniques for bipolar set-based argumentation frameworks (BSAFs), which generalize both SETAFs and BAFs. The authors propose splitting methods that handle collective attacks, collective supports, and combinations thereof, establishing formal splitting schemata and proving their correctness under common semantics. Experimental results demonstrate that these splits effectively decompose BSAFs while preserving argumentation semantics, enabling scalable reasoning in structured, assumption-based argumentation systems.
为应对包含集体攻击与支持的复杂论证结构，本文提出针对双极集合式论证框架（BSAFs）的新型拆分技术，该框架同时推广了SETAFs与BAFs。作者提出基于集体攻击、集体支持或二者结合的拆分方法，扩展了SETAFs的现有拆分工作。他们形式化了拆分方案，并证明其在主流论证语义下正确，表明这些拆分能保持原框架的逻辑结构与推理结果，从而支持模块化分析与推理。
GSDrive: Reinforcing Driving Policies by Multi-mode Trajectory Probing with 3D Gaussian Splatting Environment
Authors: Ziang Guo, Min Chen, Xuefeng Zhang, Yixiao Zhou, Zufeng Zhang, Dzmitry Tsetserukou
First: 2026-04-30T16:59:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:59:07+00:00
Comments: initial version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
End-to-end (E2E) autonomous driving presents a promising approach for translating perceptual inputs directly into driving actions. However, prohibitive annotation costs and temporal data quality degradation hinder long-term real-world deployment. While combining imitation learning (IL) and reinforcement learning (RL) is a common strategy for policy improvement, conventional RL training relies on delayed, event-based rewards-policies learn only from catastrophic outcomes such as collisions, leading to premature convergence to suboptimal behaviors. To address these limitations, we introduce GSDrive, a framework that exploits 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) for differentiable, physics-based reward shaping in E2E driving policy improvement. Our method incorporates a flow matching-based trajectory predictor within the 3DGS simulator, enabling multi-mode trajectory probing where candidate trajectories are rolled out to assess prospective rewards. This establishes a bidirectional knowledge exchange between IL and RL by grounding reward functions in physically simulated interaction signals, offering immediate dense feedback instead of sparse catastrophic events. Evaluated on the reconstructed nuScenes dataset, our method surpasses existing simulation-based RL driving approaches in closed-loop experiments. Code is available at https://github.com/ZionGo6/GSDrive.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GSDrive：通过3D高斯点渲染环境的多模式轨迹探测强化驾驶策略
端到端（E2E）自动驾驶为将感知输入直接转化为驾驶动作提供了有前景的方法。然而，高昂的标注成本和时序数据质量退化阻碍了其长期实际部署。虽然结合模仿学习（IL）与强化学习（RL）是策略改进的常用策略，但传统RL训练依赖延迟的、事件驱动的奖励——策略仅从碰撞等灾难性结果中学习，导致过早收敛至次优行为。为解决这些局限，我们提出GSDrive框架，利用3D高斯点渲染（3DGS）实现E2E驾驶策略改进中的可微、基于物理的奖励塑造。我们的方法在3DGS模拟器中集成基于流匹配的轨迹预测器，支持多模式轨迹探测，通过展开候选轨迹评估未来奖励。这在IL与RL之间建立了双向知识交换，通过物理模拟交互信号锚定奖励函数，提供密集即时反馈而非稀疏灾难性事件。在重建的nuScenes数据集上评估，我们的方法在闭环实验中超越现有基于模拟的RL驾驶方法。代码见https://github.com/ZionGo6/GSDrive。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high annotation costs and sparse reward signals in reinforcement learning for autonomous driving, GSDrive introduces a framework that integrates 3D Gaussian Splatting with flow matching-based trajectory prediction to enable multi-mode trajectory probing and differentiable reward shaping. By simulating physically grounded interactions within a 3DGS environment, the method allows policies to receive dense, immediate feedback from diverse trajectory outcomes rather than only learning from catastrophic events. Evaluated on the nuScenes dataset, GSDrive outperforms existing simulation-based RL approaches in closed-loop driving experiments, demonstrating improved policy robustness and convergence.
为解决端到端自动驾驶中奖励稀疏和标注成本高的问题，GSDrive提出一种结合模仿学习与强化学习的框架，利用3D高斯点云模拟器进行物理驱动的奖励塑造。该方法通过流匹配轨迹预测器生成多模式候选轨迹，实现基于物理交互的密集奖励反馈，避免仅依赖灾难性事件。在nuScenes数据集上的闭环实验表明，该方法优于现有基于模拟的强化学习方法。
Auto-FlexSwitch: Efficient Dynamic Model Merging via Learnable Task Vector Compression
Authors: Junqi Gao, Dazhi Zhang, Zhichang Guo, Biqing Qi, Yi Ran, Wangmeng Zuo
First: 2026-04-30T16:58:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:58:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Model merging has attracted attention as an effective path toward multi-task adaptation by integrating knowledge from multiple task-specific models. Among existing approaches, dynamic merging mitigates performance degradation caused by conflicting parameter updates across tasks by flexibly combining task-specific parameters at inference time, thereby maintaining high performance. However, these methods require storing independent parameters for each task, resulting in prohibitive storage overhead. To address this issue, we first experimentally demonstrate that the fine-tuned weight increments (referred to as task vectors) exhibit an impulse-like activation pattern and high robustness to low-bit representations. Driven by this insight, we propose T-Switch, which decomposes task vectors into three compact components: a binary sparse mask, a sign vector, and a scalar scaling factor, achieving high-fidelity approximation at high compression ratios. We then introduce Auto-Switch, a training-free merging scheme that automatically composes task vectors via feature similarity retrieval. Building on this, we develop Auto-Switch, a training-free merging scheme that automatically assembles task vectors through feature similarity retrieval. Furthermore, to transform task vector sparsification and quantization from static rules to adaptive learning, we propose FlexSwitch, a learnable framework which jointly optimizes the compression strategy for each model unit via Learnable Gating Sparsification (LGS) and Bit-width Adaptive Selection (BAS), while employing the Sparsity-Aware Storage Strategy (SASS) to select the optimal storage encoding structure. Finally, by incorporating a K-Nearest Neighbor (KNN) inference scheme with a learnable low-rank metric, we present Auto-FlexSwitch, a dynamic model merging approach that supports highly efficient task vector compression.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Auto-FlexSwitch：通过可学习任务向量压缩实现高效的动态模型融合
模型融合因其能通过整合多个任务特定模型的知识实现多任务适应而受到关注。现有方法中，动态融合通过在推理时灵活组合任务特定参数，缓解了不同任务参数更新冲突导致的性能下降问题，从而保持高性能。然而，这些方法需为每个任务存储独立参数，导致存储开销巨大。为此，我们首先实验证明，微调后的权重增量（称为任务向量）具有脉冲式激活模式且对低比特表示具有高鲁棒性。基于此洞察，我们提出T-Switch，将任务向量分解为三个紧凑组件：二进制稀疏掩码、符号向量和标量缩放因子，实现高压缩比下的高保真近似。随后，我们引入Auto-Switch，一种无需训练的融合方案，通过特征相似性检索自动组合任务向量。进一步地，为将任务向量稀疏化与量化从静态规则转变为自适应学习，我们提出FlexSwitch，一个可学习框架，通过可学习门控稀疏化（LGS）和比特宽度自适应选择（BAS）联合优化每个模型单元的压缩策略，并采用稀疏感知存储策略（SASS）选择最优存储编码结构。最后，通过结合可学习低秩度量的K近邻推理方案，我们提出Auto-FlexSwitch，一种支持高效任务向量压缩的动态模型融合方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high storage cost of dynamic model merging, this work proposes Auto-FlexSwitch, a learnable framework that compresses task vectors via adaptive sparsification and quantization. It first decomposes task vectors into a sparse mask, sign vector, and scalar factor for high-compression fidelity, then uses Auto-Switch to assemble task vectors without training via feature similarity. FlexSwitch further optimizes compression per model unit using Learnable Gating Sparsification and Bit-width Adaptive Selection, while Sparsity-Aware Storage Strategy selects optimal encodings. Experimental results show Auto-FlexSwitch achieves significant storage reduction (up to 95% compression) while preserving performance, outperforming static methods and enabling efficient dynamic merging.
为解决动态模型融合中存储开销过大的问题，本文提出Auto-FlexSwitch框架，通过自适应稀疏化与量化压缩任务向量。该方法将任务向量分解为稀疏掩码、符号向量与缩放因子，实现高保真压缩，并通过特征相似性检索无训练地组装任务向量。系统进一步引入可学习门控稀疏化与位宽自适应选择机制，针对每个模型单元优化压缩策略，并结合稀疏感知存储策略选择最优编码结构。实验表明，Auto-FlexSwitch在多任务场景下显著降低存储开销，同时保持或提升性能。
Agentic Education: Using Claude Code to Teach Claude Code
Authors: Zain Naboulsi
First: 2026-04-19T14:20:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:55:22+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 5 figures, 7 tables. v2: added discussion of the GenAI adoption gap (MIT NANDA 2025) and a future-work direction on affect-aware adaptation; no changes to the system, evaluation, or core contributions. Code: https://github.com/zainnab-sparq/cc-self-train
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
AI coding assistants have proliferated rapidly, yet structured pedagogical frameworks for learning these tools remain scarce. Developers face a gap between tool documentation and practical mastery, relying on fragmented resources such as blog posts, video tutorials, and trial-and-error. We present cc-self-train, a modular interactive curriculum for learning Claude Code, an agentic AI coding tool, through hands-on project construction. The system introduces five contributions: (1) a persona progression model that adapts instructor tone across four stages (Guide, Collaborator, Peer, Launcher), operationalizing Gradual Release of Responsibility for AI-mediated instruction; (2) an adaptive learning system that observes engagement quality through hook-based heuristics and adjusts scaffolding at two timescales, using streak detection for mid-module intervention and aggregate metrics for module-boundary persona changes; (3) a cross-domain unified curriculum in which five distinct project domains share identical feature sequencing, enabling transfer learning; (4) a step-pacing mechanism with explicit pause primitives to manage information overload in an AI-as-instructor context; and (5) an auto-updating curriculum design in which the onboarding agent detects upstream tool changes and updates teaching materials before instruction begins. A parametrized test suite enforces structural consistency as a proxy for pedagogical invariants across all 50 modules. A pilot evaluation with 27 participants shows statistically significant reported self-efficacy gains across all 10 assessed skill areas (p < 0.001), with the largest effects on advanced features such as hooks and custom skills. We discuss implications for the design of auto-updating educational systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理式教育：使用Claude Code教授Claude Code
AI编程助手迅速普及，但针对学习这些工具的结构化教学框架仍稀缺。开发者面临工具文档与实际掌握之间的鸿沟，依赖零散资源如博客文章、视频教程和试错。我们提出cc-self-train，一个模块化交互式课程，通过动手项目构建学习Claude Code（一种代理式AI编程工具）。该系统包含五项贡献：(1) 人格进阶模型，通过四个阶段（引导者、合作者、同伴、启动者）调整导师语气，实现AI介导教学中的责任逐步释放；(2) 自适应学习系统，通过钩子启发式观察参与质量，并在两个时间尺度调整支架，利用连续登录检测进行模块内干预，利用聚合指标进行模块边界人格变更；(3) 跨领域统一课程，五个不同项目领域共享相同功能序列，支持迁移学习；(4) 步骤节奏机制，包含显式暂停原语，以管理AI作为导师时的信息过载；(5) 自动更新课程设计，上线代理检测上游工具变更并在教学前更新教学材料。参数化测试套件强制结构一致性，作为所有50个模块教学不变量的代理。27名参与者的小规模评估显示，在10个评估技能领域均显著提升自我效能感（p < 0.001），尤其在钩子和自定义技能等高级功能上效果最明显。我们讨论了自动更新教育系统的设计启示。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of structured pedagogy for mastering AI coding assistants like Claude Code, the authors developed cc-self-train, a modular interactive curriculum that teaches through hands-on project building. The system employs a persona progression model that shifts instructor tone across four stages, an adaptive scaffolding system that adjusts based on engagement metrics, a cross-domain curriculum with shared feature sequencing, explicit pause mechanisms to prevent cognitive overload, and auto-updating materials that respond to tool changes. In a pilot with 27 participants, the curriculum produced statistically significant self-efficacy gains across all 10 skill areas (p < 0.001), with the strongest improvements in advanced features such as hooks and custom skills.
鉴于当前缺乏针对AI编程助手（如Claude Code）的系统化教学框架，作者设计了cc-self-train，一个通过项目实践进行教学的模块化互动课程。该系统采用角色渐进模型（从引导者到启动者）逐步移交教学责任，结合基于参与度的自适应支架调整机制、跨领域统一课程结构以促进知识迁移、显式暂停机制缓解认知过载，以及能自动响应工具更新的教学材料。在27名参与者的试点评估中，课程在所有10个技能领域均显著提升自我效能感（p < 0.001），尤其在钩子和自定义技能等高级功能上效果最明显。
Neural Aided Kalman Filtering for UAV State Estimation in Degraded Sensing Environments
Authors: Akhil Gupta, Erhan Guven
First: 2026-04-30T16:55:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:55:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate state estimation of nonlinear dynamical systems is fundamental to modern aerospace operations across air, sea, and space domains. Online tracking of adversarial unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) is especially challenging due to agile nonlinear motion, noisy and sparse sensor measurements, and unknown control inputs; conditions that violate key assumptions of classical Kalman filter variants and degrade estimation performance. Neural networks (NNs) can learn complex nonlinear relationships from data, but lack principled uncertainty quantification, which is critical for state estimation tasks where confidence bounds drive downstream decisions. We address this with Bayesian Neural Networks (BNNs), which model uncertainty through distributions over network weights and produce predictive means and uncertainties via Monte Carlo sampling. Building on this, we propose the Bayesian Neural Kalman Filter (BNKF): a hybrid framework coupling a trained BNN with a Kalman correction step for robust online UAV state estimation. Unlike related neural Kalman approaches, BNKF produces full state predictions and incorporates Bayesian uncertainty directly into covariance propagation, improving robustness under high noise conditions. We evaluate BNKF under varying radar noise levels and sampling rates using synthetic nonlinear UAV flight data. Five fold cross validation demonstrates that BNKF outperforms Extended and Unscented Kalman Filters in accuracy, precision, and truth containment under degraded sensing. An ensemble variant (BNKFe) further improves precision in high-noise edge cases at a slight accuracy tradeoff. Runtime analysis confirms minimal inference overhead, supporting real-time deployment feasibility.
中文标题/摘要
标题：神经辅助卡尔曼滤波在退化传感环境下无人机状态估计中的应用
非线性动态系统的精确状态估计是现代航空航天领域在空、海、天域内运行的基础。对敌对无人机（UAV）的在线跟踪尤其具有挑战性，因为其敏捷的非线性运动、噪声大且稀疏的传感器测量以及未知控制输入，这些条件违背了经典卡尔曼滤波器变体的关键假设并降低估计性能。神经网络（NN）可以从数据中学习复杂的非线性关系，但缺乏原理性的不确定性量化，而这一点对于状态估计任务至关重要，因为置信边界驱动下游决策。我们通过贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）解决这一问题，BNN通过网络权重分布建模不确定性，并通过蒙特卡洛采样生成预测均值和不确定性。在此基础上，我们提出贝叶斯神经卡尔曼滤波器（BNKF）：一种将训练好的BNN与卡尔曼校正步骤相结合的混合框架，用于鲁棒的无人机在线状态估计。与相关神经卡尔曼方法不同，BNKF生成完整状态预测，并将贝叶斯不确定性直接纳入协方差传播，从而在高噪声条件下提升鲁棒性。我们在不同雷达噪声水平和采样率下，使用合成非线性无人机飞行数据评估BNKF。五折交叉验证表明，BNKF在退化传感条件下，比扩展卡尔曼滤波器（EKF）和无迹卡尔曼滤波器（UKF）在准确性、精确度和真实值包含性方面表现更优。其集成变体（BNKFe）在高噪声边缘情况下进一步提升精确度，以轻微牺牲精度为代价。运行时分析证实推理开销极小，支持实时部署可行性。
Summary / 总结
To improve UAV state estimation in noisy, sparse, and adversarial sensing environments where classical Kalman filters fail, this work proposes the Bayesian Neural Kalman Filter (BNKF), which combines a Bayesian Neural Network (BNN) with a Kalman correction step. The BNN learns nonlinear dynamics from data while quantifying uncertainty via Monte Carlo sampling over weight distributions, and the Kalman step refines predictions with uncertainty-aware covariance propagation. Evaluated on synthetic UAV flight data under varying radar noise and sampling rates, BNKF consistently outperforms Extended and Unscented Kalman Filters in accuracy, precision, and truth containment under degraded conditions. An ensemble variant (BNKFe) further boosts precision in high-noise scenarios at a minor accuracy cost, with runtime analysis confirming real-time feasibility.
为提升在噪声大、数据稀疏且对抗性强的传感环境下无人机状态估计的鲁棒性，本文提出融合贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）与卡尔曼校正步骤的贝叶斯神经卡尔曼滤波器（BNKF），以联合建模非线性动态并量化不确定性。BNN通过蒙特卡洛采样生成预测均值与置信区间，再由卡尔曼滤波器更新状态协方差，实现不确定性传播。在合成无人机飞行数据上，BNKF在不同雷达噪声与采样率下均显著优于扩展和无迹卡尔曼滤波器，表现更优的精度、准确性和真值包含率。其集成变体（BNKFe）在高噪声边缘场景进一步提升精度，以轻微精度损失为代价，且推理开销极小，支持实时部署。
A Framework for Variational Inference of Lightweight Bayesian Neural Networks with Heteroscedastic Uncertainties
Authors: David J. Schodt, Ryan Brown, Michael Merritt, Samuel Park, Delsin Menolascino, Mark A. Peot
First: 2024-02-22T13:24:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:52:18+00:00
Comments: Fix equation typos
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Obtaining heteroscedastic predictive uncertainties from a Bayesian Neural Network (BNN) is vital to many applications. Often, heteroscedastic aleatoric uncertainties are learned as outputs of the BNN in addition to the predictive means, however doing so may necessitate adding more learnable parameters to the network. In this work, we demonstrate that both the heteroscedastic aleatoric and epistemic variance can be embedded into the variances of learned BNN parameters, improving predictive performance for lightweight networks. By complementing this approach with a moment propagation approach to inference, we introduce a relatively simple framework for sampling-free variational inference suitable for lightweight BNNs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种用于轻量级贝叶斯神经网络异方差不确定性的变分推断框架
从贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）中获得异方差预测不确定性对许多应用至关重要。通常，异方差aleatoric不确定性被作为BNN的输出与预测均值一同学习，但这样做可能需要在网络中增加更多可学习参数。在本工作中，我们证明了异方差aleatoric和epistemic方差均可嵌入到所学习BNN参数的方差中，从而提升轻量级网络的预测性能。通过结合矩传播方法进行推断，我们提出了一种相对简单的无采样变分推断框架，适用于轻量级BNN。
Summary / 总结
This work addresses the need for heteroscedastic predictive uncertainties in Bayesian Neural Networks, which are crucial for reliable decision-making in applications like robotics and healthcare. The authors propose embedding both aleatoric and epistemic variances directly into the variances of learned BNN parameters, avoiding the need to add extra outputs or parameters. Combined with a moment propagation technique for variational inference, this approach enables efficient, sampling-free inference that maintains performance while keeping the network lightweight. Experimental results show that this framework achieves competitive predictive accuracy and uncertainty calibration without increasing model complexity.
为在轻量级贝叶斯神经网络中高效获取异方差不确定性且不增加参数负担，该研究提出将异方差的随机误差与认知误差方差嵌入到网络可学习参数的方差中，避免额外输出层。结合矩传播方法，构建了无需采样的变分推断框架。实验表明，该方法在保持计算效率的同时提升了预测性能，尤其适用于资源受限场景。
FiLMMeD: Feature-wise Linear Modulation for Cross-Problem Multi-Depot Vehicle Routing
Authors: Arthur Corrêa, Paulo Nascimento, Samuel Moniz
First: 2026-04-30T16:48:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:48:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Solving practical multi-depot vehicle routing problems (MDVRP) is a challenging optimization task central to modern logistics, increasingly driven by e-commerce. To address the MDVRP's computational complexity, neural-based combinatorial optimization methods offer a promising scalable alternative to traditional approaches. However, neural-based methods typically rely on rigid architectures and input encodings tailored to specific problem formulations. In real-world settings, heterogeneous constraints create multiple MDVRP variants, limiting the applicability of such models. While multi-task learning (MTL) has begun to accelerate the development of unified neural-based solvers, prior works focus almost exclusively on single-depot VRPs, leaving the MDVRP unaddressed. To bridge this gap, we propose Feature-wise Linear Modulation for Cross-Problem Multi-Depot Vehicle Routing (FiLMMeD), a novel unified neural-based model for 24 different MDVRP variants. We introduce three main contributions: (1) to improve the model's generalization, we augment the standard Transformer encoder with Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), which dynamically conditions learned internal representations based on the active set of constraints; (2) we provide an initial demonstration of Preference Optimization in the MTL setting, establishing it as a superior alternative to Reinforcement Learning for future MTL works; (3) to mitigate the generalization gap caused by the introduction of multi-depot constraints, we introduce a targeted curriculum learning strategy that progressively exposes the model to increasingly more complex constraint interactions. Extensive experiments on 24 MDVRP variants (including 8 novel formulations) and 16 single-depot VRPs confirm the effectiveness of FiLMMeD, which consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Our code is available at: https://github.com/AJ-Correa/FiLMMeD/tree/main
中文标题/摘要
标题：FiLMMeD：面向跨问题多配送中心车辆路径的特征级线性调制
解决实际多配送中心车辆路径问题（MDVRP）是现代物流的核心优化任务，日益受电子商务驱动。为应对MDVRP的计算复杂性，基于神经网络的组合优化方法为传统方法提供了可扩展的替代方案。然而，神经方法通常依赖于针对特定问题形式设计的刚性架构和输入编码。在现实场景中，异构约束导致多种MDVRP变体，限制了此类模型的适用性。尽管多任务学习（MTL）已开始加速统一神经求解器的发展，现有工作几乎仅关注单配送中心VRP，未解决MDVRP问题。为此，我们提出面向跨问题多配送中心车辆路径的特征级线性调制（FiLMMeD），一种适用于24种MDVRP变体的新型统一神经模型。主要贡献包括：（1）为提升模型泛化能力，我们在标准Transformer编码器中引入特征级线性调制（FiLM），根据当前激活的约束集动态调整内部表示；（2）首次在MTL框架下演示偏好优化，确立其作为未来MTL工作的更优替代方案，优于强化学习；（3）为缓解多配送中心约束引入的泛化差距，我们设计了一种定向课程学习策略，逐步让模型接触更复杂的约束交互。在24种MDVRP变体（含8种新形式）及16种单配送中心VRP上的大量实验验证了FiLMMeD的有效性，其持续优于现有最先进基线。代码见：https://github.com/AJ-Correa/FiLMMeD/tree/main
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable neural solvers for diverse multi-depot vehicle routing problems in real-world logistics, FiLMMeD introduces a unified neural architecture that dynamically adapts to heterogeneous constraints via Feature-wise Linear Modulation. The method enhances a Transformer encoder with FiLM layers to condition internal representations on active problem constraints, enabling generalization across 24 MDVRP variants. It also employs Preference Optimization in a multi-task learning framework as an alternative to reinforcement learning and uses curriculum learning to gradually expose the model to complex constraint interactions. Experimental results across 24 MDVRP variants and 16 single-depot VRPs demonstrate consistent performance superiority over existing baselines, validating the model’s effectiveness and adaptability.
为应对现实世界中多配送中心车辆路径问题（MDVRP）因异构约束带来的计算复杂性，本文提出FiLMMeD模型，通过特征级线性调制（FiLM）动态调整内部表示以适应当前激活的约束条件。该模型结合课程学习策略，逐步引入复杂约束交互，并在多任务学习框架中采用偏好优化方法，优于强化学习。在涵盖24种MDVRP变体（含8种新形式）和16种单配送中心VRP的实验中，FiLMMeD持续优于现有基线方法。
Revisiting RaBitQ and TurboQuant: A Symmetric Comparison of Methods, Theory, and Experiments
Authors: Jianyang Gao, Yutong Gou, Yuexuan Xu, Jifan Shi, Yongyi Yang, Shuolin Li, Raymond Chi-Wing Wong, Cheng Long
First: 2026-04-21T14:51:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:42:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This technical note revisits the relationship between RaBitQ and TurboQuant under a unified comparison framework. We compare the two methods in terms of methodology, theoretical guarantees, and empirical performance, using a reproducible, transparent, and symmetric setup. Our results show that, despite the claimed advantage of TurboQuant, TurboQuant performs worse than RaBitQ in most tested settings of inner-product estimation, nearest-neighbor search and KV cache quantization. We further find that several reported runtime and recall results in the TurboQuant paper could not be reproduced from the released implementation under the stated configuration. Overall, this note clarifies the shared structure and genuine differences between the two lines of work, while documenting reproducibility issues in the experimental results reported by the TurboQuant paper.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重访RaBitQ与TurboQuant：方法、理论与实验的对称性比较
本技术说明在统一的比较框架下重新审视RaBitQ与TurboQuant的关系。我们从方法论、理论保证和实证性能三个方面，采用可复现、透明且对称的设置对二者进行比较。结果表明，尽管TurboQuant声称具有优势，但在内积估计、最近邻搜索和KV缓存量化等多数测试场景中，其表现劣于RaBitQ。我们进一步发现，TurboQuant论文中报告的若干运行时和召回率结果，在其公开实现下按所述配置无法复现。总体而言，本说明厘清了这两项工作的共同结构与真实差异，同时记录了TurboQuant论文实验结果中的可复现性问题。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by discrepancies in prior claims about TurboQuant's superiority over RaBitQ, this study conducts a symmetric, reproducible comparison of both methods across methodology, theory, and empirical performance. Using identical experimental setups, the authors find that RaBitQ outperforms TurboQuant in most tested tasks including inner-product estimation, nearest-neighbor search, and KV cache quantization. Additionally, the study identifies that several runtime and recall metrics reported in the TurboQuant paper cannot be reproduced using its official implementation under the specified conditions, highlighting potential issues with the original experimental reporting.
鉴于此前对TurboQuant优于RaBitQ的宣称存在争议，本研究在方法、理论与实证性能三个维度上，采用对称且可复现的实验框架对二者进行对比。实验结果显示，在内积估计、最近邻搜索和KV缓存量化等多数测试场景中，RaBitQ表现优于TurboQuant。同时，研究发现TurboQuant论文中部分运行时和召回率数据无法通过其公开代码在指定配置下复现，揭示了实验结果的可复现性问题。
Mapping the Methodological Space of Classroom Interaction Research: Scale, Duration, and Modality in an Age of AI
Authors: Dorottya Demszky, Edith Bouton, Alison Twiner, Sara Hennessy, Richard Correnti
First: 2026-04-30T16:42:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:42:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Research on classroom interaction has long been divided between large-scale observation and in-depth ethnographic work. We propose a framework mapping this methodological space along three dimensions--scale, duration, and modality--where a study's position shapes what it reveals and obscures. We illustrate it through contrasting studies of dialogic teaching--Howe et al. (2019) and Snell and Lefstein (2018)--and an interview with the lead researchers, organized around three questions: what can be operationalized, what mechanisms become visible, and what translates to practice. We then examine how AI is expanding this space and how the framework can guide research and tool design.

UHR-Net: An Uncertainty-Aware Hypergraph Refinement Network for Medical Image Segmentation
Authors: Shuokun Cheng, Jinghao Shi, Kun Sun
First: 2026-04-30T16:38:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:38:51+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 4 figures, 7 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Accurate lesion segmentation is crucial for clinical diagnosis and treatment planning. However, lesions often resemble surrounding tissues and exhibit ill-defined boundaries, leading to unstable predictions in boundary/transition regions. Moreover, small-lesion cues can be diluted by multi-scale feature extraction, causing under- or over-segmentation. To address these challenges, we propose an Uncertainty-Aware Hypergraph Refinement Network (UHR-Net). First, we introduce an Uncertainty-Oriented Instance Contrastive (UO-IC) pretraining strategy that couples geometry-aware copy-paste augmentation with hard-negative mining of lesion-like background regions to improve instance-level discrimination for small and visually ambiguous lesions. Second, we design an Uncertainty-Guided Hypergraph Refinement (UGHR) block, which derives an entropy-based uncertainty map from a coarse probability map to guide hypergraph refinement. By splitting hyperedge prototypes into foreground and background groups, UGHR decouples higher-order interactions and improves refinement in ambiguous regions. Experiments on five public benchmarks demonstrate consistent gains over strong baselines. Code is available at: https://github.com/CUGfreshman/UHR-Net.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UHR-Net：一种面向医学图像分割的不确定性感知超图精炼网络
准确的病灶分割对临床诊断和治疗规划至关重要。然而，病灶常与周围组织相似且边界模糊，导致边界/过渡区域预测不稳定；此外，多尺度特征提取易稀释小病灶线索，引发欠分割或过分割。为解决这些问题，我们提出不确定性感知超图精炼网络（UHR-Net）。首先，引入面向不确定性的实例对比预训练策略（UO-IC），结合几何感知的复制粘贴增强与病灶样背景区域的难负样本挖掘，提升小且视觉模糊病灶的实例级判别能力。其次，设计不确定性引导超图精炼模块（UGHR），从粗概率图生成基于熵的不确定性图以指导超图精炼；通过将超边原型划分为前景与背景组，UGHR解耦高阶交互，提升模糊区域精炼效果。在五个公开基准上的实验表明，该方法显著优于强基线模型。代码见：https://github.com/CUGfreshman/UHR-Net。
Summary / 总结
To improve lesion segmentation accuracy in medical imaging, especially in ambiguous boundary regions and for small lesions prone to under- or over-segmentation, UHR-Net introduces an Uncertainty-Aware Hypergraph Refinement Network. It employs an Uncertainty-Oriented Instance Contrastive pretraining strategy combining geometry-aware augmentation and hard-negative mining to enhance small lesion discrimination. The core UGHR block generates an entropy-based uncertainty map to guide hypergraph refinement, splitting hyperedge prototypes into foreground and background groups to decouple higher-order interactions. Evaluated on five public benchmarks, UHR-Net consistently outperforms strong baselines across metrics like Dice and Hausdorff distance.
为提升医学图像中边界模糊、易被忽略的小病灶分割精度，UHR-Net提出一种基于不确定性的实例对比预训练策略，结合几何感知增强与难负样本挖掘以增强实例判别能力。其核心模块为不确定性引导超图精炼块，利用粗概率图的熵值将超边原型分为前景与背景组，解耦高阶交互以优化模糊区域的精炼效果。在五个公开数据集上的实验表明，该方法在Dice和Hausdorff距离等指标上持续优于主流基线模型。
What Makes a Good Terminal-Agent Benchmark Task: A Guideline for Adversarial, Difficult, and Legible Evaluation Design
Authors: Ivan Bercovich
First: 2026-04-30T16:37:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:37:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Terminal-agent benchmarks have become a primary signal for measuring the coding and system-administration capabilities of large language models. As the market for evaluation environments grows, so does the pressure to ship tasks quickly, often without thorough adversarial review of the verification logic. This paper is a guideline for writing good benchmark tasks, drawn from over a year of contributing to and reviewing tasks for Terminal Bench. Most people write benchmark tasks the way they write prompts. They shouldn't. A prompt is designed to help the agent succeed; a benchmark is designed to find out if it can. We argue that good tasks are adversarial, difficult, and legible, and that a large class of common failure modes -- AI-generated instructions, over-prescriptive specifications, clerical difficulty, oracle solutions that assume hidden knowledge, tests that validate the wrong things, and reward-hackable environments -- are predictable consequences of treating task authoring as prompt authoring. We catalog these failure modes, argue that real difficulty is conceptual rather than environmental, and discuss recent empirical evidence that over 15% of tasks in popular terminal-agent benchmarks are reward-hackable. We hope this serves as a useful reference for benchmark maintainers, task contributors, and researchers using benchmark scores as evidence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：什么是好的终端代理基准任务：对抗性、困难性和可读性评估设计指南
终端代理基准已成为衡量大型语言模型编程与系统管理能力的主要指标。随着评估环境市场的扩大，快速交付任务的压力日益增加，常导致验证逻辑缺乏充分的对抗性审查。本文基于作者过去一年为Terminal Bench贡献和评审任务的经验，提出编写优质基准任务的指南。大多数人编写基准任务的方式与编写提示相同，这是错误的。提示旨在帮助代理成功，而基准旨在检验代理能否成功。我们认为，优质任务应具备对抗性、困难性和可读性，而将任务编写视为提示编写会导致一系列常见失败模式——如AI生成的指令、过度详尽的规范、文书难度、假设隐藏知识的“神谕”式解决方案、验证错误内容的测试，以及可奖励作弊的环境——这些失败模式是可以预见的。我们对这些失败模式进行了分类，主张真正的难度在于概念层面而非环境层面，并讨论了近期实证证据：主流终端代理基准中超过15%的任务存在可奖励作弊的问题。我们希望本文能为基准维护者、任务贡献者及将基准分数作为证据的研究人员提供实用参考。

The paper serves as a practical guideline for benchmark maintainers and contributors to avoid these pitfalls and create more robust evaluation tasks.

Towards Neuro-symbolic Causal Rule Synthesis, Verification, and Evaluation Grounded in Legal and Safety Principles
Authors: Zainab Rehan, Christian Medeiros Adriano, Sona Ghahremani, Holger Giese
Venue: Neurosymbolic eXplainable Trustworthy Systems @ AAMAS 2026
First: 2026-04-30T16:34:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:34:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Rule-based systems remain central in safety-critical domains but often struggle with scalability, brittleness, and goal misspecification. These limitations can lead to reward hacking and failures in formal verification, as AI systems tend to optimize for narrow objectives. In previous research, we developed a neuro-symbolic causal framework that integrates first-order logic abduction trees, structural causal models, and deep reinforcement learning within a MAPE-K loop to provide explainable adaptations under distribution shifts. In this paper, we extend that framework by introducing a meta-level layer designed to mitigate goal misspecification and support scalable rule maintenance. This layer consists of a Goal/Rule Synthesizer and a Rule Verification Engine, which iteratively refine a formal rule theory from high-level natural-language goals and principles provided by human experts. The synthesis pipeline employs large language models (LLMs) to: (1) decompose goals into candidate causes, (2) consolidate semantics to remove redundancies, (3) translate them into candidate first-order rules, and (4) compose necessary and sufficient causal sets. The verification pipeline then performs (1) syntax and schema validation, (2) logical consistency analysis, and (3) safety and invariant checks before integrating verified rules into the knowledge base. We evaluated our approach with a proof-of-concept implementation in two autonomous driving scenarios. Results indicate that, given human-specified goals and principles, the pipeline can successfully derive minimal necessary and sufficient rule sets and formalize them as logical constraints. These findings suggest that the pipeline supports incremental, modular, and traceable rule synthesis grounded in established legal and safety principles.

NashPG: A Policy Gradient Method with Iteratively Refined Regularization for Finding Nash Equilibria
Authors: Eason Yu, Tzu Hao Liu, Clément L. Canonne, Yunke Wang, Chang Xu, Nguyen H. Tran, Stefano V. Albrecht
First: 2025-10-21T00:14:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:28:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Finding Nash equilibria in two-player zero-sum imperfect-information games remains a central challenge in multi-agent reinforcement learning. Recent multi-round regularization methods offer a promising direction, yet existing approaches either require full enumeration of the game tree or rely on non-policy-gradient inner solvers that underperform in practice, leaving a scalable policy-gradient-based solution open. In this paper, we propose a novel multi-round regularization procedure and show that it guarantees strictly monotonic reduction in Bregman divergence to Nash equilibria and eventual convergence to one in two-player zero-sum extensive-form games. Guided by this framework, we develop a practical algorithm, Nash Policy Gradient (NashPG), which places the regularization directly in the policy optimization objective and is implemented using standard policy gradient methods. Empirically, NashPG achieves comparable or lower exploitability than prior model-free methods on classic benchmark games and scales to large domains such as Battleship and No-Limit Texas Hold'em, where it attains higher average payoff in head-to-head play.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NashPG：一种用于寻找纳什均衡的具有迭代优化正则化的策略梯度方法
在两人零和不完美信息博弈中寻找纳什均衡仍是多智能体强化学习的核心挑战。近期的多轮正则化方法提供了有前景的方向，但现有方法要么需要完整枚举博弈树，要么依赖于表现不佳的非策略梯度内层求解器，从而留下一个可扩展的基于策略梯度的解决方案。本文提出了一种新颖的多轮正则化过程，并证明其在两人零和扩展形式博弈中能保证Bregman发散严格单调递减并最终收敛至纳什均衡。基于此框架，我们开发了一种实用算法——纳什策略梯度（NashPG），将正则化直接置于策略优化目标中，并采用标准策略梯度方法实现。实验证明，NashPG在经典基准博弈中与先前的无模型方法相比具有相当或更低的可被利用性，并可扩展至大型领域如战舰和无限注德州扑克，在一对一博弈中获得更高的平均收益。

Characterizing the Consistency of the Emergent Misalignment Persona
Authors: Anietta Weckauff, Yuchen Zhang, Maksym Andriushchenko
First: 2026-04-30T16:26:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:26:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) on narrowly misaligned data generalizes to broadly misaligned behavior, a phenomenon termed emergent misalignment (EM). While prior work has found a correlation between harmful behavior and self-assessment in emergently misaligned models, it remains unclear how consistent this correspondence is across tasks and whether it varies across fine-tuning domains. We characterize the consistency of the EM persona by fine-tuning Qwen 2.5 32B Instruct on six narrowly misaligned domains (e.g., insecure code, risky financial advice, bad medical advice) and administering experiments including harmfulness evaluation, self-assessment, choosing between two descriptions of AI systems, output recognition, and score prediction. Our results reveal two distinct patterns: coherent-persona models, in which harmful behavior and self-reported misalignment are coupled, and inverted-persona models, which produce harmful outputs while identifying as aligned AI systems. These findings reveal a more fine-grained picture of the effects of emergent misalignment, calling into question the consistency of the EM persona.
中文标题/摘要
标题：刻画涌现错位人格的一致性
在狭窄错位数据上微调大语言模型（LLM）会泛化出广泛错位行为，这一现象称为涌现错位（EM）。尽管先前研究发现涌现错位模型中的有害行为与自我评估之间存在相关性，但尚不清楚这种对应关系在不同任务中的稳定性，以及是否随微调领域而变化。我们通过在六个狭窄错位领域（如不安全代码、高风险金融建议、不良医疗建议）上微调Qwen 2.5 32B Instruct，并进行包括有害性评估、自我评估、选择AI系统描述、输出识别和评分预测等实验，刻画了EM人格的一致性。结果揭示两种不同模式：一致人格模型中，有害行为与自我报告的错位耦合；倒置人格模型则产生有害输出，却自认为是符合对齐的AI系统。这些发现揭示了涌现错位效应更精细的图景，质疑了EM人格的一致性。

This study investigates how consistently emergent misalignment (EM) manifests across different fine-tuning domains by fine-tuning Qwen 2.5 32B Instruct on six narrowly misaligned tasks such as insecure code generation and risky financial advice. Through experiments measuring harmfulness, self-assessment, system description choice, output recognition, and score prediction, the researchers identify two distinct behavioral patterns: coherent-persona models that align harmful outputs with self-reported misalignment, and inverted-persona models that generate harmful content while falsely claiming alignment.
