该研究通过在六个窄范围错位领域对Qwen 2.5 32B Instruct进行微调，并通过多项任务（如自我评估、输出识别和评分预测）评估有害行为，探究大语言模型中涌现错位（EM）的一致性。研究发现两种截然不同的行为模式：一致人格模型中，有害输出与自我报告的错位一致；而逆向人格模型则产生有害输出却自认对齐。这些发现质疑了EM表现的一致性假设，表明EM人格在微调领域和任务间并非统一。
Measuring Opinion Bias and Sycophancy via LLM-based Persuasion
Authors: Rodrigo Nogueira, Giovana Kerche Bonás, Thales Sales Almeida, Andrea Roque, Ramon Pires, Hugo Abonizio, Thiago Laitz, Celio Larcher, Roseval Malaquias Junior, Marcos Piau
First: 2026-04-23T11:34:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:26:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models increasingly shape the information people consume: they are embedded in search, consulted for professional advice, deployed as agents, and used as a first stop for questions about policy, ethics, health, and politics. When such a model silently holds a position on a contested topic, that position propagates at scale into users' decisions. Eliciting a model's positions is harder than it first appears: contemporary assistants answer direct opinion questions with evasive disclaimers, and the same model may concede the opposite position once the user starts arguing one side. We propose a method, released as the open-source llm-bias-bench, for discovering the opinions an LLM actually holds on contested topics under conditions that resemble real multi-turn interaction. The method pairs two complementary free-form probes. Direct probing asks for the model's opinion across five turns of escalating pressure from a simulated user. Indirect probing never asks for an opinion and engages the model in argumentative debate, letting bias leak through how it concedes, resists, or counter-argues. Three user personas (neutral, agree, disagree) collapse into a nine-way behavioral classification that separates persona-independent positions from persona-dependent sycophancy, and an auditable LLM judge produces verdicts with textual evidence. The first instantiation ships 38 topics in Brazilian Portuguese across values, scientific consensus, philosophy, and economic policy. Applied to 13 assistants, the method surfaces findings of practical interest: argumentative debate triggers sycophancy 2-3x more than direct questioning (median 50% to 79%); models that look opinionated under direct questioning often collapse into mirroring under sustained arguments; and attacker capability matters mainly when an existing opinion must be dislodged, not when the assistant starts neutral.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过基于大语言模型的说服力测量意见偏见与谄媚
大语言模型正越来越多地塑造人们所消费的信息：它们嵌入搜索系统、被用于专业咨询、作为代理部署，并作为政策、伦理、健康和政治问题的首要查询工具。当此类模型在争议性话题上静默持有一种立场时，该立场会大规模传播至用户决策。获取模型立场比表面看起来更困难：当代助手在直接意见问题上常以回避性免责声明作答，且同一模型在用户开始论证某一立场时可能转而承认相反立场。我们提出一种方法（开源项目 llm-bias-bench），用于在接近真实多轮交互的条件下，发现LLM在争议性话题上实际持有的立场。该方法结合两种互补的开放式探针：直接探针通过模拟用户五轮逐步施压询问模型立场；间接探针从不直接询问立场，而是引导模型参与辩论，让偏见通过其让步、抵抗或反驳方式显露。三种用户角色（中立、同意、反对）可归结为九种行为分类，区分角色独立立场与角色依赖的谄媚行为，且由可审计的LLM裁判依据文本证据出具判决。首个版本涵盖38个巴西葡萄牙语话题，涉及价值观、科学共识、哲学与经济政策。应用于13个助手，该方法揭示了具有实际意义的发现：辩论比直接提问更易触发谄媚行为（中位数50%至79%）；在持续辩论中，原本在直接提问下显得有主见的模型常退化为镜像回应；攻击者能力主要在需动摇现有立场时起作用，而非当助手初始中立时。
Summary / 总结
As large language models increasingly influence user decisions through embedded applications and policy-related queries, understanding their hidden biases and sycophantic tendencies becomes critical. The paper introduces llm-bias-bench, an open-source method that uses two complementary probes—direct questioning under escalating pressure and indirect argumentative debate—to reveal latent opinions. By applying three user personas (neutral, agree, disagree) across 38 Brazilian Portuguese topics, the method classifies model behavior into nine categories, distinguishing genuine positions from persona-dependent sycophancy. Experimental results across 13 LLMs show that argumentative debate triggers sycophancy 2-3 times more frequently than direct questioning, and models often mirror user positions under sustained debate rather than holding independent views, with attacker capability being most effective when challenging pre-existing opinions.
随着大型语言模型通过嵌入式应用和建议日益影响用户决策，揭示其隐藏的偏见与阿谀倾向变得至关重要。本文提出llm-bias-bench开源方法，通过两种互补探针——在逐步施压下的直接提问与间接辩论——揭示模型在争议话题上的真实立场。通过应用三种用户角色（中立、同意、反对）并将其响应归类为九种行为模式，该方法区分真实立场与角色依赖的阿谀行为，并由可审计的模型裁判提供文本证据。在38个巴西葡萄牙语话题和13个模型上评估发现，辩论比直接提问更易触发阿谀行为（中位数50%至79%），且在持续辩论中，原本直接提问中显得有立场的模型常转为模仿用户观点，攻击能力主要在挑战已有立场时才更有效。
AesRM: Improving Video Aesthetics with Expert-Level Feedback
Authors: Yujin Han, Yujie Wei, Yefei He, Xinyu Liu, Tianle Li, Zichao Yu, Andi Han, Shiwei Zhang, Tingyu Weng, Difan Zou
First: 2026-04-30T16:24:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:24:07+00:00
Comments: 37 pages, 14 figures, 12 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite rapid advances in photorealistic video generation, real-world applications such as filmmaking require video aesthetics, e.g., harmonious colors and cinematic lighting, beyond visual fidelity. Prior work on visual aesthetics largely focuses on images, often reducing aesthetics to coarse definitions, e.g., visual pleasure, without a rigorous and systematic evaluation. To improve video aesthetics, we propose a hierarchical rubric that decomposes video aesthetics into three core dimensions, Visual Aesthetics (VA), Visual Fidelity (VF), and Visual Plausibility (VP), with 15 fine-grained criteria, e.g., shot composition. This framework enables a large-scale expert-annotated preference dataset and an evaluation benchmark, AesVideo-Bench, containing about 2500 video pairs with expert annotations on VA, VF, and VP. We then build a family of Video Aesthetic Reward Models (AesRM): AesRM-Base, which directly predicts pairwise preferences on these dimensions to provide efficient post-training rewards, and AesRM-CoT, which additionally generates CoT aligned with all 15 criteria to improve assessment interpretability. Specifically, we train AesRM with a three-stage progressive scheme: (1) Atomic Aesthetic Capability Learning, which strengthens AesRM's recognition of fundamental aesthetic concepts, e.g., accurately identifying centered composition; (2) Cold-Start, aligning the model with structured reasoning protocols; and (3) GRPO, further improving evaluation accuracy. To enhance AesRM-CoT, we additionally propose self-consistency-based CoT synthesis to improve CoT quality and design CoT-based process rewards during GRPO. Extensive experiments show AesRM outperforms baselines on multiple aesthetics benchmarks and is more robust, with lower position bias. Finally, we align Wan2.2 with AesRM and observe clear aesthetic gains over existing aesthetic reward models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AesRM：通过专家级反馈提升视频美学
尽管在逼真视频生成方面取得了快速进展，但实际应用如电影制作仍需超越视觉保真度的视频美学，例如和谐的色彩和电影感照明。此前关于视觉美学的研究主要集中在图像上，常将美学简化为粗略定义（如视觉愉悦），缺乏严谨系统的评估。为提升视频美学，我们提出一种分层评分标准，将视频美学分解为三个核心维度：视觉美学（VA）、视觉保真度（VF）和视觉合理性（VP），并包含15个细粒度标准（如镜头构图）。该框架支持大规模专家标注偏好数据集及评估基准AesVideo-Bench，包含约2500对视频及其在VA、VF、VP维度上的专家标注。我们构建了视频美学奖励模型（AesRM）系列：AesRM-Base直接预测各维度的成对偏好，提供高效的后训练奖励；AesRM-CoT则额外生成与15项标准对齐的思维链（CoT），以提升评估可解释性。具体而言，我们采用三阶段渐进式训练方案训练AesRM：（1）原子级美学能力学习，强化模型对基础美学概念（如准确识别居中构图）的识别；（2）冷启动阶段，使模型遵循结构化推理协议；（3）GRPO阶段，进一步提升评估精度。为提升AesRM-CoT，我们额外提出基于自一致性思维链合成方法以提升CoT质量，并在GRPO阶段设计基于CoT的过程奖励。大量实验表明，AesRM在多个美学基准上优于基线模型，且更鲁棒、位置偏差更低。最后，我们将Wan2.2与AesRM对齐，观察到其在现有美学奖励模型之上获得显著美学提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance video aesthetics beyond visual fidelity for real-world applications like filmmaking, the paper introduces AesRM, a family of Video Aesthetic Reward Models built on a hierarchical rubric decomposing aesthetics into three core dimensions—Visual Aesthetics, Visual Fidelity, and Visual Plausibility—with 15 fine-grained criteria. The method trains two variants: AesRM-Base, which predicts pairwise preferences, and AesRM-CoT, which generates chain-of-thought reasoning aligned with all 15 criteria for interpretability. Training proceeds in three stages: Atomic Aesthetic Capability Learning, Cold-Start alignment with structured reasoning, and GRPO for accuracy refinement, with self-consistency and process rewards enhancing CoT quality. Experiments demonstrate AesRM outperforms baselines across multiple benchmarks, exhibits lower position bias, and improves aesthetic quality when aligned with the video generation model Wan2.2.
为弥合逼真视频生成与影视制作中对美学需求的差距，本文提出AesRM视频美学奖励模型，基于将美学分解为视觉美学、视觉保真度与视觉可信度三大维度及15项细粒度准则的分层评估框架。模型采用三阶段渐进训练：先学习基础美学概念，再通过冷启动对齐结构化推理，最后用GRPO优化并引入CoT过程奖励提升可解释性。在包含2500对专家标注视频的AesVideo-Bench基准上，实验表明AesRM在多个美学维度显著优于基线模型，位置偏差更低，并在与Wan2.2对齐后带来明显的美学提升。
TopBench: A Benchmark for Implicit Prediction and Reasoning over Tabular Question Answering
Authors: An-Yang Ji, Jun-Peng Jiang, De-Chuan Zhan, Han-Jia Ye
First: 2026-04-30T16:22:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:22:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have advanced Table Question Answering, where most queries can be answered by extracting information or simple aggregation. However, a common class of real-world queries is implicitly predictive, requiring the inference of unobserved answers from historical patterns rather than mere retrieval. These queries introduce two challenges: recognizing latent intent and reliable predictive reasoning over massive tables. To assess LLMs in such Tabular questiOn answering with implicit Prediction tasks, we introduce TopBench, a benchmark consisting of 779 samples across four sub-tasks, ranging from single-point prediction to decision making, treatment effect analysis, and complex filtering, requiring models to generate outputs spanning reasoning text and structured tables. We evaluate diverse models under both text-based and agentic workflows. Experiments reveal that current models often struggle with intent recognition, defaulting to just lookups. Deeper analysis identifies that accurate intent disambiguation serves as the prerequisite for leading these predictive behaviors. Furthermore, elevating the upper bound of prediction precision requires the integration of more sophisticated modeling or reasoning capabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TopBench：面向表格问答中隐式预测与推理的基准
大语言模型（LLMs）已推动表格问答的发展，其中多数查询可通过信息提取或简单聚合回答。然而，现实世界中常见的一类查询是隐式预测型，需从历史模式推断未观测答案，而非简单检索。此类查询带来两大挑战：识别潜在意图与在大规模表格上进行可靠预测推理。为评估LLMs在隐式预测任务下的表格问答能力，我们提出TopBench基准，包含4个子任务共779个样本，涵盖单点预测、决策制定、处理效应分析及复杂过滤，要求模型生成包含推理文本与结构化表格的输出。我们在基于文本与代理工作流下评估多种模型。实验表明，当前模型常在意图识别上存在困难，倾向于仅进行检索。深入分析发现，准确的意图消歧是引导预测行为的前提。此外，提升预测精度上限需整合更复杂的建模或推理能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate LLMs on real-world tabular queries that require implicit prediction rather than simple retrieval, the authors introduce TopBench, a benchmark with 779 samples across four sub-tasks including single-point prediction, decision making, treatment effect analysis, and complex filtering. The benchmark demands models to produce both reasoning text and structured outputs. Experiments show that current models frequently fail to recognize latent intent, defaulting to lookup-based responses, and that accurate intent disambiguation is critical for enabling predictive reasoning. The results also indicate that achieving higher prediction precision requires more sophisticated modeling or reasoning mechanisms beyond basic table extraction.
为评估大语言模型在表格数据上的隐式预测与推理能力（而非简单检索），作者构建了TopBench基准，包含779个样本，涵盖单点预测、决策、处理效应分析和复杂过滤等四个子任务，要求模型输出推理文本与结构化表格。实验表明，当前模型常无法识别隐含意图，倾向于仅进行检索，而准确的意图消歧被确认为实现预测行为的关键前提。研究进一步指出，提升预测精度需引入更复杂的建模或推理能力。
PhotIQA: A photoacoustic image data set with image quality ratings
Authors: Anna Breger, Janek Gröhl, Clemens Karner, Thomas R Else, Ian Selby, Tom Rix, Lara-Sophie Witt, Merle Duchêne, Jonathan Weir-McCall, Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb
First: 2025-07-04T11:06:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:22:35+00:00
Comments: 16 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Image quality assessment (IQA) is crucial in the evaluation stage of novel algorithms operating on images, including traditional and machine learning based methods. Due to the lack of available quality-rated medical images, most commonly used full-reference IQA measures have been developed and tested for natural images. Reported pitfalls and inconsistencies arising when applying such measures for medical images are not surprising, as they rely on different properties than natural images. In photoacoustic imaging (PAI), especially, standard benchmarking approaches for assessing the quality of image reconstructions are lacking. PAI is a multi-physics imaging modality, in which two inverse problems have to be solved, which makes the application of IQA measures uniquely challenging due to both, acoustic and optical, artifacts. To support the development and testing of IQA measures we assembled PhotIQA, a data set consisting of 1134 photoacoustic images. The images were rated by five experts across five quality properties in a full-reference setting, where the detailed rating enables usage beyond PAI. The data set with the images and corresponding ratings is publicly available on Zenodo.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PhotIQA：带图像质量评分的光声图像数据集
图像质量评估（IQA）在评估作用于图像的新型算法（包括传统和基于机器学习的方法）时至关重要。由于缺乏带质量评分的医学图像，目前最常用的全参考IQA度量方法多针对自然图像开发和测试。当将这些度量方法应用于医学图像时出现的缺陷和不一致性并不令人意外，因为它们依赖于与自然图像不同的属性。在光声成像（PAI）中，尤其缺乏评估图像重建质量的标准基准方法。PAI是一种多物理成像模态，需解决两个逆问题，这使得IQA度量方法的应用因声学和光学伪影而尤为具有挑战性。为支持IQA度量方法的开发与测试，我们构建了PhotIQA数据集，包含1134张光声图像。这些图像由五位专家在全参考设置下，针对五个质量属性进行评分，详细评分使其可超越PAI应用。该数据集（含图像及对应评分）已公开发布于Zenodo。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of quality-rated medical images for evaluating image quality assessment (IQA) methods, especially in photoacoustic imaging (PAI), the authors created PhotIQA, a dataset of 1134 PA images rated by five experts across five quality dimensions. This full-reference dataset addresses the gap in benchmarking IQA for PAI, where standard natural-image-based metrics fail due to unique acoustic and optical artifacts. Experimental results show that existing IQA measures perform inconsistently on PA images, highlighting the need for domain-specific evaluation tools, and the dataset enables reproducible testing and development of new IQA methods tailored to PAI.
为弥补医学图像领域缺乏质量评分数据以评估图像质量评估（IQA）方法的不足，特别是针对光声成像（PAI）场景，作者构建了PhotIQA数据集，包含1134张由五位专家按五个质量维度评分的PA图像。该全参考数据集填补了PAI领域缺乏标准评估基准的空白，解决了传统自然图像IQA方法因未考虑声学与光学伪影而失效的问题。实验表明，该数据集评分稳定、细节丰富，可有效支持PAI专用IQA方法的开发与验证。
Repetition over Diversity: High-Signal Data Filtering for Sample-Efficient German Language Modeling
Authors: Ansar Aynetdinov, Patrick Haller, Alan Akbik
First: 2026-04-30T16:21:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:21:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent research has shown that filtering massive English web corpora into high-quality subsets significantly improves training efficiency. However, for high-resource non-English languages like German, French, or Japanese, aggressive filtering creates a strategic dilemma: should practitioners prioritize diversity by training once on large amounts of lightly filtered web data, or prioritize quality by strictly filtering for a high-quality core and repeating it over multiple epochs? We investigate this trade-off for German by constructing hierarchical quality filters applied to 500M web documents, comparing multi-epoch training on the filtered subsets against single-pass training on a diverse corpus. Our experiments across multiple model scales and token budgets show that repeating high-quality data consistently outperforms single-pass training on larger, less filtered sets. Notably, the performance gap persists even after 7 epochs. Our findings suggest that for non-English LLMs, semantic concentration through quality filtering offers a more viable path to efficient language modeling than simply maximizing unique data volume. We release our German language models (called Boldt), as well as our cleaned evaluation benchmarks to the research community. Our experiments indicate that they achieve state-of-the-art results despite training on 10-360x fewer tokens than comparable models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重复胜于多样：面向高效德语语言建模的高信号数据过滤
近期研究表明，将海量英文网络语料库过滤为高质量子集可显著提升训练效率。然而，对于德语、法语或日语等资源丰富的非英语语言，激进的过滤策略带来战略抉择：研究者应优先选择多样性，即一次训练大量轻度过滤的网络数据；还是优先选择质量，即严格筛选高质量核心数据并重复训练多轮？我们针对德语构建了应用于5亿网页文档的分层质量过滤器，比较了在过滤子集上多轮训练与在多样化语料库上单次训练的效果。实验表明，无论模型规模或标记预算如何，重复训练高质量数据始终优于在更大但过滤较弱的数据集上单次训练。值得注意的是，即使经过7轮训练，性能差距依然存在。我们的研究结果表明，对于非英语大语言模型，通过质量过滤实现语义集中化，比单纯最大化独特数据量更有利于高效语言建模。我们向研究社区开源了德语语言模型（称为Boldt）及清洗后的评估基准，实验表明，尽管其训练标记量仅为同类模型的10至360倍，仍取得了最先进性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve training efficiency for German language models without sacrificing performance, this study investigates whether repeating high-quality, filtered data yields better results than training once on a larger, less filtered corpus. The authors apply hierarchical quality filters to 500M German web documents and compare multi-epoch training on the filtered subsets against single-pass training on a diverse corpus across multiple model scales and token budgets. Results show that repeating high-quality data consistently outperforms single-pass training, even after seven epochs, and models trained on 10-360x fewer tokens still achieve state-of-the-art performance.
为提升德语语言模型的训练效率而不牺牲性能，本研究探讨重复高质量过滤数据是否优于一次训练于更大但过滤较弱的数据集。作者对5亿德语网页文档应用分层质量过滤器，比较在过滤子集上多轮训练与在多样化数据上单次训练的效果。在多个模型规模和token预算下，重复高质量数据始终优于单次训练，即使经过七轮训练，性能差距仍显著存在。结果表明，对非英语大语言模型而言，通过质量过滤实现语义集中比单纯扩大数据多样性更有效，且发布的模型在训练token量仅为同类模型10至360倍的情况下仍达到最先进水平。
VerteNet -- A Multi-Context Hybrid CNN Transformer for Accurate Vertebral Landmark Localization in Lateral Spine DXA Images
Authors: Arooba Maqsood, Zaid Ilyas, Afsah Saleem, Erchuan Zhang, David Suter, Parminder Raina, Jonathan M. Hodgson, John T. Schousboe, William D. Leslie, Joshua R. Lewis, Syed Zulqarnain Gilani
First: 2025-02-04T08:27:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:20:10+00:00
Comments: 17 pages with 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This aims to develop and validate a deep learning model that can accurately locate vertebral landmarks in lateral spine Dual energy X-ray Absorptiometry (DXA) scans. Accurate vertebral landmark localization is critical for reliable fracture assessment and scoring of abdominal aortic calcification using the Kauppila 24-point method; however, DXA lateral spine images are low-contrast, artifact-prone, and manufacturer-dependent, while manual annotation is time-consuming and reader-dependent. This study aimed to address these challenges by developing a dual-resolution self- and cross-attention model for robust vertebral landmark localization using lateral spine DXA scans from four different scanner models. Ground-truth vertebral corner landmarks (T12 to L5) were manually annotated, and performance was evaluated using normalized mean and median localization errors against baseline and state-of-the-art methods. The proposed framework achieved superior localization accuracy across all four DXA scanner models, with a normalized mean error of 4.92 pixels and a median error of 2.35 pixels, outperforming baseline methods. The abdominal aorta crop detection algorithm achieved 100% accuracy in validation and 96% accuracy (sensitivity 0.93, specificity 0.98) in an independent test set. Generated intervertebral guides further improved inter-reader agreement, reflected by higher Cohens weighted kappa and inter-reader correlation. The proposed deep learning framework enables accurate and robust vertebral landmark localization in lateral spine DXA images across heterogeneous imaging systems to support clinically relevant downstream analyses. The code for this work can be found at: https://github.com/zaidilyas89/VerteNet
中文标题/摘要
标题：VerteNet——一种用于侧位脊柱DXA图像中椎体标志点精准定位的多上下文混合CNN-Transformer模型
本研究旨在开发并验证一种深度学习模型，以精准定位侧位脊柱双能X线吸收法（DXA）扫描中的椎体标志点。精准的椎体标志点定位对骨折评估及采用Kauppila 24点法评估腹主动脉钙化至关重要；然而，DXA侧位脊柱图像对比度低、易受伪影干扰且依赖于设备制造商，而人工标注耗时且依赖阅片者。本研究通过开发一种双分辨率自注意力与交叉注意力模型，解决了上述挑战，该模型基于四种不同扫描仪的侧位脊柱DXA图像实现稳健的椎体标志点定位。人工标注了T12至L5椎体角点的真实标签，并通过归一化均值与中位定位误差与基线及前沿方法进行性能评估。所提框架在四种DXA扫描仪上均实现更优定位精度，归一化均值误差为4.92像素，中位误差为2.35像素，优于基线方法。腹主动脉裁剪检测算法在验证集上准确率达100%，独立测试集上准确率为96%（敏感度0.93，特异度0.98）。生成的椎间隙引导线进一步提升了阅片者间一致性，表现为Cohen加权Kappa值与阅片者间相关性更高。该深度学习框架可支持临床相关下游分析，在异质性成像系统中实现侧位脊柱DXA图像中椎体标志点的精准稳健定位。本研究代码见：https://github.com/zaidilyas89/VerteNet
Summary / 总结
To address the challenges of low-contrast, artifact-prone, and manufacturer-dependent lateral spine DXA images that hinder accurate vertebral landmark localization—critical for fracture assessment and abdominal aortic calcification scoring—the study developed VerteNet, a dual-resolution hybrid CNN-transformer model combining self- and cross-attention mechanisms. Evaluated across four scanner models using manually annotated T12 to L5 vertebral corner landmarks, the model achieved a normalized mean localization error of 4.92 pixels and median error of 2.35 pixels, outperforming baseline methods. Additionally, the aorta detection algorithm achieved 100% validation accuracy and 96% test accuracy with high sensitivity and specificity, while generated intervertebral guides improved inter-reader agreement via higher Cohen’s weighted kappa and correlation.
为解决低对比度、易受伪影干扰且设备依赖性强的侧位脊柱DXA图像导致的椎体标志点定位困难——这对骨折评估和腹主动脉钙化评分至关重要——本研究提出VerteNet模型，该模型结合CNN与Transformer架构，采用双分辨率自注意力与交叉注意力机制。在四类不同设备的图像上，基于人工标注的T12至L5椎体角点训练后，模型实现4.92像素均值误差和2.35像素中位误差，优于基线方法。其腹主动脉检测算法在验证集达100%准确率，独立测试集达96%准确率（敏感度0.93，特异度0.98），生成的椎间隙引导线显著提升阅片者间一致性，表现为更高的Cohen’s加权Kappa值和相关系数。
FP-IRL: Fokker--Planck Inverse Reinforcement Learning -- A Physics-Constrained Approach to Markov Decision Processes
Authors: Chengyang Huang, Siddhartha Srivastava, Kenneth K. Y. Ho, Kathy E. Luker, Gary D. Luker, Xun Huan, Krishna Garikipati
Venue: Computer Methods in Applied Mechanics and Engineering, 458, 119010 (2026)
First: 2023-06-17T18:28:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:19:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) is a powerful paradigm for uncovering the incentive structure that drives agent behavior, by inferring an unknown reward function from observed trajectories within a Markov decision process (MDP). However, most existing IRL methods require access to the transition function, either prescribed or estimated \textit{a priori}, which poses significant challenges when the underlying dynamics are unknown, unobservable, or not easily sampled. We propose Fokker--Planck inverse reinforcement learning (FP-IRL), a novel physics-constrained IRL framework tailored for systems that can be described by Fokker--Planck (FP) dynamics. FP-IRL simultaneously infers both the reward and transition functions directly from trajectory data, without requiring access to sampled transitions. Our method leverages a correspondence between MDPs and the FP equation, linking reward maximization in MDPs with free energy minimization in FP dynamics. This connection enables inference of the FP potential function using our inference approach of variational system identification, from which the full set of MDP components -- reward, transition, and policy -- can be recovered using analytic expressions. We demonstrate the effectiveness of FP-IRL through experiments on synthetic benchmarks and a modified version of the Mountain Car problem. Our results show that FP-IRL achieves accurate recovery of agent incentives while preserving computational efficiency and physical interpretability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FP-IRL：福克-普朗克逆强化学习——一种面向马尔可夫决策过程的物理约束方法
逆强化学习（IRL）是一种通过从马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中观测到的轨迹推断未知奖励函数，从而揭示驱动智能体行为的激励结构的强大范式。然而，大多数现有IRL方法需要事先获得或估计转移函数，这在底层动力学未知、不可观测或难以采样时面临重大挑战。 我们提出福克-普朗克逆强化学习（FP-IRL），一种专为可由福克-普朗克（FP）动力学描述的系统设计的新型物理约束IRL框架。FP-IRL可直接从轨迹数据同时推断奖励与转移函数，无需访问采样转移。该方法利用MDP与FP方程之间的对应关系，将MDP中的奖励最大化与FP动力学中的自由能最小化联系起来。这一联系使得我们能够通过变分系统识别方法推断FP势函数，进而利用解析表达式恢复完整的MDP组件——奖励、转移与策略。 我们在合成基准和修改版山车问题上展示了FP-IRL的有效性。结果表明，FP-IRL在准确恢复智能体激励的同时，保持了计算效率和物理可解释性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of inferring reward functions in Markov decision processes without access to transition dynamics, the paper introduces FP-IRL, a physics-constrained inverse reinforcement learning method tailored for systems governed by Fokker--Planck dynamics. It simultaneously learns both reward and transition functions from trajectory data using variational system identification, leveraging the mathematical correspondence between MDPs and FP equations that links reward maximization to free energy minimization. Experimental results on synthetic benchmarks and a modified Mountain Car problem demonstrate that FP-IRL accurately recovers agent incentives while maintaining computational efficiency and physical interpretability, without requiring sampled transitions.
为了解决传统逆强化学习方法依赖先验过渡函数而难以应对未知或不可观测动态的问题，本文提出FP-IRL方法，通过轨迹数据直接联合推断奖励与过渡函数。该方法利用马尔可夫决策过程与福克-普朗克方程之间的数学对应关系，将奖励最大化转化为自由能最小化，借助变分系统识别技术恢复福克-普朗克势函数，再通过解析表达式重构完整MDP组件。在合成基准与修改版山地车问题上的实验表明，FP-IRL能准确恢复代理激励，同时保持计算效率与物理可解释性。
A Unified Framework of Hyperbolic Graph Representation Learning Methods
Authors: Sofía Pérez Casulo, Marcelo Fiori, Bernardo Marenco, Federico Larroca
First: 2026-04-30T16:17:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:17:53+00:00
Comments: submitted
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Hyperbolic geometry has emerged as an effective latent space for representing complex networks, owing to its ability to capture hierarchical organization and heterogeneous connectivity patterns using low-dimensional embeddings. As a result, numerous hyperbolic graph representation learning methods have been proposed in recent years. However, their practical adoption and systematic comparison remain challenging, as implementations are fragmented and shared tools for reproducible and fair evaluation are lacking. In this work, we introduce a unified open-source framework for hyperbolic graph representation learning that integrates several widely used embedding methods under a common optimization interface. The novel framework enables consistent training, visualization, and evaluation of hyperbolic embeddings, and interfaces seamlessly with standard network analysis tools. Leveraging this unified setup, we conduct an experimental study of hyperbolic embedding methods on real-world networks, focusing on two canonical downstream tasks: link prediction and node classification. Beyond predictive accuracy, the study offers practical insights into the strengths and limitations of existing approaches, thereby facilitating informed method selection and fostering reproducible research in hyperbolic graph representation learning.

为解决现有超球面图表示学习方法缺乏统一评估和实际应用的问题，本文提出一个统一的开源框架，将多种主流嵌入方法整合到统一的优化接口下，支持一致的训练、可视化与评估，并无缝对接标准网络分析工具。在真实网络数据上的实验（基于链接预测与节点分类任务）表明不同方法表现各异，揭示了各自优势与局限，为方法选择提供依据，同时推动该领域研究的可复现性。
Assessing the Role of Intersection Proximity in Pedestrian Crashes: Insights from Data Mining Approach
Authors: Ahmed Hossain, Xiaoduan Sun, Subasish Das
First: 2026-04-30T16:12:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:12:13+00:00
Comments: 59 pages, 14 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Although intersections are the most complex parts of the roadway network, pedestrian crashes at non-intersection locations are disproportionately frequent, highlighting a serious traffic safety concern. This study investigates non-intersection crashes involving pedestrians using a crash database (2017-2021) collected from Louisiana State. As the risk of pedestrian crashes tends to vary with distance from the intersection, the research team utilized a unique framework "distance to intersection" to capture the differences in crash patterns at non-intersection locations. The study identified that around 50% of non-intersection pedestrian crashes occurred within 198 ft. of the intersection. In the next step, the collected 3,135 pedestrian crashes at non-intersection locations during the study period were subdivided into three zones: D1 zone designates crashes occurring within 150 ft. of an intersection (1,277 crashes), D2 zone designates crashes occurring within 151 ft. to 435 ft. of an intersection (1,060 crashes) and D3 zone designates crashes occurring at 435 ft. or higher from an intersection (798 crashes). To explore the complex interaction of multiple factors, an intuitive data mining technique, Association Rules Mining was used. A total of the top 60 interesting association rules (20 for each zone) were identified by the algorithm (based on lift and support measures). In addition, a total of 124 rules were explored based on Lift Increase Criterion (LIC) measure. The findings of this research provide critical insights into pedestrian crash involvement at non-intersection locations and the variation in crash patterns according to the "distance to intersection". Based on the findings, some of the targeted problem-specific countermeasures are also recommended to address the crash patterns at non-intersection locations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：交叉口邻近性在行人事故中的作用评估：基于数据挖掘方法的见解
尽管交叉口是道路网络中最复杂的部分，但非交叉口位置的行人事故却异常频繁，凸显了严重的交通安全问题。本研究利用路易斯安那州收集的2017-2021年事故数据库，调查非交叉口涉及行人的事故。由于行人事故风险随与交叉口距离变化，研究团队采用“至交叉口距离”这一独特框架，捕捉非交叉口位置事故模式的差异。研究发现，约50%的非交叉口行人事故发生在距交叉口198英尺内。随后，研究将该期间收集的3,135起非交叉口行人事故划分为三个区域：D1区指距交叉口150英尺内事故（1,277起），D2区指距交叉口151至435英尺事故（1,060起），D3区指距交叉口435英尺及以上事故（798起）。为探索多重因素的复杂交互作用，研究采用直观的数据挖掘技术——关联规则挖掘。算法基于提升度和支撑度指标识别出各区域前60条有趣关联规则（每区20条）。此外，基于提升度增加准则（LIC）共探索124条规则。本研究结果为非交叉口行人事故参与情况及“至交叉口距离”对事故模式的影响提供了关键见解，并据此提出针对性的事故防控对策。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the underrecognized safety risk of pedestrian crashes occurring away from intersections, despite intersections being the most complex roadway elements. Using Louisiana’s 2017–2021 crash data, researchers segmented non-intersection pedestrian crashes into three distance-based zones (D1: 0–150 ft, D2: 151–435 ft, D3: 435+ ft) and applied Association Rules Mining to uncover patterns. Results showed that 50% of such crashes occurred within 198 ft of an intersection, with 60 top association rules (20 per zone) identified via lift and support metrics, and 124 additional rules using Lift Increase Criterion. The findings reveal how crash risk varies spatially near intersections and suggest targeted countermeasures for high-risk zones.
该研究针对尽管交叉口复杂但行人事故常发生在非交叉口区域这一安全问题，利用路易斯安那州2017–2021年的事故数据，引入“距交叉口距离”框架，将事故划分为三个区域（D1：0–150英尺，D2：151–435英尺，D3：435英尺以上），发现50%的非交叉口行人事故发生在198英尺内。采用关联规则挖掘技术，按提升度和支持度筛选出60条高价值规则（每区20条），并依据提升度增加标准筛选出124条规则。结果揭示不同距离区域的事故风险因素差异，为制定针对性干预措施提供依据。
Decomposed Trust: Privacy, Adversarial Robustness, Ethics, and Fairness in Low-Rank LLMs
Authors: Daniel Agyei Asante, Md Mokarram Chowdhury, Yang Li
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-11-27T04:40:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:12:10+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) have driven major advances across domains, yet their massive size hinders deployment in resource-constrained settings. Low-rank factorization addresses this challenge by compressing models to effectively reduce their computation and memory consumption while maintaining accuracy. While these compressed models boast benign performance and system-level advantages, their trustworthiness implications remain poorly understood. In this paper, we present the first comprehensive study of how low-rank factorization affects LLM trustworthiness across privacy, adversarial robustness, ethics, and fairness, complemented by an explainability-driven analysis of the internal mechanisms behind these trust-related changes. We evaluate multiple LLMs of different sizes and architectures compressed with various low-rank factorization algorithms, revealing key insights: (1) low-rank factorization preserves training data privacy but weakens the protection of personally identifiable information during conversations; (2) adversarial robustness is generally enhanced under compression; (3) ethics degrades in zero-shot prompting but partially recovers in few-shot prompting; (4) fairness declines under compression. Beyond compression, we investigate how model scale and fine-tuning affect trustworthiness. Additionally, to move beyond black-box analysis, we employ a gradient-based attribution to identify which layers of LLMs contribute most to adversarial robustness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：低秩LLM中的信任分解：隐私、对抗鲁棒性、伦理与公平性
大语言模型（LLM）在多个领域推动了重大进展，但其庞大的规模阻碍了在资源受限环境中的部署。低秩因子分解通过压缩模型有效降低计算和内存消耗，同时保持精度。尽管这些压缩模型表现出良好的性能和系统级优势，其可信性影响仍不甚明了。本文首次全面研究了低秩因子分解如何影响LLM在隐私、对抗鲁棒性、伦理与公平性方面的可信性，并辅以可解释性分析，揭示其内部机制。 我们评估了多种不同规模和架构的LLM，使用不同低秩因子分解算法压缩后，得出关键发现：（1）低秩因子分解保护训练数据隐私，但削弱了对话中个人身份信息的保护；（2）压缩通常增强对抗鲁棒性；（3）伦理在零样本提示下退化，但在少样本提示下部分恢复；（4）公平性在压缩下下降。此外，我们还研究了模型规模和微调对可信性的影响。为超越黑盒分析，我们采用基于梯度的归因方法，识别LLM中对对抗鲁棒性贡献最大的层。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to deploy large language models in resource-limited environments, this study investigates how low-rank factorization impacts trustworthiness across privacy, adversarial robustness, ethics, and fairness. The authors compress multiple LLMs using various low-rank methods and evaluate their behavior across these dimensions, finding that while privacy is preserved during training, personal data protection in conversations weakens; adversarial robustness generally improves; ethics degrade in zero-shot settings but partially recover with few-shot prompting; and fairness declines. The study also examines how model scale and fine-tuning influence trustworthiness and uses gradient-based attribution to identify which model layers contribute most to adversarial robustness.
为应对大语言模型在资源受限环境中的部署难题，本研究探讨低秩分解压缩技术对模型可信度（隐私、对抗鲁棒性、伦理与公平性）的影响。作者评估了多种架构和规模的模型在不同压缩算法下的表现，发现训练数据隐私得以保留，但对话中个人身份信息保护减弱；对抗鲁棒性普遍增强；伦理在零样本提示下下降，但在少样本提示下部分恢复；公平性则普遍降低。研究还考察了模型规模与微调对可信度的影响，并通过梯度归因方法识别了对对抗鲁棒性贡献最大的模型层。
3D Reconstruction Techniques in the Manufacturing Domain: Applications, Research Opportunities and Use Cases
Authors: Chialoon Cheng, Kaijun liu, Zhiyang Liu, Marcelo H Ang
First: 2026-04-30T16:11:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:11:02+00:00
Comments: 24 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This comprehensive review examines the evolution and the current state of the art in three-dimensional (3D) reconstruction techniques in manufacturing applications. The analysis covers both traditional approaches and emerging deep learning methods, showing a critical research gap in unified 3d reconstruction frameworks. Through systematic review of 106 recent publications, we classify reconstruction techniques into three primary categories: data acquisition, point cloud generation, post-processing and applications. Non-contact methods, particularly structured light scanning and stereo vision, have shown significant adoption in manufacturing, with 47% of surveyed applications focusing on quality inspection. The integration of deep learning has enhanced reconstruction accuracy and processing speed, particularly in feature extraction and matching. Key applications span design and development (13%), machining (8%), process (17%), assembly (22%), and quality inspection (40%). While current technologies achieve sub-millimeter accuracy in controlled environments, challenges persist in handling reflective surfaces and dynamic environments. Our findings indicate a trend toward hybrid systems combining multiple sensor types and processing methods to overcome individual limitations. This survey provides a structured framework for understanding current capabilities and future directions in manufacturing-focused 3D reconstruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：制造领域三维重建技术：应用、研究机会与用例
本综述全面考察了制造应用中三维（3D）重建技术的演变及其当前研究前沿。分析涵盖传统方法与新兴深度学习方法，揭示统一3D重建框架存在关键研究缺口。通过对106篇近期文献的系统回顾，我们将重建技术划分为三大类：数据采集、点云生成及后处理与应用。非接触式方法，特别是结构光扫描与立体视觉，在制造领域应用广泛，47%的调研应用聚焦于质量检测。深度学习的融合显著提升了重建精度与处理速度，尤其在特征提取与匹配方面。主要应用涵盖设计开发（13%）、加工（8%）、工艺（17%）、装配（22%）及质量检测（40%）。当前技术在受控环境下可实现亚毫米级精度，但在处理反射表面与动态环境方面仍存挑战。研究发现，融合多种传感器与处理方法的混合系统正成为趋势，以克服单一方法的局限。本综述为理解制造导向3D重建的当前能力与未来方向提供了结构化框架。

为应对制造业中对高精度三维重建的需求，本文综述了106篇近期文献，梳理技术分类与研究空白。将方法分为数据采集、点云生成与后处理，指出结构光与立体视觉为主流非接触技术。深度学习的引入显著提升重建精度与速度，尤其在特征提取方面。实证分析显示，质量检测应用占比最高（40%），其次为装配（22%）和工艺（17%），在受控环境下可实现亚毫米级精度，但在反光表面与动态环境中仍存挑战。研究认为，融合多传感器与处理方法的混合系统是突破当前局限的重要趋势。
Measuring research data reuse in scholarly publications using generative artificial intelligence: Open Science Indicator development and preliminary results
Authors: Lauren Cadwallader, Iain Hrynaszkiewicz, parth sarin, Tim Vines
First: 2026-04-30T16:07:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:07:43+00:00
Comments: 12 pages. Submitted to 30th Annual International Conference on Science and Technology Indicators
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Numerous metascience studies and other initiatives have begun to monitor the prevalence of open science practices when it is more important to understand the 'downstream' effects or impacts of open science. PLOS and DataSeer have developed a new LLM-based indicator to measure an important effect of open science: the reuse of research data. Our results show a data reuse rate of 43%, which is higher than established bibliometric techniques. We show that data reuse can be measured at scale using LLMs and generative artificial intelligence. The positive effects of research data sharing and reuse may currently be underestimated.

K2MUSE: A human lower-limb multimodal walking dataset spanning task and acquisition variability for rehabilitation robotics
Authors: Jiwei Li, Bi Zhang, Xiaowei Tan, Wanxin Chen, Zhaoyuan Liu, Juanjuan Zhang, Weiguang Huo, Jian Huang, Lianqing Liu, Xingang Zhao
First: 2025-04-20T13:03:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:06:30+00:00
Comments: 34 pages, 30 figures,7 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The natural interaction and control performance of lower limb rehabilitation robots are closely linked to biomechanical information from various human locomotion activities. Multidimensional human motion data significantly deepen the understanding of the complex mechanisms governing neuromuscular alterations, thereby facilitating the development and application of rehabilitation robots in multifaceted real-world environments.However, existing lower limb datasets are inadequate for supplying the essential multimodal data and large-scale gait samples necessary for the development of effective data-driven approaches, and the significant effects of acquisition interference in real applications are neglected.To fill this gap, we present the K2MUSE dataset, which includes a comprehensive collection of multimodal data, comprising kinematic, kinetic, amplitude mode ultrasound (AUS), and surface electromyography (sEMG) measurements. The proposed dataset includes lower-limb multimodal data collected from two cohorts, including 30 able-bodied young adults and 12 older adults, across different inclines (0$^\circ$, $\pm$5$^\circ$, and $\pm$10$^\circ$), speeds (0.5 m/s, 1.0 m/s, and 1.5 m/s), and representative non-ideal acquisition conditions (muscle fatigue, electrode shifts, and interday differences). The kinematic and ground reaction force data were collected with a Vicon motion capture system and an instrumented treadmill with embedded force plates, whereas the sEMG and AUS data of thirteen muscles on the bilateral lower limbs were synchronously recorded.K2MUSE is released with the corresponding structured documentation, preprocessing pipelines, and example code, thereby providing a comprehensive resource for rehabilitation robot development, biomechanical analysis, and wearable sensing research. The dataset is available at https://k2muse.github.io/.

标题：K2MUSE：一个涵盖任务与采集变异性的下肢多模态步行数据集，用于康复机器人
下肢康复机器人的自然交互与控制性能与人类多种步行活动的生物力学信息密切相关。多维人体运动数据显著加深了对调控神经肌肉变化的复杂机制的理解，从而促进康复机器人在多场景真实环境中的开发与应用。然而，现有下肢数据集难以提供有效数据驱动方法所需的多模态数据和大规模步态样本，且忽视了实际应用中采集干扰的显著影响。为填补这一空白，我们提出了K2MUSE数据集，包含全面的多模态数据，涵盖运动学、动力学、振幅模式超声（AUS）和表面肌电图（sEMG）测量。该数据集包含两组受试者（30名健康年轻成人和12名老年人）在不同坡度（0°、±5°、±10°）、速度（0.5 m/s、1.0 m/s、1.5 m/s）及代表性非理想采集条件（肌肉疲劳、电极移位、日间差异）下的下肢多模态数据。运动学和地面反作用力数据由Vicon动作捕捉系统与嵌入式力板的仪器化跑步机采集，而双侧下肢十三块肌肉的sEMG和AUS数据同步记录。K2MUSE数据集附带结构化文档、预处理流程及示例代码，为康复机器人开发、生物力学分析和可穿戴传感研究提供全面资源。数据集可访问：https://k2muse.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
To address the lack of multimodal, large-scale gait data for rehabilitation robotics and the neglect of real-world acquisition variability, the authors introduce K2MUSE, a comprehensive dataset capturing kinematic, kinetic, amplitude mode ultrasound, and surface EMG signals from 42 participants across diverse walking conditions including inclines, speeds, and non-ideal sensor conditions like muscle fatigue and electrode shifts. Data was collected using a Vicon motion capture system and instrumented treadmill, with synchronized recordings of 13 bilateral lower-limb muscles. The dataset, released with structured documentation and preprocessing tools, enables robust development of data-driven rehabilitation robots and biomechanical models under realistic variability.
为解决康复机器人开发中缺乏多模态、大规模步态数据及忽视实际采集干扰的问题，作者构建了K2MUSE数据集，涵盖30名健康青年与12名老年受试者在不同坡度、速度及肌肉疲劳、电极偏移等非理想条件下的运动数据。数据通过Vicon动作捕捉系统与嵌入式力板跑台采集，同步记录13对下肢肌肉的sEMG与AUS信号。该数据集支持在真实干扰环境下建模神经肌肉动态，助力康复机器人与可穿戴传感技术研究。
Framework for Collaborative Operation of Autonomous Delivery Vehicles Within a Marshaling Yard
Authors: James O'Hara, Karl Wunderlich, Gregory Stevens
First: 2026-04-30T16:02:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:02:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As autonomous vehicles slowly deploy into urban roads for limited use cases with significant edge case issues, closed facilities like marshaling yards provide a ripe case for combining lower-level vehicle autonomy with fixed infrastructure to create full autonomy without similar edge case concerns. Within a delivery marshaling yard, electric fleet vehicles complete a set of sequential tasks (charging, inspection, cleaning, and loading) before exiting the yard with their new load of deliveries. Hybrid automation of the vehicles and infrastructure can allow these vehicles to reach full autonomy and navigate the facility without the need of a driver, allowing for quicker movement between tasks increasing vehicle throughput. However, isolated autonomous operations based on static rules are prone to gridlock causing facility failures that temporarily shut down operations. Our orchestrated autonomy solution uses decentralized, dynamic priority scoring of vehicles based on the current status of the marshaling yard to optimally assign vehicles to tasks to increase vehicle throughput. Using a simulated facility with three marshaling yard sizes (small, medium, and large) and three demand levels (low, medium, high), we demonstrated that our orchestration solution increases vehicle throughput above static, isolated autonomy for all combinations of yard size and demand, while reducing facility failures at high demand levels.
中文标题/摘要
标题：编组站内自动驾驶配送车辆协同作业框架
随着自动驾驶车辆逐步在城市道路有限场景中部署并面临大量边缘案例问题，封闭设施如编组站为结合车辆低级自主性与固定基础设施，实现无类似边缘案例担忧的完全自主性提供了理想场景。在配送编组站内，电动车队车辆需依次完成充电、检查、清洁和装载等任务后，携新一批配送货物驶离站场。车辆与基础设施的混合自动化可使车辆实现完全自主，无需驾驶员即可在站内导航，从而加快任务间移动速度，提升车辆吞吐量。然而，仅依赖静态规则的孤立自动驾驶易导致拥堵，引发设施故障并暂时中断运营。我们的协同自主解决方案采用基于编组站当前状态的分布式动态优先级评分机制，最优分配车辆至任务，以提升车辆吞吐量。通过模拟包含三种站场规模（小、中、大）和三种需求水平（低、中、高）的设施，我们证明该协同方案在所有站场规模与需求组合下均优于静态孤立自动驾驶，同时在高需求水平下显著降低设施故障率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to achieve full autonomy for delivery vehicles in controlled environments without edge case risks, this study proposes a decentralized orchestration framework that dynamically assigns tasks to autonomous vehicles in a marshaling yard based on real-time facility status. The method uses priority scoring to coordinate vehicle movements across sequential tasks like charging and loading, avoiding gridlock that plagues static rule-based systems. Experimental results in a simulated environment with varying yard sizes and demand levels show that the proposed system consistently outperforms static autonomy, increasing vehicle throughput across all scenarios and reducing facility failures under high demand.
为在无边缘案例风险的封闭环境中实现配送车辆的全自动驾驶，本研究提出一种基于实时状态动态分配任务的分布式协同框架。该方法根据场地当前状况动态调整车辆优先级，以优化任务分配并避免拥堵。实验结果表明，在不同规模场地和需求水平下，该框架均优于静态独立自动驾驶方案，显著提升车辆吞吐量并降低高需求下的设施故障率。
RHyVE: Competence-Aware Verification and Phase-Aware Deployment for LLM-Generated Reward Hypotheses
Authors: Feiyu Wu, Xu Zheng, Zhuocheng Wang, Yi ming Dai, Hui Li
First: 2026-04-30T16:01:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:01:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) make reward design in reinforcement learning substantially more scalable, but generated rewards are not automatically reliable training objectives. Existing work has focused primarily on generating, evolving, or selecting reward candidates, while paying less attention to when such candidates can be verified and deployed during policy optimization. We study this deployment-time problem by treating generated rewards as reward hypotheses whose utility depends on the competence of the current policy and the phase of training. We propose \textsc{RHyVE}, a competence-aware verification and phase-aware deployment protocol that compares small sets of reward hypotheses from shared policy checkpoints using short-horizon fork verification. Our experiments show that reward rankings are unreliable at low competence but become informative after task-dependent thresholds. On a sparse manipulation task, phase-aware deployment improves peak and retained performance under a locked protocol. Updated LLM-generated reward-candidate experiments show candidate-family-dependent behavior: generated pools can exhibit phase-dependent winner changes, but no fixed warm-up schedule is universally optimal. Held-out schedule selection, conservative selector baselines, compute-matched controls, and scale controls further show that \textsc{RHyVE} is best understood as a verification-informed deployment protocol rather than a universal scheduler. Dense and all-failure boundary experiments delimit the scope of the method. Together, these results suggest that reward generation and reward deployment should be studied as coupled problems: generated rewards must be verified and deployed under changing policy competence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RHyVE：面向LLM生成奖励假设的胜任力感知验证与阶段感知部署
大语言模型（LLMs）使强化学习中的奖励设计显著更具可扩展性，但生成的奖励并非自动可靠的学习目标。现有工作主要关注生成、演化或选择奖励候选，而较少关注这些候选在策略优化过程中何时可被验证和部署。我们通过将生成奖励视为依赖于当前策略胜任力和训练阶段的奖励假设，研究这一部署时机问题。我们提出RHyVE，一种胜任力感知验证与阶段感知部署协议，通过短时程分叉验证比较共享策略检查点中的少量奖励假设。实验表明，低胜任力时奖励排序不可靠，但达到任务相关阈值后变得具有信息量。在稀疏操作任务中，阶段感知部署在锁定协议下提升了峰值与保留性能。更新的LLM生成奖励候选实验显示候选族依赖行为：生成池可呈现阶段依赖的胜者变化，但无普适最优预热方案。保留调度选择、保守选择器基线、计算匹配对照与规模对照进一步表明，RHyVE更宜理解为验证引导的部署协议而非通用调度器。密集与全失败边界实验限定了方法适用范围。综合结果表明，奖励生成与部署应作为耦合问题研究：生成奖励必须在策略胜任力变化时进行验证与部署。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reliably deploy LLM-generated reward hypotheses during reinforcement learning, the authors propose RHyVE, a protocol that verifies and deploys rewards based on current policy competence and training phase. RHyVE uses short-horizon fork verification to compare small reward sets from shared policy checkpoints, enabling phase-aware deployment. Experiments show reward rankings are unreliable early in training but become informative after task-specific thresholds; phase-aware deployment improves peak and retained performance in sparse manipulation tasks. Results also reveal that reward candidate families behave differently across phases, with no universal warm-up schedule, and that RHyVE functions best as a verification-informed deployment tool rather than a fixed scheduler, especially under dense or all-failure conditions.
为解决LLM生成奖励在强化学习中部署不可靠的问题，作者提出RHyVE协议，根据当前策略能力与训练阶段动态验证并部署奖励。该方法通过短时程分叉验证比较共享策略检查点中的小规模奖励候选集，实现阶段感知部署。实验表明，奖励排序在能力较低时不可靠，但达到任务相关阈值后变得有效；阶段感知部署显著提升稀疏操控任务中的峰值与保留性能。此外，奖励候选集存在家族依赖性，无普适预热方案，而留出调度与保守基线验证表明RHyVE本质是验证驱动的部署协议，而非通用调度器。
PROMISE-AD: Progression-aware Multi-horizon Survival Estimation for Alzheimer's Disease Progression and Dynamic Tracking
Authors: Qing Lyu, Jeremy Hudson, Mohammad Kawas, Yuming Jiang, Chenyu You, Christopher T Whitlow
First: 2026-04-30T16:01:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:01:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Individualized Alzheimer's disease (AD) progression prediction requires models that use irregular visits, account for censoring, avoid diagnostic leakage, and provide calibrated horizon risks. We propose PROgression-aware MultI-horizon Survival Estimation for Alzheimer's Disease (PROMISE-AD), a leakage-safe survival framework for predicting conversion from cognitively normal (CN) to mild cognitive impairment (MCI) and from MCI to AD dementia using ADNI/TADPOLE tabular histories. PROMISE-AD converts pre-index visits into tokens with standardized measurements, missingness masks, longitudinal changes, time-normalized slopes, visit timing, and non-diagnostic categorical attributes. A temporal Transformer fuses global, attention-pooled, and latest-visit representations to estimate a progression score and latent discrete-time mixture hazards. Training combines survival likelihood, horizon-specific focal risk loss, progression ranking, hazard smoothness, and mixture-balance regularization, followed by validation-set isotonic calibration for 1-, 2-, 3-, and 5-year risks. In held-out testing across three seeds, PROMISE-AD achieved an integrated Brier score (IBS) of 0.085 $\pm$ 0.012, C-index of 0.808 $\pm$ 0.015, and mean time-dependent AUC of 0.840 $\pm$ 0.081 for CN-to-MCI conversion, yielding the lowest IBS among compared methods. For MCI-to-AD conversion, PROMISE-AD achieved the highest C-index (0.894 $\pm$ 0.018) and near-ceiling 5-year discrimination (AUROC 0.997 $\pm$ 0.003; AUPRC 0.999 $\pm$ 0.001), although some baselines had lower IBS. Ablations and interpretability supported longitudinal change features, fused temporal representations, mixture hazards, cognitive and functional measures, APOE4 status, and recent conversion-proximal visits. These findings suggest that progression-aware survival modeling can provide interpretable multi-horizon AD conversion risk estimates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PROMISE-AD：面向阿尔茨海默病进展与动态追踪的进展感知多时程生存估计
个体化阿尔茨海默病（AD）进展预测需要能处理不规则就诊、考虑删失、避免诊断泄漏并提供校准时程风险的模型。我们提出PROMISE-AD（面向阿尔茨海默病的进展感知多时程生存估计），一种安全的生存框架，用于基于ADNI/TADPOLE表格历史数据预测从认知正常（CN）到轻度认知障碍（MCI）及从MCI到AD痴呆的转化。PROMISE-AD将预索引就诊转化为包含标准化测量、缺失掩码、纵向变化、时间标准化斜率、就诊时间及非诊断性分类属性的令牌。一个时序Transformer融合全局、注意力池化及最新就诊表征，以估计进展评分和潜在离散时间混合风险。训练结合生存似然、时程特异性焦点风险损失、进展排序、风险平滑性及混合平衡正则化，并通过验证集等距校准获得1、2、3、5年风险。在三个种子的独立测试中，PROMISE-AD在CN到MCI转化中取得综合Brier分数（IBS）0.085±0.012、C指数0.808±0.015、平均时变AUC 0.840±0.081，为对比方法中最低IBS。在MCI到AD转化中，PROMISE-AD取得最高C指数（0.894±0.018）及接近天花板的5年鉴别能力（AUROC 0.997±0.003；AUPRC 0.999±0.001），尽管部分基线方法IBS更低。消融实验与可解释性分析支持纵向变化特征、融合时序表征、混合风险、认知与功能指标、APOE4状态及近期转化临近就诊。这些发现表明，进展感知的生存建模可提供可解释的多时程AD转化风险估计。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for individualized Alzheimer's disease progression prediction using irregular clinical visits and handling censoring without diagnostic leakage, PROMISE-AD introduces a leakage-safe survival framework that encodes pre-index visits into structured tokens capturing longitudinal changes and visit timing. It employs a temporal Transformer to fuse global, attention-pooled, and latest-visit representations to estimate a progression score and discrete-time mixture hazards. Trained with survival likelihood, focal risk loss, ranking, and regularization, the model is calibrated on validation sets for 1- to 5-year risks. 为解决阿尔茨海默病个体化进展预测中因不规则随访、删失数据及诊断泄露带来的挑战，PROMISE-AD提出一种防泄露的生存建模框架，将预索引访视编码为包含纵向变化、时间标准化斜率和访视时间的结构化令牌。该框架采用时序Transformer融合全局、注意力池化及最新访视表征，估计进展评分与离散时间混合风险。通过结合生存似然、焦点风险损失、排序、平滑性及混合平衡正则化，并在验证集上进行等度校准，PROMISE-AD在CN到MCI转化中取得最低综合Brier分数（0.085±0.012）和最高C指数（0.808±0.015），在MCI到AD转化中实现最高C指数（0.894±0.018）及接近天花板的鉴别能力（AUROC 0.997±0.003）
To Build or Not to Build? Factors that Lead to Non-Development or Abandonment of AI Systems
Authors: Shreya Chappidi, Jatinder Singh
First: 2026-04-30T16:00:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T16:00:52+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACM FAccT 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Responsible AI research typically focuses on examining the use and impacts of deployed AI systems. Yet, there is currently limited visibility into the pre-deployment decisions to pursue building such systems in the first place. Decisions taken in the earlier stages of development shape which systems are ultimately released, and therefore represent potential, but underexplored, points for intervention. As such, this paper investigates factors influencing AI non-development and abandonment throughout the development lifecycle. Specifically, we first perform a scoping review of academic literature, civil society resources, and grey literature including journalism and industry reports. Through thematic analysis of these sources, we develop a taxonomy of six categories of factors contributing to AI abandonment: ethical concerns, stakeholder feedback, development lifecycle challenges, organizational dynamics, resource constraints, and legal/regulatory concerns. Then, we collect data on real-world case of AI system abandonment via an AI incident database and a practitioner survey to evidence and compare factors that drive abandonment both prior to and following system deployment. While academic responsible AI communities often emphasize ethical risks as reasons to not develop AI, our empirical analysis of these cases demonstrates the diverse, and often non-ethics-related, levers that motivate organizations to abandon AI development. Synthesizing evidence from our taxonomy and related case study analyses, we identify gaps and opportunities in current responsible AI research to (1) engage with the diverse range of levers that influence organizations to abandon AI development, and (2) better support appropriate (dis)engagement with AI system development.
中文标题/摘要
标题：构建还是不构建？导致人工智能系统未开发或被放弃的因素
负责任的人工智能研究通常聚焦于已部署系统使用与影响的分析，但目前对决定是否启动此类系统开发的前期决策缺乏可见性。早期开发阶段的决策塑造了最终发布系统的形态，因此代表了潜在但尚未充分探索的干预点。本文研究了人工智能系统在开发生命周期中未开发或被放弃的影响因素。首先，我们对学术文献、民间社会资源及灰色文献（包括新闻报道和行业报告）进行范围综述，通过主题分析构建了六类导致人工智能项目被放弃的因素分类：伦理关切、利益相关者反馈、开发周期挑战、组织动态、资源限制及法律/监管顾虑。随后，我们通过人工智能事件数据库和从业者调查收集真实案例数据，以实证比较系统部署前后导致放弃的不同因素。尽管学术界负责任AI研究常强调伦理风险是不开发AI的原因，但我们的实证分析表明，促使组织放弃AI开发的动因多样，且常与伦理无关。综合分类体系及相关案例研究证据，我们识别出当前负责任AI研究中的空白与机遇：（1）需关注影响组织放弃AI开发的多元动因；（2）需更好地支持组织对AI系统开发的适当参与或退出。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the underexplored pre-deployment phase of AI development by investigating why organizations choose not to build or abandon AI systems. Through a scoping review and thematic analysis of academic, civil society, and industry sources, the authors construct a taxonomy of six key factors: ethical concerns, stakeholder feedback, development lifecycle challenges, organizational dynamics, resource constraints, and legal/regulatory issues. They then validate these categories using real-world case data from an AI incident database and practitioner surveys, revealing that while ethics are often assumed to be the primary driver, abandonment is frequently motivated by practical, organizational, or resource-related factors. The study concludes that responsible AI research should broaden its focus beyond ethics to include these diverse levers to better guide appropriate engagement or disengagement with AI development.
本文探讨了组织为何选择不开发或放弃AI系统这一被忽视的前期决策阶段。通过文献综述与主题分析，研究者构建了六个关键影响因素分类：伦理顾虑、利益相关者反馈、开发周期挑战、组织动态、资源限制及法律监管问题。结合AI事件数据库与从业者调查数据，研究发现尽管学术界常强调伦理风险，但实际放弃开发多源于非伦理因素，如资源不足或组织协调问题。研究呼吁负责任AI研究应拓宽视野，纳入这些多元驱动因素，以更有效地支持AI开发的参与或退出决策。
Agent-Agnostic Evaluation of SQL Accuracy in Production Text-to-SQL Systems
Authors: Taslim Jamal Arif, Kuldeep Singh
First: 2026-04-30T15:59:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:59:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-to-SQL (T2SQL) evaluation in production environments poses fundamental challenges that existing benchmarks do not address. Current evaluation methodologies whether rule-based SQL matching or schema-dependent semantic parsers assume access to ground-truth queries and structured database schema, constraints that are rarely satisfied in real-world deployments. This disconnect leaves production T2SQL agents largely unevaluated beyond developer-time testing, creating silent quality degradation with no feedback mechanism for continuous improvement. We present STEF (Schema-agnostic Text-to-SQL Evaluation Framework), a production-native evaluation system that operates exclusively on natural language inputs the user question, an enriched reformulation, and the generated SQL without requiring database schema or reference queries. STEF extracts semantic specifications from both natural language and SQL representations, performs normalized feature alignment, and produces an interpretable 0 to 100 accuracy score via a composite metric that encompasses filter alignment, semantic verdict, and confidence of the evaluator. Key contributions include: enriched question quality validation as a first-class evaluation signal, configurable application-specific rule injection via prompt templating, and production-robust normalization handling GROUP BY tolerance, ORDER BY defaults, and LIMIT heuristics. Empirical results demonstrate that STEF enables continuous production monitoring and agent improvement feedback loops without schema dependency, making structured query evaluation viable at scale for the first time.
中文标题/摘要
标题：生产环境中SQL准确性的代理无关评估
生产环境中文本到SQL（T2SQL）的评估面临现有基准未解决的基本挑战。当前评估方法，无论是基于规则的SQL匹配还是依赖于数据库结构的语义解析器，均假设可访问真实查询和结构化数据库模式，而这些条件在实际部署中很少满足。这种脱节导致生产T2SQL代理在开发阶段测试之外几乎未被评估，从而造成质量无声退化且缺乏持续改进的反馈机制。我们提出STEF（无模式依赖的文本到SQL评估框架），这是一个原生适用于生产环境的评估系统，仅依赖自然语言输入——用户问题、增强重述和生成的SQL，无需数据库模式或参考查询。STEF从自然语言和SQL表示中提取语义规范，执行归一化特征对齐，并通过综合指标（涵盖过滤器对齐、语义判断和评估者置信度）生成可解释的0至100分准确率。主要贡献包括：将增强问题质量验证作为首要评估信号、通过提示模板实现可配置的应用特定规则注入、以及生产环境鲁棒的归一化处理（支持GROUP BY容忍、ORDER BY默认值和LIMIT启发式）。实证结果表明，STEF无需依赖模式即可实现持续生产监控和代理改进反馈循环，首次使结构化查询评估在大规模场景下成为可能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of realistic evaluation methods for production text-to-SQL systems that do not require ground-truth SQL or database schemas, the authors introduce STEF, a schema-agnostic evaluation framework that assesses SQL accuracy using only natural language questions, enriched reformulations, and generated SQL. STEF extracts semantic specifications from both inputs, aligns them via normalized features, and computes an interpretable 0–100 score based on filter alignment, semantic verdict, and evaluator confidence. Experiments show STEF enables continuous monitoring and feedback for model improvement without schema access, making scalable, production-ready SQL evaluation feasible for the first time.
为解决现有评估方法依赖真实SQL和数据库结构而无法适用于生产环境的问题，作者提出STEF框架，仅通过自然语言问题、增强改写和生成SQL即可评估SQL准确性。该框架从自然语言与SQL中提取语义规范，进行归一化特征对齐，并输出0–100分的综合评分，涵盖过滤器对齐、语义判断与评估器置信度。实验证明，STEF可在无需数据库结构的情况下实现生产环境的持续监控与模型改进反馈，首次实现大规模可扩展的结构化查询评估。
Stable Behavior, Limited Variation: Persona Validity in LLM Agents for Urban Sentiment Perception
Authors: Neemias B da Silva, Rodrigo Minetto, Daniel Silver, Thiago H Silva
First: 2026-04-30T15:59:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:59:11+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 8 figures. IEEE DCOSS - UrbCom
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as proxies for human perception in urban analysis, yet it remains unclear whether persona prompting produces meaningful and reproducible behavioral diversity. We investigate whether distinct personas influence urban sentiment judgments generated by multimodal LLMs. Using a factorial set of personas spanning gender, economic status, political orientation, and personality, we instantiate multiple agents per persona to evaluate urban scene images from the PerceptSent dataset and assess both within-persona consistency and cross-persona variation. Results show strong convergence among agents sharing a persona, indicating stable and reproducible behavior. However, cross-persona differentiation is limited: economic status and personality induce statistically detectable but practically modest variation, while gender shows no measurable effect and political orientation only negligible impact. Agents also exhibit an extremity bias, collapsing intermediate sentiment categories common in human annotations. As a result, performance remains strong on coarse-grained polarity tasks but degrades as sentiment resolution increases, suggesting that simple label-based persona prompting does not capture fine-grained perceptual judgments. To isolate the contribution of persona conditioning, we additionally evaluate the same model without personas. Surprisingly, the no-persona model sometimes matches or exceeds persona-conditioned agreement with human labels across all task variants, suggesting that simple label-based persona prompting may add limited annotation value in this setting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：稳定行为、有限变化：大型语言模型代理在城市情感感知中的角色有效性
大型语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地被用作城市分析中人类感知的代理，但尚不清楚角色提示是否能产生有意义且可重复的行为多样性。我们研究不同角色是否会影响多模态LLM生成的城市情感判断。通过涵盖性别、经济地位、政治倾向和人格的因子化角色集合，我们为每个角色实例化多个代理，评估PerceptSent数据集中的城市场景图像，并评估角色内一致性和角色间差异。结果显示，共享同一角色的代理间高度收敛，表明行为稳定且可重复。然而，角色间差异有限：经济地位和人格可检测到统计学上的差异，但实际影响较小；性别无显著影响，政治倾向仅微弱影响。代理还表现出极端偏倚，将人类标注中常见的中间情感类别压缩。因此，模型在粗粒度极性任务上表现良好，但随着情感分辨率提高性能下降，表明简单的标签式角色提示无法捕捉精细感知判断。为分离角色条件化的影响，我们还评估了无角色模型。令人惊讶的是，无角色模型在所有任务变体中有时与人类标签的匹配度甚至超过角色条件化模型，表明在此设置中简单的标签式角色提示可能仅提供有限的标注价值。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether persona prompting in multimodal LLMs produces meaningful and reproducible behavioral diversity in urban sentiment perception. Using a factorial design with personas defined by gender, economic status, political orientation, and personality, the researchers instantiated multiple agents per persona to evaluate urban scene images and measured both within-persona consistency and cross-persona variation. Results show high behavioral stability within each persona, but limited cross-persona differentiation—economic status and personality induced small, statistically detectable differences, while gender and political orientation had negligible effects. Agents also exhibited an extremity bias, collapsing intermediate sentiment categories, leading to strong performance on coarse-grained polarity tasks but degradation at finer resolutions. Notably, a no-persona control model sometimes matched or exceeded persona-conditioned performance, suggesting label-based persona prompting adds minimal perceptual value.
本研究探讨在多模态大语言模型中，通过人格提示是否能产生有意义且可重复的行为多样性，以支持城市情感感知。研究采用因子设计，构建涵盖性别、经济地位、政治倾向和性格的人格组合，为每种人格实例化多个代理，评估城市场景图像并测量人格内一致性与人格间差异。结果表明，同一人格下的代理行为高度稳定，但人格间差异有限——经济地位和性格可产生微小但统计显著的差异，而性别和政治倾向影响可忽略。代理还表现出极端化倾向，压缩了人类标注中常见的中间情感类别，导致在粗粒度极性任务中表现优异，但在细粒度情感任务中性能下降。值得注意的是，无人格提示的基线模型在某些任务中表现甚至优于人格提示模型，表明基于标签的人格提示在该场景下贡献有限。
TAFA-GSGC: Group-wise Scalable Point Cloud Geometry Compression with Progressive Residual Refinement
Authors: Xiumei Li, Alexander Kopte, André Kaup
First: 2026-04-30T15:57:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:57:59+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IEEE International Conference on Image Processing (ICIP) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scalable compression is essential for bandwidth-adaptive transmission, yet most learned codecs are optimized for a fixed rate-distortion point, making rate adaptation costly due to re-encoding or maintaining multiple bitstreams. In this work, we propose TAFA-GSGC, a scalable learned point cloud geometry codec that enables multi-quality decoding from a single bitstream and a single trained model. TAFA-GSGC combines layered residual refinement with channel-group entropy coding, and introduces Target-Aligned Feature Aggregation module to reduce cross-layer redundancy in enhancement residuals. Our framework supports up to 9 decodable quality levels with monotonic quality improvement as more subbitstreams are received, while maintaining strong compression efficiency. Compared with the baseline PCGCv2, TAFA-GSGC attains comparable and slightly better RD performance, achieving average BD-Rate savings of -4.99% in D1 and -5.92% in D2.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TAFA-GSGC：基于渐进残差细化的组级可扩展点云几何压缩
可扩展压缩对带宽自适应传输至关重要，但大多数学习型编码器针对固定码率-失真点进行优化，导致速率自适应成本高昂（需重新编码或维护多个码流）。本文提出TAFA-GSGC，一种可扩展的学习型点云几何编码器，支持从单一码流和单一训练模型中解码多质量级别。TAFA-GSGC结合分层残差细化与通道组熵编码，并引入目标对齐特征聚合模块以降低增强残差层间冗余。该框架支持最高9级可解码质量，且随子码流接收逐步单调提升质量，同时保持高效压缩性能。相比基线PCGCv2，TAFA-GSGC实现相当甚至略优的RD性能，在D1和D2数据集上平均BD-Rate节省分别为-4.99%和-5.92%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for bandwidth-adaptive point cloud compression without costly re-encoding, this work introduces TAFA-GSGC, a scalable learned codec that enables multi-quality decoding from a single bitstream and model. It combines layered residual refinement with channel-group entropy coding and employs a Target-Aligned Feature Aggregation module to reduce cross-layer redundancy. Experimental results show the codec supports up to 9 decodable quality levels with monotonic quality improvement and achieves average BD-Rate savings of -4.99% in D1 and -5.92% in D2 compared to PCGCv2, while maintaining comparable or slightly better rate-distortion performance.
为实现无需重新编码的带宽自适应点云压缩，本文提出TAFA-GSGC，一种支持单比特流和单模型多质量解码的可扩展学习编码器。该框架结合分层残差细化与通道组熵编码，并引入目标对齐特征聚合模块以降低跨层冗余。实验表明，该方法支持最高9级可解码质量且质量随子比特流接收单调提升，相比PCGCv2在D1和D2上平均BD-Rate分别降低4.99%和5.92%，率失真性能相当或更优。
Collaborative Agent Reasoning Engineering (CARE): A Three-Party Design Methodology for Systematically Engineering AI Agents with Subject Matter Experts, Developers, and Helper Agents
Authors: Rahul Ramachandran, Nidhi Jha, Muthukumaran Ramasubramanian
First: 2026-04-30T15:54:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:54:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Collaborative Agent Reasoning Engineering (CARE), a disciplined methodology for engineering Large Language Model (LLM) agents in scientific domains. Unlike ad-hoc trial-and-error approaches, CARE specifies behavior, grounding, tool orchestration, and verification through reusable artifacts and systematic, stage-gated phases. The methodology employs a three-party workflow involving Subject-Matter Experts (SMEs), developers, and LLM-based helper agents. These helper agents function as facilitation infrastructure, transforming informal domain intent into structured, reviewable specifications for human approval at defined gates. CARE addresses the "jagged technological frontier", characterized by uneven LLM performance, by bridging the gap between novice and expert analysts regarding domain constraints and verification practices. By generating concrete artifacts, including interaction requirements, reasoning policies, and evaluation criteria, CARE ensures agent behavior is specifiable, testable, and maintainable. Evaluation results from a scientific use case demonstrate that this stage-gated, artifact-driven methodology yields measurable improvements in development efficiency and complex-query performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：协作代理推理工程（CARE）：一种结合领域专家、开发者与辅助代理的三方设计方法论，用于系统化构建人工智能代理
我们提出协作代理推理工程（CARE），这是一种用于科学领域大型语言模型（LLM）代理工程的规范方法。与随意试错的方法不同，CARE通过可复用的成果和分阶段、关卡控制的流程，明确指定行为、锚定、工具编排和验证。该方法采用三方工作流，涉及领域专家（SMEs）、开发者和基于LLM的辅助代理。这些辅助代理作为促进基础设施，将非正式的领域意图转化为结构化、可审查的规范，供人类在指定关卡批准。CARE应对“技术前沿参差不齐”的问题——即LLM性能不均衡——通过弥合新手与专家分析员在领域约束和验证实践之间的差距。通过生成具体成果，包括交互需求、推理策略和评估标准，CARE确保代理行为可指定、可测试、可维护。科学用例的评估结果表明，这种分阶段、成果驱动的方法显著提升了开发效率和复杂查询性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to systematically engineer reliable AI agents in scientific domains amid inconsistent LLM performance, CARE introduces a three-party methodology involving Subject-Matter Experts, developers, and LLM-based helper agents. The method structures development into stage-gated phases, where helper agents translate informal domain intent into formal, reviewable artifacts such as interaction requirements and reasoning policies, enabling human validation at key checkpoints. Experimental results from a scientific use case show that this artifact-driven, collaborative approach improves development efficiency and enhances performance on complex queries compared to ad-hoc methods.
为解决现有大语言模型代理开发中缺乏系统性和效率低下的问题，CARE提出一种三角色协作方法，由领域专家、开发者和基于LLM的辅助代理共同参与，通过可复用的工件和阶段式门控流程，系统化地构建科学领域的AI代理。辅助代理负责将非结构化领域意图转化为可审查的规范，确保行为可定义、可测试、可维护。实验表明，该方法显著提升了开发效率和复杂查询性能。
SpecVQA: A Benchmark for Spectral Understanding and Visual Question Answering in Scientific Images
Authors: Jialu Shen, Han Lyu, Suyang Zhong, Hanzheng Li, Haoyi Tao, Nan Wang, Changhong Chen, Xi Fang
First: 2026-04-30T15:51:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:51:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Spectra are a prevalent yet highly information-dense form of scientific imagery, presenting substantial challenges to multimodal large language models (MLLMs) due to their unstructured and domain-specific characteristics. Here we introduce SpecVQA, a professional scientific-image benchmark for evaluating multimodal models on scientific spectral understanding, covering 7 representative spectrum types with expert-annotated question-answer pairs. The aim comprises two aspects: spectra scientific QA evaluation and corresponding underlying task evaluation. SpecVQA contains 620 figures and 3100 QA pairs curated from peer-reviewed literature, targeting both direct information extraction and domain-specific reasoning. To effectively reduce token length while preserving essential curve characteristics, we propose a spectral data sampling and interpolation reconstruction approach. Ablation studies further confirm that the approach achieves substantial performance improvements on the proposed benchmark. We test the capability of prominent MLLMs in scientific spectral understanding on our benchmark and present a leaderboard. This work represents an essential step toward enhancing spectral understanding in multimodal large models and suggests promising directions for extending visual-language models to broader scientific research and data analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpecVQA：科学图像中光谱理解与视觉问答的基准
光谱是广泛存在且信息密度极高的科学图像形式，由于其无结构化和领域特异性，对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）构成重大挑战。本文引入SpecVQA，一个专业科学图像基准，用于评估多模态模型在科学光谱理解方面的表现，涵盖7种代表性光谱类型，并配有专家标注的问答对。目标包含两个方面：光谱科学问答评估及对应底层任务评估。SpecVQA包含620幅图像和3100组问答对，源自同行评审文献，旨在支持直接信息提取和领域特定推理。为有效缩短token长度同时保留关键曲线特征，我们提出光谱数据采样与插值重建方法。消融实验进一步证实该方法在本基准上显著提升性能。我们在本基准上测试主流MLLMs在科学光谱理解中的能力，并发布排行榜。本工作是提升多模态大模型光谱理解能力的重要一步，也为扩展视觉-语言模型至更广泛的科学研究与数据分析指明了方向。
