To handle the unstructured nature of spectra while reducing token length, the authors propose a sampling and interpolation method that preserves key curve features. Experimental results show this approach significantly improves model performance on the benchmark, and evaluations of leading MLLMs reveal substantial variation in spectral understanding capability, with a leaderboard highlighting top-performing models.
为应对多模态大语言模型在解析复杂科学光谱时的困难，本文提出SpecVQA基准，包含620幅光谱图像和3100组专家标注的问答对，覆盖七类典型光谱。为在压缩token长度的同时保留关键曲线特征，作者提出光谱采样与插值重建方法，消融实验表明该方法显著提升模型性能。在该基准上测试主流MLLMs发现其在光谱理解方面存在明显不足，作者发布排行榜以引导未来模型改进。
Early Detection of Water Stress by Plant Electrophysiology: Machine Learning for Irrigation Management
Authors: Eduard Buss, Till Aust, Heiko Hamann
First: 2026-04-30T15:49:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:49:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Purpose: Fast detection of plant stress is key to plant phenotyping, precision agriculture, and automated crop management. In particular, efficient irrigation management requires early identification of water stress to optimize resource use while maintaining crop performance. Direct physiological sensing offers the potential to detect stress responses before visible symptoms appear. Methods: In this study, we recorded electrophysiological signals from greenhouse-grown tomato plants subjected to water stress and developed a framework based on machine learning for online stress detection. The recorded time-series data were processed using a processing pipeline that includes statistical feature extraction and selection, automated machine learning or alternatively deep learning, and probability calibration. Results: Across multiple input time horizons, we found that a 30-minute look-back window strikes the best balance between rapid decision-making and classification performance. Using automated machine learning, the framework achieved classification accuracies of up to 92%, outperforming deep learning approaches. Sequential backward selection reduced the feature set while maintaining performance. Importantly, the framework detects transitions from healthy to stressed states in recordings that were not included in the training set. Conclusion: Overall, we provide a decision-support tool for farmers and establish a foundation for biofeedback-driven irrigation control to improve resource efficiency in (semi-)autonomous crop production systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用植物电生理学早期检测水分胁迫：机器学习用于灌溉管理
目的：快速检测植物胁迫对植物表型分析、精准农业和自动化作物管理至关重要。尤其在灌溉管理中，早期识别水分胁迫有助于优化资源利用并维持作物性能。直接生理传感可在可见症状出现前检测胁迫反应。方法：本研究记录了温室种植番茄植株在水分胁迫下的电生理信号，并基于机器学习构建了在线胁迫检测框架。所记录的时间序列数据经处理流程处理，包括统计特征提取与选择、自动化机器学习或深度学习，以及概率校准。结果：在多个输入时间窗口中，30分钟回溯窗口在快速决策与分类性能间取得最佳平衡。使用自动化机器学习，该框架分类准确率最高达92%，优于深度学习方法。顺序后向选择可减少特征集同时保持性能。重要的是，该框架能在未参与训练集的记录中检测健康状态向胁迫状态的转变。结论：总体而言，我们为农民提供了决策支持工具，并为基于生物反馈的灌溉控制奠定了基础，以提高（半）自主作物生产系统中的资源效率。
Summary / 总结
To enable timely irrigation decisions and improve resource efficiency in agriculture, this study leverages plant electrophysiology to detect early water stress in greenhouse-grown tomato plants using machine learning. Electrophysiological time-series data were processed through statistical feature extraction, automated machine learning, and probability calibration, with a 30-minute look-back window identified as optimal for balancing speed and accuracy. The framework achieved up to 92% classification accuracy using automated machine learning, outperforming deep learning, and successfully detected stress transitions even in unseen data, demonstrating its potential as a decision-support tool for precision agriculture.
为实现农业灌溉的及时决策和资源高效利用，本研究利用植物电生理信号在可见症状出现前检测番茄植株的早期水分胁迫。研究人员对温室中受控水分胁迫的番茄植株采集电生理时序数据，通过统计特征提取、自动化机器学习和概率校准构建模型。该框架在30分钟回溯窗口下达到最高92%的分类准确率，优于深度学习方法，且经特征降维后性能仍保持稳定。重要的是，模型能成功识别训练集中未出现的胁迫状态转换，展现出良好的泛化能力，为实时生物反馈驱动的灌溉控制提供了实用工具。
Exponential families from a single KL identity
Authors: Marc Dymetman
First: 2026-04-30T15:48:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:48:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Exponential families encompass the distributions central to modern machine learning -- softmax, Gaussians, and Boltzmann distributions -- and underlie the theory of variational inference, entropy-regularized reinforcement learning, and RLHF. We isolate a simple identity for exponential families that expresses the KL difference $\mathrm{KL}(q \| p_{λ_2}) - \mathrm{KL}(q \| p_{λ_1})$ in terms of the log-partition function $A(λ)$ and the moment $μ_q$. Remarkably, this identity together with the single fact that $\mathrm{KL} \geq 0$ (with equality iff $p = q$) suffices, by direct substitution and rearrangement, to derive a cluster of results that are classically obtained by separate, heavier arguments: a generalized three-point identity for arbitrary reference distributions, Pythagorean theorems for I-projections and reverse I-projections, convexity of the log-partition function, identification of its Legendre dual in KL terms, the Gibbs variational principle, and the explicit optimizer in KL-regularized reward maximization, including the exponential tilting formula underlying entropy-regularized control and RLHF. Beyond these purely algebraic consequences, standard analytic arguments recover the gradient formula for the log-partition function, the Bregman representation of within-family KL divergence, and the surjectivity of the moment map. The note is self-contained.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从单个KL恒等式出发的指数族
指数族涵盖了现代机器学习中至关重要的分布——softmax、高斯分布和玻尔兹曼分布，并构成了变分推断、熵正则化强化学习和RLHF理论的基础。我们推导出指数族的一个简单恒等式，将KL散度差 $\mathrm{KL}(q \| p_{λ_2}) - \mathrm{KL}(q \| p_{λ_1})$ 表示为对数配分函数 $A(λ)$ 和矩 $μ_q$ 的函数。令人惊讶的是，仅凭这一恒等式与KL散度非负性（当且仅当 $p = q$ 时取等）这一基本事实，通过直接代入和重排，即可推导出一系列经典上需独立、复杂论证才能得到的结果：任意参考分布下的广义三点恒等式、I投影与逆I投影的毕达哥拉斯定理、对数配分函数的凸性、其Legendre对偶在KL意义下的识别、Gibbs变分原理，以及KL正则化奖励最大化中的显式最优解（包括支撑熵正则化控制和RLHF的指数倾斜公式）。除这些纯代数推论外，标准分析论证还可恢复对数配分函数的梯度公式、族内KL散度的Bregman表示，以及矩映射的满射性。本文自洽完备。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the centrality of exponential families in machine learning and variational inference, the paper derives a single KL identity expressing the difference between two KL divergences in terms of the log-partition function and moments. This identity, combined with the basic property that KL divergence is non-negative, enables direct derivation of multiple classical results including the generalized three-point identity, Pythagorean theorems for I-projections, convexity of the log-partition function, its Legendre dual, the Gibbs variational principle, and the optimizer in KL-regularized reward maximization. Analytic extensions further yield the gradient of the log-partition, Bregman representation of KL divergence, and surjectivity of the moment map, all without requiring separate proofs.
为统一并简化指数族理论及其在机器学习中的应用推导，该论文提出一个单一的KL恒等式，将两个KL散度之差表示为对数分区函数与矩的函数。仅利用该恒等式和KL散度非负性（等号成立当且仅当分布相等），作者通过直接代入和重排推导出一系列经典结果，包括广义三点恒等式、I投影与逆I投影的毕达哥拉斯定理、对数分区函数的凸性、其Legendre对偶、Gibbs变分原理以及KL正则化奖励最大化中的最优解。该方法不依赖复杂证明，仅通过代数操作即可完成推导。由于该工作纯属理论性，未进行实验验证，但其展示了该恒等式在变分推断、RLHF及熵正则化控制等领域基础结果推导中的强大统一性。
Ease of dependency distance minimization in star-like structures
Authors: Emília Garcia-Casademont, Ramon Ferrer-i-Cancho
First: 2026-04-30T15:47:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:47:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The syntactic structure of a sentence can be represented as a tree where edges indicate syntactic dependencies between words. When that structure is a star, it has been demonstrated that the head should be placed in the middle of the linear arrangement according to the principle of syntactic dependency distance minimization. However, hubs of stars tend to be put at one of the ends, against that principle. Here we address two questions: (1) How difficult is it to minimize dependency distance? (2) Why anti dependency distance minimization effects have been found in star structures but not in path structures? The ease of optimization is determined by the shape of the optimization landscape. It was demonstrated that the landscape of star structures is quasiconvex (Ferrer-i-Cancho 2015, Language Dynamics and Change). As for (1), here we show that it is indeed convex (a particular case of quasiconvexity) both for star trees and quasistar trees and thus the distance-based optimization problem is simpler than previously believed. As for (2), we argue that (a) competing principles, rather than the difficulty of optimization, must be the actual reason for anti-dependency distance minimization effects and that (b) dependency distance minimization on star-like structures is less rewarding compared to other structures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：星形结构中依赖距离最小化的易实现性
句子的句法结构可表示为树形结构，其中边表示词间的句法依赖关系。当该结构为星形时，根据句法依赖距离最小化原则，中心词应置于线性排列的中间位置。然而，星形结构的中心词常被置于一端，违背该原则。本文探讨两个问题：(1) 最小化依赖距离有多容易？(2) 为何星形结构中观察到反依赖距离最小化效应，而路径结构中未见？优化的易实现性取决于优化景观的形状。研究表明，星形结构的景观是拟凸的（Ferrer-i-Cancho 2015, Language Dynamics and Change）。针对(1)，本文证明星形树和拟星形树的景观均为凸的（拟凸性的一种特例），因此基于距离的优化问题比此前认为的更简单。针对(2)，我们认为：(a) 导致星形结构中反依赖距离最小化效应的原因应是竞争性原则，而非优化难度；(b) 相较于其他结构，星形结构中依赖距离最小化带来的收益较低。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates why syntactic dependency distance minimization, which favors placing the central head word in the middle of a linear arrangement in star-like syntactic structures, is often violated in natural language. The authors analyze the optimization landscape of such structures and demonstrate that it is convex — a stronger property than previously assumed — making distance minimization easier than thought. They further argue that the observed violations are not due to optimization difficulty but rather to competing linguistic principles, and that star-like structures offer less reward for distance minimization compared to other syntactic forms like paths.
该研究探讨了为何星形句法结构中依赖距离最小化比预期更容易，尽管实证发现此类结构常将中心词置于句首或句尾，违背最小化原则。作者证明星形与近星形结构的优化景观是凸的（比之前认知更强），意味着该优化问题可解。他们进一步指出，反最小化现象并非因优化难度所致，而是由于其他句法原则的竞争，且星形结构在距离最小化上的收益低于路径结构。
Shuffling-Aware Optimization for Private Vector Mean Estimation
Authors: Shun Takagi, Seng Pei Liew
First: 2026-04-30T15:47:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:47:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study $d$-dimensional unbiased mean estimation in the single-message shuffle model, where each user sends a single privatized message and the analyzer only observes the shuffled multiset of reports. While minimax-optimal mechanisms are well understood in the local differential privacy setting, the corresponding notion of optimality after shuffling has remained largely unexplored. To address this gap, we introduce the recently proposed shuffle index and use it to formulate the post-shuffling mechanism design problem as an explicit optimization problem. We then establish a minimax lower bound on the achievable mean squared error in terms of the shuffle index, which implies that mechanisms that are optimal under LDP can become suboptimal once shuffling is applied. Finally, we construct an asymptotically minimax optimal mechanism in the high privacy regime, which as a consequence achieves a privacy-utility trade-off nearly identical to that of the central Gaussian mechanism.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向私有向量均值估计的洗牌感知优化
我们研究单消息洗牌模型下的 $d$ 维无偏均值估计，其中每位用户发送一条私有化消息，分析器仅观察到这些报告的洗牌多重集。虽然在本地差分隐私设置下，最优机制已被充分理解，但洗牌后对应的最优性概念仍鲜有探索。为填补这一空白，我们引入最近提出的洗牌索引，并将其用于将洗牌后机制设计问题形式化为一个显式优化问题。随后，我们建立了可实现的均方误差的最小最大下界，该下界以洗牌索引为参数，表明在洗牌后原本在LDP下最优的机制可能变得次优。最后，我们在高隐私场景下构造了一个渐近最小最大最优机制，其结果实现了与中心高斯机制几乎相同的隐私-效用权衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand optimal private mean estimation under shuffling, this work introduces the shuffle index to formalize the post-shuffling mechanism design problem as an optimization task. It establishes a minimax lower bound on mean squared error using this index, revealing that LDP-optimal mechanisms can become suboptimal after shuffling. The authors then construct an asymptotically minimax optimal mechanism in the high privacy regime, achieving a privacy-utility trade-off nearly matching that of the central Gaussian mechanism.
为理解shuffle模型下隐私与效用的权衡，本文研究了在单消息shuffle模型中d维空间的无偏均值估计问题。引入shuffle index将后shuffle机制设计形式化为优化问题，并推导出均方误差的minimax下界，揭示LDP最优机制在shuffle后可能变得次优。随后，本文在高隐私场景下构造了渐近minimax最优机制，其隐私-效用权衡接近中心高斯机制。
Models Recall What They Violate: Constraint Adherence in Multi-Turn LLM Ideation
Authors: Garvin Kruthof
First: 2026-04-30T15:46:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:46:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When researchers iteratively refine ideas with large language models, do the models preserve fidelity to the original objective? We introduce DriftBench, a benchmark for evaluating constraint adherence in multi-turn LLM-assisted scientific ideation. Across 2,146 scored benchmark runs spanning seven models from five providers (including two open-weight), four interaction conditions, and 38 research briefs from 24 scientific domains, we find that iterative pressure reliably increases structural complexity and often reduces adherence to original constraints. A restatement probe reveals a dissociation between declarative recall and behavioral adherence, as models accurately restate constraints they simultaneously violate. The knows-but-violates (KBV) rate, measuring constraint non-compliance despite preserved recall, ranges from 8% to 99% across models. Structured checkpointing partially reduces KBV rates but does not close the dissociation, and complexity inflation persists. Human validation against blind raters confirms that the LLM judge under-detects constraint violations, making reported constraint adherence scores conservative. Sensitivity analyses confirm the findings are robust to temperature (0.7 vs.\ 1.0) and pressure type (novelty vs.\ rigor). We release all briefs, prompts, rubrics, transcripts, and scores as an open benchmark.
中文标题/摘要
标题：模型会回忆起它们违反的内容：多轮LLM辅助科学创意中的约束遵守
当研究人员使用大语言模型迭代优化创意时，模型是否能保持对原始目标的忠实性？我们提出了DriftBench，一个用于评估多轮LLM辅助科学创意中约束遵守能力的基准。在涵盖五个提供商（包括两个开源模型）的七种模型、四种交互条件和24个科学领域中的38份研究简报的2,146次评分基准运行中，我们发现迭代压力会可靠地增加结构复杂性，并常导致对原始约束的遵守度下降。重述探测揭示了声明性回忆与行为遵守之间的分离，即模型能准确重述约束的同时却违反它。衡量尽管保留回忆但违反约束的“知道却违反”（KBV）率在不同模型间介于8%至99%。结构化检查点部分降低了KBV率，但未能消除该分离，且复杂性膨胀持续存在。人类盲评验证确认LLM裁判低估了约束违反情况，导致报告的约束遵守分数保守。敏感性分析证实，无论温度（0.7 vs. 1.0）或压力类型（新颖性 vs. 严谨性），结果均稳健。我们公开所有简报、提示、评分标准、转录和评分作为开放基准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess whether large language models maintain constraint adherence during iterative ideation, the study introduces DriftBench, a benchmark evaluating multi-turn LLM behavior across 2,146 runs with seven models from five providers. Results show that iterative refinement consistently increases structural complexity while reducing constraint adherence, even when models accurately restate original constraints—a dissociation termed 'knows-but-violates' (KBV), which ranges from 8% to 99% across models. Structured checkpointing partially mitigates KBV but fails to eliminate the dissociation or complexity inflation. Human validation confirms LLMs under-detect violations, leading to overly conservative adherence scores, and sensitivity tests confirm robustness across temperature and pressure types.
为评估大语言模型在多轮创意迭代中是否保持约束一致性，研究构建了DriftBench基准测试，涵盖2146次运行、七种模型、38个研究简报及四种交互条件。结果表明，迭代过程显著增加结构复杂度，同时降低约束遵守率，‘知道却违反’（KBV）率在8%至99%之间，显示模型常能准确复述约束却实际违反。结构化检查点部分缓解KBV，但无法消除认知与行为的脱节，且人工验证证实模型对违规行为检测不足，导致其报告的遵守率偏低。结果在温度与压力类型上均具稳健性。
MIFair: A Mutual-Information Framework for Intersectionality and Multiclass Fairness
Authors: Jeanne Monnier, Thomas George, Frédéric Guyard, Christèle Tarnec, Marios Kountouris
First: 2026-04-30T15:46:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:46:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fairness in machine learning remains challenging due to its ethical complexity, the absence of a universal definition, and the need for context-specific bias metrics. Existing methods still struggle with intersectionality, multiclass settings, and limited flexibility and generality. To address these gaps, we introduce MIFair, a unified framework for bias assessment and mitigation based on mutual information. MIFair provides a flexible metric template and an in-processing mitigation method inspired by the Prejudice Remover, defining group fairness as statistical independence between prediction-derived variables and sensitive attributes. We further strengthen its information-theoretic foundation by establishing equivalences with widely used fairness notions such as independence and separation. MIFair naturally supports intersectionality, complex subgroup structures, and multiclass classification and employs regularization-based training to reduce bias according to the selected metric. Its key advantage is its versatility: it consolidates diverse fairness requirements into a single coherent framework, enabling consistent benchmarking and simplifying practical use. Experiments on real-world tabular and image datasets show that MIFair effectively reduces bias, including previously unaddressed multi-attribute scenarios, while maintaining strong predictive performance across the evaluated settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MIFair：面向交叉性和多类公平性的互信息框架
机器学习中的公平性因伦理复杂性、缺乏统一定义及需情境特定的偏见度量而仍具挑战。现有方法在处理交叉性、多类设置及灵活性与通用性方面仍存不足。为弥补这些缺陷，我们提出MIFair，一个基于互信息的统一框架，用于偏见评估与缓解。MIFair提供灵活的度量模板及受Prejudice Remover启发的处理中缓解方法，将群体公平性定义为预测变量与敏感属性间的统计独立性。我们进一步通过建立与常用公平概念（如独立性与分离性）的等价关系，强化其信息论基础。MIFair天然支持交叉性、复杂子群体结构及多类分类，并通过正则化训练依据选定度量降低偏见。其核心优势在于通用性：将多样公平需求整合为单一连贯框架，便于一致基准评估并简化实际应用。在真实世界表格与图像数据集上的实验表明，MIFair有效降低偏见（包括此前未被处理的多属性场景），同时在评估设置中保持强预测性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of a universal fairness definition and the difficulty in handling intersectionality and multiclass bias, MIFair introduces a mutual-information-based framework for assessing and mitigating bias. It defines fairness as statistical independence between predictions and sensitive attributes, and extends this foundation to support complex subgroup structures and multiclass settings through regularization-based training. Experimental results on real-world datasets demonstrate that MIFair successfully reduces bias across multiple sensitive attributes while preserving predictive accuracy, outperforming existing methods in previously unaddressed intersectional and multiclass scenarios.
为应对机器学习公平性中的伦理复杂性、缺乏统一定义以及处理交叉性与多分类场景的困难，MIFair提出一种基于互信息的公平性评估与缓解框架。该框架将群体公平性定义为预测变量与敏感属性间的统计独立性，并通过训练时正则化实现对选定公平度量的对齐。它天然支持复杂子群体结构与多属性场景，其理论基础与独立性、分离性等主流公平概念等价。在真实世界表格与图像数据集上的实验表明，MIFair能有效降低偏见——包括此前未被解决的多属性场景——同时在各类评估设置中保持较强的预测性能。
NanoKnow: How to Know What Your Language Model Knows
Authors: Lingwei Gu, Nour Jedidi, Jimmy Lin
Venue: SIGIR 2026
First: 2026-02-23T18:37:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:46:03+00:00
Comments: SIGIR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
How do large language models (LLMs) know what they know? Answering this question has been difficult because pre-training data is often a "black box" - unknown or inaccessible. The recent release of nanochat - a family of small LLMs with fully open pre-training data - addresses this as it provides a transparent view into where a model's parametric knowledge comes from. Towards the goal of understanding how knowledge is encoded by LLMs, we release NanoKnow, a benchmark dataset that partitions questions from Natural Questions and SQuAD into splits based on whether their answers are present in nanochat's pre-training corpus. Using these splits, we can now properly disentangle the sources of knowledge that LLMs rely on when producing an output. To demonstrate NanoKnow's utility, we conduct experiments using eight nanochat checkpoints. Our findings show: (1) closed-book accuracy is strongly influenced by answer frequency in the pre-training data, (2) providing external evidence can mitigate this frequency dependence, (3) even with external evidence, models are more accurate when answers were seen during pre-training, demonstrating that parametric and external knowledge are complementary, and (4) non-relevant information is harmful, with accuracy decreasing based on both the position and the number of non-relevant contexts. We release all NanoKnow artifacts at https://github.com/castorini/NanoKnow.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NanoKnow：如何了解你的语言模型知道什么
大型语言模型（LLM）如何知道它们所知道的内容？回答这一问题一直困难，因为预训练数据往往是“黑箱”——未知或不可访问。近期发布的nanochat——一组拥有完全开放预训练数据的小型LLM——解决了这一问题，它提供了透明视角，揭示模型参数知识的来源。为理解LLM如何编码知识，我们发布了NanoKnow基准数据集，该数据集根据问题答案是否存在于nanochat的预训练语料库中，将Natural Questions和SQuAD中的问题划分为不同子集。通过这些子集，我们现在能够正确分离LLM生成输出时所依赖的知识来源。为展示NanoKnow的实用性，我们使用八个nanochat检查点进行实验。我们的发现表明：（1）闭卷准确率强烈受预训练数据中答案频率的影响；（2）提供外部证据可缓解这种频率依赖性；（3）即使提供外部证据，模型在预训练时见过答案时仍更准确，表明参数化知识与外部知识互补；（4）非相关信息有害，准确率随非相关上下文的位置和数量增加而下降。我们将在https://github.com/castorini/NanoKnow发布所有NanoKnow相关材料。
Summary / 总结
Understanding how large language models acquire and retrieve knowledge has been hindered by opaque pre-training data. To address this, NanoKnow introduces a benchmark dataset derived from Natural Questions and SQuAD, partitioned based on whether answers appear in the open-source nanochat pre-training corpus. Experiments across eight nanochat checkpoints reveal that model accuracy is strongly tied to answer frequency in training data, which can be mitigated by external evidence; however, parametric knowledge from pre-training remains more reliable. Additionally, non-relevant context harms performance, with accuracy declining as the number or position of such contexts increases.
由于预训练数据通常不可见，理解大语言模型如何获取和调用知识一直困难重重。NanoKnow通过将自然问题和SQuAD中的问题按其答案是否出现在nanochat公开预训练语料中进行划分，构建了一个基准测试。在八个nanochat检查点上的实验表明，模型准确率高度依赖于预训练数据中答案的频率，外部证据可缓解此依赖，但参数化知识与外部知识仍具互补性；此外，无关上下文会降低准确率，且其负面影响随数量或位置增加而加剧。
Constrained Policy Optimization with Cantelli-Bounded Value-at-Risk
Authors: Rohan Tangri, Jan-Peter Calliess
First: 2026-01-30T13:57:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-30T15:45:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce the Value-at-Risk Constrained Policy Optimization algorithm (VaR-CPO), a sample efficient and conservative method designed to optimize Value-at-Risk (VaR) constrained reinforcement learning (RL) problems. Empirically, we demonstrate that VaR-CPO is capable of safe exploration, achieving zero constraint violations during training in feasible environments, a critical property that baseline methods fail to uphold. To overcome the inherent non-differentiability of the VaR constraint, we employ Cantelli's inequality to obtain a tractable approximation based on the first two moments of the cost return. Additionally, by extending the trust-region framework of the Constrained Policy Optimization (CPO) method, we provide worst-case bounds for both policy improvement and constraint violation during the training process.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于Cantelli界值险的约束策略优化
我们提出了值险约束策略优化算法（VaR-CPO），这是一种样本效率高且保守的方法，旨在优化值险（VaR）约束的强化学习问题。实证表明，VaR-CPO能够实现安全探索，在可行环境中训练期间无约束违反，这是基线方法无法实现的关键特性。为克服VaR约束的固有不可微性，我们采用Cantelli不等式，基于成本回报的一阶和二阶矩获得可处理的近似解。此外，通过扩展约束策略优化（CPO）方法的信任域框架，我们为训练过程中策略改进和约束违反提供了最坏情况的界。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for safe and sample-efficient reinforcement learning under Value-at-Risk constraints, this paper proposes VaR-CPO, a method that leverages Cantelli’s inequality to approximate the non-differentiable VaR constraint using first and second moments of cost returns. By integrating this approximation into a trust-region framework inspired by CPO, VaR-CPO provides theoretical bounds on both policy improvement and constraint violation. Empirical results show that VaR-CPO achieves zero constraint violations during training in feasible environments, outperforming baseline methods that often violate constraints.
为实现受VaR约束下的安全且高效的强化学习，本文提出VaR-CPO算法，利用Cantelli不等式将不可微的VaR约束转化为基于成本回报一阶与二阶矩的可处理近似。该方法在CPO的置信域框架基础上，为策略改进与约束违反提供最坏情况的理论界。实验表明，VaR-CPO在可行环境中训练期间实现零约束违反，优于常出现约束违规的基线方法。
